- **1914.08** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/fever-behind-the-shadows` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  后半夜Klara烧起来，额头烫得像炉盖。Josef Bauer把湿布叠成窄条，一遍遍擦孩子的手心；煤气灯的影子在墙上忽长忽短，Klara哭一阵停一阵。天快亮时，Josef Bauer抱着孩子去敲隔壁的门，借来一支体温计。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (an infant fever at night); condition; cash -1 (a borrowed thermometer and medicine); mood -0.04000000000000001; flag +infant_night_fever_

- **1914.08** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Bauer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt病倒了，痢疾让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1914.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的痢疾没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1914.12** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从痢疾中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1915.11** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/neighbor-carries` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  Anna Moser的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Klara坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Anna Moser把桶一放，夹起Klara进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Josef Bauer赶来时，Klara正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (膝盖擦破，已冲洗); food_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Klara拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Anna Moser弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Klara接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Grete Huber朝Klara招手。Klara挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Klara把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Klara后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Klara没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Klara用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1923.11** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  菜摊前的地面是湿的。Klara弯腰捡土豆时，一枚铜板从指缝里滑出去，顺着水沟格栅掉了下去。Klara把剩下的钱摊在柜台上，让摊主帮忙数，还是少了三十克鲁泽。晚上Josef Bauer把钱一枚枚排开，问了三遍怎么少的，Klara每答一次，声音就低一点。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.06_

  _effects: cash -1.5 (酒棚的新酒和牌局输的铜板); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Klara在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Klara的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

  Klara把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Josefine Hofer，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Josefine Hofer点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1934.01** `project_completed` tags: project
  「Klara召集的会议」完成了当前阶段，参与者留下了实际的记录和后续安排。
  _effects: project_

- **1934.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil Huber嫌Klara的床单滴水滴到Emil Huber家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Klara下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Klara把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Emil Huber那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

  Friedrich Hofer在会议上先一步拿到了多数。Klara的提案被改成一段附注，连名字都没有留下。散会后，Klara在走廊里站了一会儿，然后去下一间办公室。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; reputation -0.04999999999999999; state_add political-suspect state:world-vienna-1912:384; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1934.01** `state_expire:stage-sovereign` tags: stage-sovereign, performance, theatre, pretend, status, irony, power, no-real-authority

- **1934.05** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/read-and-decline` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Klara把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1934.08** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.bridge` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Klara在桥边站了一会儿，看一艘平底船靠岸卸煤，几个工人用铁锹往岸上甩。风把煤灰吹到她脸上，她抹了一把，往回走时脚步比来时轻，回到巷口还给自己买了块三赫勒的糖。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖)_

- **1934.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/helps_fetch_doctor` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  隔壁Alois Weiss的小儿子烧到说胡话，额头烫得能烘手。Alois Weiss在楼道里抹眼泪，说请不起医生。Klara下楼叫了街角药房的伙计，花四十赫勒买了一包退烧药粉，又帮着用湿布敷了一整夜。天亮时孩子退了烧，Alois Weiss端来一碗热汤面，面底下卧着一个鸡蛋。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1935.01** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Rosa Weiss 2在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Josef Bauer没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室); reputation 0.06; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:006836; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:political-suspect/police-interest/avoided` tags: political-suspect, politics, repression, legal, surveillance, state, state_tick
  Klara换了路线，避开那家咖啡馆和门口的两个便衣。档案里又添了一页，但今年没有传唤。
  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.3 -> 0.25_

- **1935.01** `r13-fam.teach-parent-to-read::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/parent-gives-up` tags: task, care, literacy, parent, school, intergenerational, vienna, urban
  写到第三遍，Josef Bauer把手指在围裙上擦了擦，说这玩意儿不是给自己这个年纪的人学的。Klara还想劝，老人已经起身去灶边看火；桌上那层撒平的面粉留着半个字，被热气慢慢洇湿。晚上，Klara把那张写着名字的纸压在盐罐底下。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +parent_gave_up_reading_lessons; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; condition_

- **1935.03** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sibling-medicine-bill/advance-with-room-claim` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara把十四克朗的整张票子压在药费单上，说这钱不用还，只要求母亲那间朝院子的屋子以后归自己照看，钥匙也归自己管。Anna Bauer的咖啡杯在桌上顿了一下，说照看可以，房契上的名字另说；药费单总算从糖罐底下抽了出来，兄妹之间却多了一层账。
  _effects: cash -14 (先垫付母亲全部药费（其中9.88克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-anna-bauer; flag +claims_mother_room_

- **1935.07** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.flour-loan/flour-loan.full-bag` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara把一整袋面粉倒进Lena的空口袋，袋口用麻绳扎了两道，说等她男人发了薪再还。Lena连声道谢，把写着借煤日期的小纸片折好放回围裙。柜子里只剩一袋面粉，Klara当晚煮汤时多加了一勺水。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1935.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/courtyard-quarrel` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  话没说完就变成了争。Rosa Huber把外套扔在晾衣杆下，Klara说了一句不中听的，声音大得整院都听见。Hermann Moser在三楼把窗开了一条缝，看完了全程，第二天院子里人人都知道；这一架把两个人的关系吵断了，也把Klara的名字在院子里吵薄了一层。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; reputation -0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; mood -0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.04999999999999999; flag +faction_split_

  _effects: reputation 0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007209_

  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1939.01** `p10-migration-tasks.ask-travel-money/uncle-part-loan-note` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, loan, relative
  舅父从裤袋里数出十五克朗推过来，说剩下的让他去车站帮车夫装货抵工钱。舅父在灯下写了一张欠条，写明三个月内还清，他签了名，把钱对折放进内袋，欠条留给舅父收在账簿里。
  _effects: cash 15 (舅父借给的路费); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; flag +owes_uncle_15_kronen; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1939.12** `r13-rel.proposal-rejected::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refused-in-public` tags: relationship, courtship, rejection, proposal, shame, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara在舞会上当着众人的面把戒指递过去，Anton后退一步，说别这样。乐队停了，旁边的人开始笑。Klara把戒指收回口袋，接下来的三支曲子都站在门边，谁请也不跳。
  _effects: cash -2 (退回的银顶针（其中2克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.10000000000000003; role; relation_end partner with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

- **1940.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Klara没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.03 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008615_

- **1940.01** `state_reaction:ad20-private-bargain/a-betrayal-reprices-the-favour` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  背叛的消息传来时，Klara先想到的不是损失，而是那笔私下的账。对方也知道Klara会先想到这个。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 62 -> 74; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 55 -> 45; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.exit 22 -> 17; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/motion-carries` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  投票结果是十九比十七。Klara把印章、钥匙和一本写满批注的账本放进纸箱，最后一圈走廊走得比平时慢。Mitzi Moser 2在门口等着接手，手里已经拿着新的编制表；表上第一个被划掉的名字是Klara的秘书。
  _effects: position_end 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:006836; reputation -0.04999999999999999; condition; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; flag +removed_by_vote; knowledge “新编制表第一个划掉的是我的秘书。”_

- **1940.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction

  _effects: reputation 0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009828_

  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.77 -> 0.92_

- **1940.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Klara没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1940.08** `r3-mig.colonial-return::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/colonial-land-claim-rejected` tags: migration, colonial, return, land, family, vienna, urban, crisis
  Alois Stein 2把地契翻过来，说上面的登记号属于另一个区，而且战后地籍重编过。Berta Weiss说他在那边种了十二年咖啡，Alois Stein 2说“这里只认这里的纸”。Klara扶着他走出公所，他在台阶上坐了很久，皮箱放在脚边。
  _effects: condition; flag +colonial_land_claim_rejected; relation friend with person-berta-weiss; knowledge “the colonial land registry was never transferred”_

- **1940.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara没有立刻答话，先去敲Josef Bauer的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Josef Bauer提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Klara把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1941.01** `r15-wish.power-intrigue::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/crossed-the-line` tags: politics, power, wish, intrigue, office, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara让人去查Friedrich Hofer的私人材料。材料没有查到，但查的人被看见了。第二天，Anton Weiss 2把Klara叫去，只说了一句：“你比我以为的更急。”
  _effects: reputation -0.06; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:687; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

  队伍在公路边停下，Alois Moser 2在田埂上支了一口锅，一勺开水两分钱。Klara替Friedrich Hofer买了一勺，Alois Moser 2又多舀了半勺，说“老人不算钱”。Alois Stein 2在桥头喊队伍继续走，Friedrich Hofer把搪瓷杯揣进怀里，水洒了一路。
  _effects: cash -2 (公路边的开水锅); food; flag +bought_hot_water_on_the_road; relation friend with person-friedrich-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1942.06** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.bridge` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Klara在桥边站了一会儿，看一艘平底船靠岸卸煤，几个工人用铁锹往岸上甩。风把煤灰吹到她脸上，她抹了一把，往回走时脚步比来时轻，回到巷口还给自己买了块三赫勒的糖。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖)_

- **1942.09** `oj18-mortuary.first-body::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/coffin-coop` tags: death, mortuary, funeral, work, odd_job, night, everyday, vienna
  师傅Elisabeth Huber把一块冷毛巾塞进Klara手里，让Klara先擦死者的脸，再擦自己的。殡葬合作社的规矩写在门背后：先入殓，后结账；无名者也要有一块写着日期的木牌。Klara做完第一具，手抖得点不着烟。
  _effects: organization_found 圣马克斯殡葬合作社 (other); position 入殓工 @mortuary:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:011150; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:753; cash 1 (first mortuary wages); skill; flag +mortuary_worker; knowledge “殡葬合作社的规矩：先入殓，后结账；无名者也要有一块日期木牌。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1942.11** `ld18-performance.tango::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/two-women-tango` tags: performance, desire, dance, public, queer, nightlife, public_desire, vienna
  Klara和Helene Hofer在工人舞会上跳了一支探戈，两个女人贴得很近，乐队起先乱了半拍，随后有人吹口哨。第二天，舞厅门口卖票的人说，昨晚来看“那两个女人”的人比看乐队的人还多。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:754; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001432:child; cash 1 (the dance fee and the extra tickets); reputation -0.030000000000000027; flag +tango_in_public; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1943.01** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/appointed-ally` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Klara把Josef Hofer的名字写在“联络员”后面，月薪一克朗，职责是每周来两次、把外面的消息带进来。Mitzi Moser 2说这个岗位没有编制，Klara说先按临时用工算；第一个月，Josef Hofer带来的消息比文件还多。
  _effects: position 联络员 @mortuary:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:011150; organization_funds; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001627:child; flag +appointed_ally; knowledge “联络员不在正式编制里，工资走临时用工。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Klara的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Klara想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 25 -> 9; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Klara，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Klara没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 45 -> 53; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 15 -> 19; reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Klara在Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus召集了一次小范围会议，和Grete Stein把下一次行动、会费和分工写在纸上。
  _effects: project; historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1933 as organizer; historical_influence; knowledge “我召集了一次关于Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Öster的会议。”; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.05** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-shared-crates` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  木箱里装的是钉子，一车推上斜坡时Klara在前头拉，Anna Moser 2在后头推。结账时工头只点了一个名字，Anna Moser 2的嗓门整条巷子都听得见，最后多给了两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (争执后补到的搬运工钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  面试在一间改作办公室的公寓里进行。圣马克斯殡葬合作社把三把椅子摆成一排，自己坐中间，让Klara坐最边上。问到第三句，圣马克斯殡葬合作社忽然说：“客人若认出你，你先介绍店，还是先介绍自己？”Klara说先介绍店；圣马克斯殡葬合作社在纸上画了一个圈，又把它涂成实心。

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Klara的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Klara想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 9 -> 0; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 45 -> 35; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Klara，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Klara没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 61 -> 69; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 23 -> 27; reputation -0.02999999999999997; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.01** `agency:recruit_members` tags: agency, agency:recruit_members
  Klara找到Elisabeth Bauer，把圣马克斯殡葬合作社的章程和会费说清楚；对方留下地址，答应下次再来。
  _effects: organization_membership; knowledge “我通过Klara加入了圣马克斯殡葬合作社。”_

- **1945.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara没有立刻答话，先去敲Anna Bauer的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Anna Bauer提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Klara把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1945.04** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Klara把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Josef Hofer递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (屋里整天没生旺火，受冻); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001627:child_

- **1945.06** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.school-fork/ask-for-reduction` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara带着成绩单去学校找Grete Stein，问减免和奖学金。Grete Stein领到办公室，一位Elisabeth Bauer翻着册子说可以减一半书本费，服装费仍得自己出，答复要等开学前一周。Klara把两张纸重新折好，回家没有对母亲提准信，只把桌布底下的决定又压了几天。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +awaiting_school_aid_

- **1946.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013309_

  午休短，他跑过去时嘴里还嚼着面包。对方说缺熟手，一天一克朗六十分，明天就能上工。回来圣马克斯殡葬合作社冷着脸扣钱，Elisabeth Bauer靠在门边没吭声，只帮他把工具袋口系紧。

- **1946.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Klara攥紧扶手。Mitzi站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.06000000000000001 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.07** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Anton Winter没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Klara听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000140:child; flag +funeral_society_left_

  Maria Berg 3嫌Klara的床单滴水滴到Maria Berg 3家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Klara下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Klara把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Maria Berg 3那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

### 230 subject events

- **1914.01** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-coat-is-just-a-coat` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, rural, agriculture
  Rosa Lechner把Mitzi抱起来，走到衣钩下面，让孩子伸手去抓那件旧大衣。粗呢的袖口擦过掌心，Mitzi先愣住，随后皱着鼻子哭了一声。Rosa Lechner笑着把大衣掀开，说“看，只有衣服”，灯影也跟着晃了一下。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +learned_the_coat_rack_

- **1914.02** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/a-word-for-bread` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, rural, agriculture
  Mitzi指着桌上的面包说出一个短词，声音轻得像在试音。Rosa Lechner把面包掰下一角，泡进热水里喂给孩子；Mitzi吃完又指了一次，这次说得更清楚。厨房里的人都笑了，连灶上的水汽也像在往上冒。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +first_word_bread_

- **1914.02** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Rosa Lechner提热水壶往木盆里兑水时壶底脱了焊，滚水溅在左手背上。她咬着牙把壶搁下，Mitzi被响声惊得大哭。手背起了水泡，接下来三天她只能用一只手洗尿布。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (左手被开水烫伤); mood -0.05_

- **1917.07** `kernel-child.first-school-day::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-school-back-bench` tags: childhood, school, enrolment, teacher, fees, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Grete Stein让新生挨个伸手，看指甲，又让念一遍主祷文。Mitzi念到一半卡住，Grete Stein用尺子敲了敲桌沿，说回家再练。座位被排到最后一排，靠门，冬天风从缝里灌进来。Mitzi把石板立在膝上，尽量不让手抖。
  _effects: education primary; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; condition; flag +back_bench_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-neighbor-speaks` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “记我名下。”Ferdinand Hofer说完，掌柜才把秤砣挪开。账本上多出两赫勒的利息，煤筐却只装了半下。Mitzi跟着Ferdinand Hofer走出门，Ferdinand Hofer低声嘱咐明天先把钱送来，别让掌柜再找到话头。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (赊账添的两赫勒利息（其中0.02克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-day/recites` tags: childhood, school, hunger
  Alois Stein让Mitzi站到黑板前背乘法表。孩子背到七乘八时停了一下，还是背完了；放学时手指冻得发红，石板上的字被袖子擦掉一半，Alois Stein在名册上给了一个小小的勾，Mitzi把石板抱在胸前，一路没舍得放进书包。
  _effects: education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1919.01** `iw18-play.toy-theatre::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-audience-claps` tags: childhood, play, theatre, family, wonder, comic, rural, agriculture
  Mitzi用鞋盒搭了一个戏台，把画在硬纸上的国王、仆人和一条狗插在缝里。Rosa Lechner坐在床沿当观众，看到国王被狗追下台时笑出了声。Mitzi谢幕三次，最后把那只狗也抱出来，说它才是主角。
  _effects: mood 0.05; skill; reputation 0.01; flag +toy_theatre_performed_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Rosa Lechner把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Mitzi伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Rosa Lechner从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Mitzi手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Mitzi一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1919.04** `iw18-friend.chair-by-the-stove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/a-guest-named-by-the-child` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, family, wonder, rural, agriculture
  Mitzi从碗橱里多拿了一只缺口的盘子，摆在灶边那把没人坐的椅子上，还认真地告诉屋里的人：今天有客人。盘子里放着一小块土豆和一片面包边。Rosa Lechner问客人叫什么，Mitzi说了一个从没听过的名字。
  _effects: state_add iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:30; food; mood 0.04; flag +invisible_guest_named; flag +imaginary_friend_active_

- **1919.10** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/vinegar-compress` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  没有钱请医生，Alois Lechner把醋瓶拎到床边，拿两只碗轮换着兑水。Mitzi烧得嘴唇起皮，Alois Lechner用小勺喂了两口温水，又把冷布敷上去。鸡叫时热度退了一点，Mitzi睁开眼，看见Alois Lechner趴在床沿，手指还攥着湿布。
  _effects: health 0.03 (湿敷后退了烧); mood 0.019999999999999997; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1920.01** `iw18-friend.sugar-cube::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-cube-disappears` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, food, family, comic, rural, agriculture
  Mitzi把家里最后一块方糖放在空椅子旁边的碟子里，说是给朋友留的。下午糖不见了，Mitzi一口咬定是朋友吃了；晚上Rosa从口袋里摸出黏着糖渣的纸，笑得直不起腰。Mitzi追着Rosa绕了三圈桌子。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 60 -> 64; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 25 -> 28; relation kin with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1920.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/fading` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  Mitzi这一年很少再和那把椅子说话。学校里有了新的同桌，放学后要在楼梯间跳格子；有一天Mitzi把书包扔在椅面上，才想起自己已经三天没有给朋友留位置。椅面凉得很快，像一块普通木头。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 64 -> 56; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.boldness 40 -> 38; mood -0.009999999999999995_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Katharina Lechner的鞋掉在井台边，Mitzi先去捡鞋，回头才看见Katharina Lechner摔在湿石头上。Hermann Kral用围裙裹住Katharina Lechner，血从膝盖渗出来。Alois Lechner夜里把Mitzi叫到灶台前，数落了很久，最后把锅里剩下的半勺汤倒回大锅，只给Mitzi留了半碗。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.06; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1920.03** `vienna-childhood-friends.hand-in-hand/glove-left-behind` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  手套是灰蓝色的，指尖磨得发亮。同桌在课间把它推到Mitzi手边，说自己戴不着。放学后同桌先走了，手套落在抽屉最里面。Mitzi把它卷起来塞进衣兜，回家后藏进枕头套，谁问都说没看见。
  _effects: mood 0.060000000000000005; condition_

- **1920.04** `r1-child.change-counted-wrong::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/blamed-for-the-missing-coin` tags: numeracy, shop, money, humiliation, family, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Alois Lechner发现钱少了，以为Mitzi偷买糖，打了一巴掌。Mitzi哭着说出柜台缝的事，Alois Lechner第二天去铺子里吵，Hermann Kral赔了钱，但再也不肯赊账。Mitzi从此买东西前先把价钱在心里算三遍。
  _effects: cash 1 (少找的一枚十字币); flag +family_cut_off_from_credit; skill; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation rival with person-hermann-kral_

- **1920.04** `state_reaction:wish-knowledge/knowledge-humiliation` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_reaction
  Mitzi把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.04; flag +wish:knowledge:wounded_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001114_

- **1920.07** `kernel-child.sibling-illness::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-illness-neighbor-soup` tags: childhood, family, illness, care, night, rural, agriculture, small_town
  邻居Berta Hofer端来一锅汤，说家里孩子也病过，医院会给穷人减费。Rosa Lechner抱着Rosa去了门诊，排到午后，医生开了药，收了三个克朗。回家的电车上，Rosa第一次睁开眼要水喝。那锅汤的咸味Mitzi记了很久。
  _effects: food; cash -3 (reduced outpatient fee and medicine（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (outpatient treatment); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

  _effects: state_remove iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:30; flag -imaginary_friend_active_

- **1921.01** `r13-edu.school-roll-call::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/truancy-notice` tags: education, school, attendance, childhood, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Mitzi在巷口替人跑腿，忘了下午的课。第二天督学站在教室后排，Mitzi Moser把缺课的名字念出来，Rosa Lechner被叫到学校，罚了两克朗，回家路上没有跟Mitzi说话。那晚Mitzi把跑腿挣的两枚硬币放在桌上，谁也没动。第二天一早自己去了学校。
  _effects: cash -2 (点名册上的第三个空位); mood -0.05; flag +truancy_notice; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1921.09** `r1-child.library-card::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school::reading-room-culture/third-name-on-the-card` tags: library, reading, books, poverty, school, university, culture, schooling
  Mitzi在借书卡上看见前两个名字都被划掉了，第三个空着。Elisabeth Huber说卡上只能写三个名字，写满就换新卡。Mitzi借了一本讲机器的书，回家路上把书贴在胸口，怕雨点打湿。
  _effects: flag +first_library_card; role; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1922.01** `r14-edu.music-lessons::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/self-taught-on-old-piano` tags: education, arts_training, music, craft, university, culture, schooling, school
  Mitzi交不起学费，Grete Stein把旧琴谱借给Mitzi，说先自己练，有问题再来问。Mitzi把琴谱抄在牛皮纸上，用家里的风琴练指法。邻居来敲门，说吵，Mitzi就在琴键之间垫一条布，声音闷得像从隔壁传来。琴谱一克朗，是Grete Stein替Mitzi垫的。
  _effects: cash -1 (琴房里漏风的窗); flag +self_taught_musician; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1922.08** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Mitzi在土豆摊前蹲了很久，挑了一磅没有芽眼的。付完钱，手里只剩几个铜板，又去买了盐。围裙兜太浅，Mitzi用下巴压住布边，走两步就低头看一眼。到家Alois Lechner把土豆倒进盆里，盐包放在灶台边。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Mitzi拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Hermann Kral弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Mitzi接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1923.04** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书页在桌肚里翻动时，Hermann Weiss已经站在旁边。Mitzi的手还按在算术本上，书被抽走，书脊在讲台边磕了一声。Hermann Weiss看了一眼折角，说“放学留下”，又拉开抽屉把书放进去。Mitzi盯着抽屉缝，整节课没听进去。
  _effects: mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1923.04** `r14-edu.humiliation::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/caned-for-answering` tags: education, school_humiliation, reputation, childhood, university, culture, schooling, school
  Mitzi说补丁是母亲缝的，Emil Hofer让Mitzi伸出手心，用戒尺打了三下。Franz Winter在下面咳了一声。放学后，Mitzi把手泡在井水里，掌心肿得握不住笔。第二天，Mitzi用左手写完了听写，错了一半。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (黑板前的湿鞋印); reputation -0.05; flag +caned_for_answering_back; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1923.11** `r1-child.truancy-vintage::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/officer-on-the-field-path` tags: truancy, rural, harvest, school, labour, university, culture, schooling
  九月的葡萄园缺人手，Rosa Lechner让Mitzi留在家里捡落果，说学校少一个孩子没人会数。第三天，Emil Huber骑着自行车沿田埂找来，车铃在石墙间响。Rosa Lechner说孩子在学怎么活，Emil Huber说活也得先识数。
  _effects: cash 2 (葡萄熟的时候不上学); flag +harvest_absence; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +wish:knowledge:pursuing_

- **1924.01** `r13-edu.ask-teacher::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/note-under-inkwell` tags: education, teacher, help, school, confidence, university, culture, schooling
  Mitzi等到天黑，Grete Stein一直在改别的班的卷子，头也没抬。Mitzi把问题写在纸条上压在墨水瓶下，第二天发现纸条还在原处，只是背面多了一行字：“先看第三章。”Mitzi按纸条翻到第三章，发现那页夹着一张旧的辅助线图，图上的线画得很轻。
  _effects: skill_

- **1924.10** `r3-crime.juvenile-court::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/admonished-and-sent-home` tags: crime, youth, court, family, school, university, culture, schooling
  Josefine Hofer把案卷合上，问Mitzi知不知道错了。Mitzi的腿在长椅上晃着，够不着地，说“知道”。Rosa Lechner在旁边一直点头，Friedrich Hofer把一张写着“训诫”的纸递给她。出门时，Rosa Lechner把纸折好放进内衣口袋，怕人看见。
  _effects: flag +admonished_by_juvenile_court; role; relation guardian with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “the juvenile court keeps a record even without a sentence”_

- **1925.01** `major-education-scholarship-examination/half-scholarship` tags: education, scholarship, adolescence, major
  成绩排在第七名，离全免只差两个名次。Emil Hofer替Mitzi去问，回来把一张纸摊在桌上：学费减半，另给十五克朗书费，车钱和冬衣自理。Rosa Lechner算了一夜，把准备换炉子的钱挪出来，第二天在同意书末尾按了手印。
  _effects: education secondary; cash 15 (half scholarship book grant); role; flag +half_fee_place; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/first-recital` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, university, schooling, student
  Mitzi在期末演奏会上拉完一首短曲，弓毛断了两根，最后一个音还是稳住了。Hermann Weiss在台下点头，师母把手套摘下来鼓掌。回家后，Mitzi把琴擦了三遍，才发现肩托把脖子磨出一道红印，碰一下就疼。Mitzi没有告诉任何人。琴盒没关。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +first_recital; skill_

- **1925.02** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/kept-home-for-kin` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Rosa Lechner病倒在床，Mitzi留在家里看弟妹、煮稀粥、去井边打水。Hermann Weiss托人带来一包旧课本，Mitzi在灶火边翻了两页，弟弟哭了，只好合上。一个月后，学校把Mitzi的名字从名册上划掉，墨迹很淡。
  _effects: mood -0.059999999999999984; flag +left_school_for_family; role_

- **1925.03** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Gustav Berg 3把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Mitzi看期限、工钱和出师考试。Mitzi签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:66; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; education apprenticeship_

- **1925.08** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Mitzi在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Hermann Weiss跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Mitzi去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; education apprenticeship_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Mitzi废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1926.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-improved` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Gustav Lechner把算术本摊在厨房桌上，Mitzi用豆子摆出十以内的加减。Gustav Lechner算错时，Mitzi不骂，只把豆子重新排一遍。两个月后，Gustav Lechner的听写和算术都及格了，Rosa Lechner在饭桌上多给Mitzi盛了半碗汤。
  _effects: flag +tutored_sibling_well; skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000844:child_

- **1926.03** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/wade-in-first` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  多瑙河的浴棚按男女分开，木板缝里能看见对面的人影。Mitzi穿着借来的毛线泳衣，站在第三间门口不敢下水；Rosa从水里冒出来，头发贴在额头上，说水不冷。Mitzi走进河里时，满脑子都是自己肋骨上那道疤。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:97; health 0.020000000000000018 (cold river water and a long swim); mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.04** `r3-teen.scholarship-interview::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-scholarship-board/waitlisted` tags: education, scholarship, interview, class, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Otto Winter把Mitzi的名字写在候补栏第一行，说前面还有一个名额。Emil Hofer说候补也可能轮不到，先别退学。Mitzi回家路上数了两遍名单上的空行，越数越像一排没关紧的窗。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; flag +waitlisted_for_scholarship; knowledge “one scholarship place remains ahead of the subject”_

- **1926.07** `state_reaction:wish-knowledge/knowledge-humiliation` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_reaction
  Mitzi把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002482_

  Mitzi把两件衬衫、一双袜子和小时候的洗礼证明卷进一块旧桌布，塞到床底下。Rosa Lechner在厨房里数硬币，数了三遍都没有抬头；Mitzi躺在黑暗里，听见夜车从远处经过，铁轨的声音一直传到枕头下面。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:96; flag +yf18_bundle_hidden; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  毕业钟敲过三下，Hermann Weiss把成绩单按在讲台上，说Mitzi还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Mitzi签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; education apprenticeship_

- **1927.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Mitzi翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Rosa在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Mitzi翻回来时，Elisabeth Bauer把一支烟塞进Mitzi嘴里，手抖得点不着火。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 60; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 15 -> 30; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 5; health -0.030000000000000027 (cold and fear on the bridge); reputation 0.02; flag +yf18_walked_bridge_rail; mood 0.05000000000000002_

  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 13 -> 33; skill; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 15 -> 27; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 25 -> 27; mood 0.009999999999999995_

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 57 -> 62; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 30 -> 38; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.03_

  Mitzi在寄宿学校住了不到一个月，夜里总听见火车声，想起家里的炉子和妹妹。Mitzi给Rosa Lechner写信，写了撕、撕了写，最后只寄出一张明信片。Rosa Lechner来接Mitzi那天，Mitzi的箱子还没完全打开。回到家，Mitzi把铁皮箱放在床底，再没打开，腌菜罐却一直留在桌上。

  德语作文题是《我的家乡》。Mitzi写了半页多瑙河，剩下半页写一个总在电车上看书的人。Grete Stein没有打分，只在页边写“感情太多，论据太少”，把作文折好放进自己的公文包；放学后，Mitzi看见Klara在门口等，忽然不敢走过去。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 67 -> 73; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 9 -> 12; skill; reputation -0.009999999999999995; mood 0.009999999999999995_

  Mitzi在操场边堵住Lena，问他从哪听来的。他先说是别人说的，后来承认是自己编的，因为Mitzi在课堂上答对了他答错的问题。Emil Hofer让他们各写一份说明，两份都锁进抽屉。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; flag +confronted_rumor_source; relation rival with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the classmate invented the rumour out of envy”_

  Mitzi转到城另一头的学校，校服是二手的，袖口短了一截。新班级里没有人认识Mitzi，也没有人问旧事。Mitzi把头发剪短，成绩单上的名次一点点往前挪。毕业那天，Mitzi没有请旧学校的任何一个人。转学费三克朗。
  _effects: cash -3 (旧校服上的墨迹); reputation 0.04; flag +transferred_after_humiliation_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Friedrich Hofer把Mitzi叫到巷口，问Elisabeth Lechner叫什么、住在哪间屋。Mitzi答到一半就停住了。Friedrich Hofer说这半天的活不算数，说冒名顶替坏了规矩。Mitzi回到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时，Elisabeth Lechner正扶着床沿站着，听完只说“别去了”，说完又低下头，像是自己连累了Mitzi。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000525:child; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西)_

- **1929.08** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/take-it-to-the-director` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi把油印纸交到校长室，Mitzi Moser 2正好坐在里面喝茶。校长问纸是从哪儿来的；Mitzi说在课本里捡的。Grete Stein当天下午被叫走，第二天换了一个代课老师；Mitzi的拉丁文成绩突然好了起来，教室里却没有人再和Mitzi说话。
  _effects: reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +yf18_denounced_teacher; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1930.01** `r14-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/learned-to-read` tags: education, adult_learning, literacy, night_school, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Mitzi下班后不回家，直接去夜校。教室由一间酒馆后屋改成，桌上还留着酒渍。Hermann Weiss从字母表教起，Mitzi把每个字母念三遍，喉咙发干。三个月后，Mitzi第一次独立读完一张报纸上的短讯，把那张报纸叠好，放进工具箱最底层。学费一克朗，Mitzi用加班钱付的。
  _effects: cash -1 (夜校的第二排长凳); flag +adult_literacy; skill_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002174_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Emil的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1930.01** `r13-civ.publish-at-own-cost::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/printer-absconded` tags: culture, publishing, book, debt, reputation, university, schooling, student
  Mitzi把印费交给Ferdinand Kral，说好两周取书。到了日子，铺面已经换了锁，隔壁木匠说Ferdinand Kral连夜搬走了滚筒。Mitzi站在门口，手里还捏着那张写着“五百册”的收据。
  _effects: cash -10 (自费印刷的账单); mood -0.12; flag +printer_absconded_with_printing_fee; knowledge “the printer left with the press and the advance payment”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1930.04** `r1-fam.courtship-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/dance.glove` tags: social, courtship, dance, marriage, chaperone, reform, medical, schooling
  第二支曲子还没完，Karl Kraus就站起来说要回家，说Emil家的父亲欠着裁缝的钱。Mitzi把手套落在椅子上，走到门口才想起来；回去拿时，Emil正把那只手套贴在自己胸口，见她进来，慌忙放下。
  _effects: mood 0.06; flag +glove_left_behind; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “姨母知道男方家欠着裁缝的钱。”_

- **1930.07** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.friend-intercession/intercession-too-far` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Mitzi听到Ferdinand Hofer把工钱和家底夸过了头，脸沉下来，当晚就照地址找上门去解释。话到嘴边绕成一团，Emil的母亲把网兜往桌上一搁，说年轻人有话该早说，不该让旁人来传。Mitzi站在门里，名声在面包店门口折了一道，Emil始终没有出来。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1930.09** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi数出八赫勒交给Ferdinand Hofer，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Ferdinand Hofer说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Mitzi盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1931.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Emil没有答应，只说现在不合适。Mitzi把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004193_

- **1931.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-by-the-day` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne只招日工。Mitzi天亮前到门口，名字写在临时名单上，干一天算一天，一天一克朗，月底结二十六克朗。工头说下个月可能有正式位置，也可能没有；Mitzi把每天的工时记在烟盒背面，怕漏掉一天。
  _effects: employment Tagelöhner @ org-winter-print; role; cash 2 (first daily pay advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; flag +day_labour_only; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Hermann Moser的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1931.01** `r13-edu.lecture-attendance::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-school/lecture-cancelled` tags: education, university, lecture, politics, study, medical, knowledge, school
  Mitzi赶到时，教室门上贴着“因故停课”。Mitzi在走廊里遇到同样白跑的同学，两人去咖啡馆把上周的笔记对了一遍。Mitzi发现自己漏记了整整一页，借对方的本子补到天黑。咖啡馆的灯比教室亮，Mitzi把漏掉的那页补完，才发现自己抄错了行。
  _effects: skill; knowledge “the missed lecture covered the 1896 currency reform”_

- **1931.03** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Otto Weiss排在前面，回头替Mitzi看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Mitzi用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; mood 0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1931.05** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Mitzi看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; health -0.04000000000000001 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912_

- **1932.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Hermann Moser在几天后找到Mitzi，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; household; role; role; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004651_

- **1932.01** `r14-edu.first-recital::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/broken-string` tags: education, arts_training, music, performance, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Mitzi把琴谱翻到最后一页，琴弦突然断了一根，声音像鞭子。Grete Stein上台把琴盖合上，说今天就到这里。Mitzi蹲下去捡断弦，手指被钢丝划破。Maria Kral 2在幕布后面看着，没有出来。修琴八克朗，Mitzi分了三个月还。
  _effects: cash -8 (礼堂第三排的掌声); mood -0.12; flag +instrument_broken_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001555_

- **1932.01** `r14-edu.sibling-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-passed` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, exam, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Gustav Lechner从考场出来，手里攥着准考证，说最后一道题做出来了。放榜那天，Mitzi陪Gustav Lechner去看榜，名字在中间偏下，够了。Gustav Lechner跳起来，又立刻站好，回头问Mitzi能不能把旧课本留下。报名费两克朗，Mitzi替Gustav Lechner交了。
  _effects: cash -2 (弟弟妹妹的准考证); flag +sibling_passed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000844:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002649_

- **1932.01** `r14-edu.self-taught-examination::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/passed-civil-exam` tags: education, adult_learning, self_taught, library, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Mitzi用夜校的结业证报了市政招考，考的是公文抄写和简单算术。考场设在旧修道院，桌腿一高一低，Mitzi垫了张折起来的传单。放榜那天，Hermann Weiss比Mitzi先看到名字，隔着街喊了一声，声音哑了。月薪六克朗，Mitzi第一次有了自己的办公桌。
  _effects: cash 6 (借书证背面的铅笔号); flag +passed_self_taught_exam; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; education secondary_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003991_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Mitzi在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Mitzi的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `r13-edu.law-study::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-university/case-reasoned-both-ways` tags: education, law, university, study, court, medical, knowledge, student
  Mitzi把一桩房租纠纷的判例背得滚瓜烂熟，Emil Hofer在课上让Mitzi复述判决理由。Mitzi讲到一半，Emil Hofer打断，问如果换了被告的处境会怎么判。Mitzi想了很久，说可能不一样。Emil Hofer说，这才是学法律的开头，然后让全班把这个问题抄下来。问题写了满满一页。
  _effects: flag +law_study_progress; skill; education higher_

- **1932.03** `major-education-university-registration/rejected-documents` tags: education, university, migration, major
  窗口在十二点关上。Friedrich Hofer把材料推回来：拉丁文课时不够，中学毕业证上的印章也模糊。Mitzi问能不能先注册再补，Friedrich Hofer说下一位。走出注册处时，雨把税单打湿了一角；四十五克朗还在口袋里，但名册上没有编号。
  _effects: cash -6 (certified copies and stamps); reputation -0.039999999999999994; flag +matriculation_rejected; role; mood -0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1932.05** `vienna-printshop-daily.wage-shortfall/wage-take-it` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  Mitzi把少了钱的工钱袋塞进内袋，没再问。回家路上买了两块面包，比平时少一块。
  _effects: cash -1.8 (工钱被扣); food; mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1932.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.work-injury/union-rep` tags: health, medical, family, money
  工会代表从内袋掏出铅笔头，舔一下笔尖才写。事故单摊在工具箱上，工头说机器本来就该换了，代表不接话，只把编号念了两遍。Mitzi举着伤手站在旁边，听见远处另一台冲床还在响。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (工会垫付后自理的零头); health 0.06000000000000005 (及时就医处理伤口); flag +union_contact_

- **1933.01** `r14-edu.sibling-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/sibling-left-for-work` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, exam, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Gustav Lechner没有等到毕业，跟着同乡去了铁路工地上工。走的那天，Mitzi把攒下的两克朗塞进Gustav Lechner的口袋，Gustav Lechner没有推辞。火车开走后，Mitzi在站台上站了很久，手里还拿着那本没讲完的算术书。
  _effects: cash 2 (弟弟妹妹的准考证); flag +sibling_left_school; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000844:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003041_

- **1933.01** `r14-edu.women-barred::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/gave-up-the-course` tags: education, school_humiliation, barred_from_course, gender_barrier, university, culture, schooling, school
  Mitzi把申请表折好，没有再交。Rosa Lechner说女孩子读书读到这个份上已经够了，不如早点找个稳当的活。Mitzi去布店做了售货员，站柜台时看见女学生从门口经过，抱着书，走得很快。Mitzi低下头，把一匹布重新卷紧。柜台工钱四克朗。
  _effects: cash 4 (讲堂门口的名单); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +abandoned_higher_education; role_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003518_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-depression/laid-off` tags: adulthood, depression, work, unemployment
  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Mitzi的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1933.01** `task-vienna-bourgeois-youth.salon-invitation/polite-tea` tags: bourgeois, education, culture, salon, university, family, task, task:social
  Rosa Lechner亲手给Mitzi倒了茶，问了他父亲做什么生意，然后转身去招呼新到的客人。整晚没有人再来搭话，Mitzi在窗边站到八点半，把茶喝完了才走。
  _effects: mood -0.08999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner; skill_

- **1933.02** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1933.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1933.04** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1934.01** `kernel-family.housing-relative::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement::housing-relative-rural/housing-relative.harvest-refused` tags: task, task:family, family, housing, relatives, godparent, guardian, household
  收成不好，Mitzi自家也缺人手，只能让Anton去别家问。Anton走时把工具留在门口，说等明年再来；Mitzi把工具收进棚里，心里知道明年未必有活。棚里的工具落了灰，他也没去擦。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; role; flag +harvest_relative_refused_

- **1934.06** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.repair-shop` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Mitzi用围裙裹着钟走到钟表铺，铺子里的老头把钟翻过来看了看，说要换发条，连工带料一克朗二十赫勒。她算了算这星期的菜钱，把钟又裹回围裙里提回家，搁在柜上，没再提修的事。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1935.01** `state_reaction:wish-knowledge/knowledge-humiliation` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_reaction
  Mitzi把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006762_

- **1935.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/pay-small-part` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  替他还了四克朗，剩下的数目不碰，条件是把赌债字据交出来，当天就去戒酒会挂个名。对方把字据给了，人却没去挂名。四克朗从工资袋里出去，母亲那瓶药只好再等十天，晚上咳得更紧。
  _effects: cash -4 (替友人偿还部分赌债); health -0.030000000000000027 (母亲的药钱被挪去还债，家里几夜没睡好); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1935.04** `vienna-history-kraus-lecture/question-the-reader` tags: history, culture, karl_kraus, cafe_central, public_lecture, press
  提问时，Mitzi 问Karl Kraus 是否也会嘲笑普通读者的迟钝。Karl Kraus 盯着Mitzi 看了几秒，说“我先嘲笑自己，再嘲笑你们”；台下有人笑，也有人起身。Mitzi 没有等到散场，只把没喝完的咖啡钱压在杯底。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; knowledge “Kraus 的公开朗读既是文学批评，也是当场点名新闻界的表演；被点名的人可能在场。”; cash -2 (coffee and admission to the reading); reputation -0.010000000000000002_

- **1935.08** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, injury
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Hermann Moser在楼梯间坐了很久；Mitzi在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Mitzi的名字；此后邻居提起Mitzi，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: health -0.12 (stillbirth); cash -6 (midwife and burial); role_

- **1935.08** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Mitzi对Mitzi Winter说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Mitzi Winter把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Mitzi的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000436:child_

- **1935.11** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Mitzi把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Ferdinand Berg站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Mitzi手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.give-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Mitzi只喝了半杯就看见门口站着等位的老主顾，手里转着帽子。Mitzi把报纸叠好放回木架，起身让出窗边的位子，一杯 Melange 三十赫勒，侍者点头致意，没再收第二杯的钱。走出门时雨小了些，Mitzi站在檐下理了理衣领，觉得这半个下午也不算白来。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (一杯 Melange 的价钱); reputation 0.03; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Ferdinand 2这周主动擦了黑板。Mitzi把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007775_

- **1936.03** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  Mitzi点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Anton Winter量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); health -0.040000000000000036 (通宵缝纫，眼睛疲劳，手指扎伤); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.10** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Ferdinand Berg合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1937.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Mitzi在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg生下一个男婴，取名Otto Moser。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Hermann Moser把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Mitzi躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.07999999999999996 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:522_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Mitzi把Ferdinand 2的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Ferdinand 2今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Mitzi听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `p10-family-tasks.appeal-inheritance/appeal-inheritance.court` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, appeal
  职员把信翻过来看背面，说私人纠纷不归这里管，去法院。律师的写字间在隔壁楼，开价三十克朗。Mitzi数了数证件夹里的钱，差得远。兄弟在门口说，那就等传票。Mitzi把信收好，下楼时鞋跟磕在石阶上。
  _effects: mood -0.14; relation rival with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “遗产诉讼费要三十克朗，办事处不受理私人纠纷”_

- **1937.03** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Hermann Moser却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Mitzi把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1937.03** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Ferdinand Berg排在前面，回头替Mitzi看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Mitzi用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.04** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Otto Moser这周主动擦了黑板。Mitzi把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008117_

- **1937.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Ferdinand Berg抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Mitzi把床单对折，腾出一半，Ferdinand Berg用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005160_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938, injury
  Mitzi在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Friedrich Hofer记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Mitzi带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: health -0.12 (beaten in police custody); condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1938.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Mitzi在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg生下一个女婴，取名Helene Moser。接生的人收六克朗，Hermann Moser在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Mitzi起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.08000000000000002 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:558_

  夜里疼醒时，Mitzi摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Ferdinand Hofer第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Mitzi只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); health -0.020000000000000018 (牙病拖延未治); condition_

  Mitzi在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Friedrich Winter从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.04999999999999999 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000323:child_

- **1939.01** `r14-edu.sibling-favour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sibling-paid-the-fees` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, reunion, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Gustav Lechner在城里站稳了脚，托人带来一个信封，里面是八克朗和一张夜校报名单。信上只写：当年你教我算术，现在轮到我付学费。Mitzi把报名单看了三遍，填好，第二天一早就去交了。
  _effects: cash 8 (多年后的一封家信); flag +sibling_repaid_tutoring; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000844:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004951_

- **1939.01** `vienna-family-divorce/separate-under-one-roof` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation
  Mitzi和Hermann Moser把床铺分开，米和煤也各算各的。两人仍住在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，因为谁都没有钱再租一间房；孩子在两床之间来回，三克朗买了隔开的锅碗和煤油，家里从此有两本账，夜里两盏灯先后熄灭。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; cash -3 (separate household goods（其中3克朗记入债务）)_

- **1939.01** `sm18-object.tram-lost::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/accuses-the-conductor` tags: object, loss, child, tram, family, everyday, vienna, urban
  Mitzi认定是电车售票员把纸包收进了自己的口袋，在站台上拦住他，声音大得整条街都回头。售票员把票夹和口袋都翻出来，里面只有几张旧票和一只坏表。Josef Lechner站在后面，把脸埋进围巾里。
  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:603; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000651:child; reputation -0.03; flag +accused_the_tram_conductor; flag +something_is_missing_

  Mitzi数出八赫勒交给Otto Winter，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Otto Winter说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Mitzi盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

  Franz Lechner看完租约，没有加价，也没有提补充条款。Mitzi把房租数好，放在桌上；Franz Lechner数了一遍，又从里面拿出一枚硬币，说“上次多收了”。Mitzi接过硬币，觉得这一年总算有一件事没有变。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid on time（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +rent_renewed; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.07** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Mitzi请楼下的Ferdinand Bauer白天来照看Alois Lechner，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Ferdinand Bauer上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Mitzi下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Alois Lechner的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; health 0.04 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

- **1940.09** `r3-mig.remittance-failure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::war-severed-post/returned-address-unknown` tags: migration, remittance, family, failure, money, vienna, urban, crisis
  Alois Stein 2把汇款单和一张退条一起推过来，退条上盖着“地址不明”。Mitzi说上个月还寄到过，Alois Stein 2说“上个月是上个月”。五克朗扣了两克朗手续费，退回来的钱只够买三天的面包。
  _effects: cash -5 (退回来的汇款单（其中2.2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.08000000000000007; condition; flag +remittance_returned_address_unknown; knowledge “returned remittances lose the fee twice”_

- **1941.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007421_

- **1941.01** `r14-edu.sibling-favour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sibling-paid-the-fees` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, reunion, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Gustav Lechner在城里站稳了脚，托人带来一个信封，里面是八克朗和一张夜校报名单。信上只写：当年你教我算术，现在轮到我付学费。Mitzi把报名单看了三遍，填好，第二天一早就去交了。
  _effects: cash 8 (多年后的一封家信); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000844:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005468_

- **1941.01** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/meat-ledger` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  肉商的账本上少了二十公斤马肉，Alois Lechner要Mitzi签字认账。Mitzi知道是夜班门房把肉转卖给了普拉特的香肠摊，但门房是Alois Lechner的小舅子。Mitzi不签，第二天被调去扫兽栏；臭味洗了三遍还在指甲缝里。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; cash -1 (lost the night-shift supplement); mood -0.030000000000000027; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:691; flag +zoo_night_shift; flag +refused_meat_ledger; knowledge “少掉的二十公斤马肉被夜班门房卖给了香肠摊，门房是园长的亲戚。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Mitzi被叫到学校，老师说Ferdinand 2这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Mitzi没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 42 -> 37; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 39 -> 44; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Mitzi被叫到学校，老师说Helene Moser这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Mitzi没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。

  Mitzi把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Mitzi忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 43 -> 51; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 11 -> 9_

- **1941.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.quit-job` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  Mitzi把半天工的工钱单交回去，换回自己看孩子。晚饭从两顿并成一顿，土豆和面包皮煮一锅，伴侣把煤钱数了两遍。孩子睡下后，Mitzi在窗边缝袜子，一枚针扎了手指，她含住指头继续缝。
  _effects: cash -4 (辞掉半天工少了一份收入（其中3克朗记入债务）); health -0.04 (营养不足和夜里缝补的劳累); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1941.05** `lc17-care.offers-to-care::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hires-the-neighbour` tags: care, family, aging, health, household, duty, vienna, urban
  Mitzi算了一夜账，第二天去敲楼下退休护士的门。看护每天下午来两小时，工钱按周付；Alois Lechner嫌贵，Mitzi说“总比我辞工强”。第一周结束时，药盒上的时间第一次没有写错。
  _effects: state_add lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:689; cash -3 (the first week of paid care（其中3克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; flag +paid_for_parent_care_

- **1941.05** `bc18-inherit.forty-seven-franz::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/sell-the-horses` tags: inheritance, mistaken_identity, debt, funeral, work, absurd, family, vienna
  Mitzi把灵车和两匹马卖给了城西的殡仪馆，拿到的钱正好抵掉欠账，只剩下一盒黑手套和半瓶擦铜油。Friedrich Hofer说这笔遗产算是结清了；Mitzi却总觉得，自己像替一个陌生人做了一场没有尸体的买卖。
  _effects: cash 3 (卖掉灵车和马匹的余款); reputation -0.019999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; condition; flag +sold_inherited_hearse; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1941.05** `ld18-fitting.measurement::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/the-mother-sees` tags: love, desire, erotic, craft, queer, fitting, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Anna Stein的母亲在试衣间外站了很久，忽然进来替女儿拉上帘子。她没有骂人，只把工钱放在桌上，说以后不必再来了。Mitzi把钱收好，出门时听见Anna Stein在里面说“妈”，声音像被布料闷住了。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:690; reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1942.01** `r14-edu.music-teacher-legacy::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/inherited-the-scores` tags: education, arts_training, music, legacy, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Grete Stein退休前把一箱琴谱交给Mitzi，说里面有些是自己抄的，错音不少，别照着弹。Mitzi翻到一本旧练习曲，页边有铅笔写的指法，还有几处被橡皮擦破的洞。Grete Stein说这些谱子跟了自己四十年，现在该换个地方落灰了。箱底还有三克朗纸币。
  _effects: cash 3 (琴谱边上的铅笔字); flag +inherited_music_scores; skill; relation colleague with person-grete-stein_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004950_

- **1942.01** `ad20-school-meeting::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/teacher-blames` tags: family, school, child, meeting, everyday, public, vienna, urban
  Grete Stein说Otto Moser这学期退步，是因为家里晚上太吵。Mitzi想说自己夜里在做计件活，话到嘴边又咽回去；Otto Moser站在走廊尽头，书包带子断了一边。回家的路上，Mitzi把书包带打了个结，谁也没有提老师的话。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 58 -> 54; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; cash -1 (extra exercise books after the meeting)_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/worn-thin` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Alois Lechner今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Mitzi在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 20 -> 28; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 5 -> 11; cash -2 (medicine and hospital fees)_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Mitzi是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Mitzi在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 30 -> 26; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 25 -> 27; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  两年过去，Mitzi已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Mitzi也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 18 -> 28; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 22 -> 32; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

  Emil Lechner 3没有接话，先从抽屉里撕下一角纸。Mitzi把名字、住址和手艺一样样说完，工头边写边应，写完把纸压回抽屉，说月底来问。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1942.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Franz Winter把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Mitzi打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1942.07** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sibling-medicine-bill/refuse-push-bill` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Mitzi把药费单从糖罐底下抽出来，对折，推回桌子中央，说上个月的工钱已经贴过煤钱，这回一个赫勒也拿不出。Rosa的脸沉下来，练习本翻得哗哗响，最后把单子重新压回糖罐底下，起身去够门后的外套。三只咖啡杯里的渣子凉在桌上，兄妹这一回把话都说薄了。

- **1943.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Mitzi把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Mitzi来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1943.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.neighbor` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  老太太接过孩子，用旧围裙兜着坐在窗边，说一克朗一周，六点送来六点接。Mitzi数出硬币，把奶瓶放在她灶台边。第三夜孩子还是哭醒，Mitzi抱着他在屋里走，天亮前靠着床沿打了个盹。
  _effects: cash -1 (每周付邻居老太太的照看费); relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss_

- **1943.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-wedding-invitation` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  请柬上写着Mitzi的名字，旁边用铅笔注了一句“一个人来也可以”。婚礼上，新娘把手捧花直接递给她，笑着说“下一个该你了”；同桌的人都在等Mitzi怎么回答。她把花放在膝上，说这花很香。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 27 -> 35; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 39 -> 44; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012243_

- **1943.04** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Mitzi的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Mitzi家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011641_

- **1943.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/reply-at-bakery` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  第四天，Grete Winter 2的回信从门缝底下塞进来，纸边被门槛磨毛了。Grete Winter 2约Mitzi星期六早上在街角面包店门口见，说想买一个两赫勒的小圆面包，两个人分。Anna Huber 2在楼道里撞见Mitzi读信，笑着问是不是好消息；Mitzi把信折回四折，塞进内袋贴着胸口。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; mood 0.07000000000000006_

- **1943.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Friedrich Winter在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Friedrich Winter敲开Mitzi的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000323:child; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1943.08** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +watch_pawned_

- **1943.11** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Mitzi眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Gustav Lechner隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); food_

- **1944.01** `pc20-family.parent-mistake::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cover` tags: parent, child, mistake, second_chance, family, school, repetition, intervention
  Mitzi替Anton Lechner向Grete Stein请了病假，又把那件可能惹麻烦的东西藏进自己的工具箱。Grete Stein看了Mitzi一眼，说病假可以补，别的东西补不了；Anton Lechner在里屋听见，门没有关严。
  _effects: state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:851; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004125:child; flag +covered_child_mistake; knowledge “病假可以补，别的东西补不了。”; flag +second_chance_open_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Mitzi是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Mitzi在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 34 -> 30; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 43 -> 45; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Mitzi走到Berggasse 19, Wohnung und Ordination门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1944.03** `oj18-taxi.night-fare::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/coop-keys` tags: taxi, night, driver, passenger, work, odd_job, everyday, vienna
  合作社的调度Alois Lechner把一枚铜牌和一把车钥匙推过柜台，说夜班只跑环城路，十一点以后不接去普拉特的车。Mitzi把铜牌挂在后视镜上，第一晚就拉了七个客人，最后一个在车上睡着，打鼾像一台小马达。
  _effects: organization_found 第二区夜班出租合作社 (other); position 夜班司机 @taxi:person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:012566; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:852; cash 2 (night taxi fares); flag +taxi_night_shift; knowledge “夜班只跑环城路，十一点以后不接去普拉特的车。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1944.06** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-wedding-invitation` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  请柬上写着Mitzi的名字，旁边用铅笔注了一句“一个人来也可以”。婚礼上，新娘把手捧花直接递给她，笑着说“下一个该你了”；同桌的人都在等Mitzi怎么回答。她把花放在膝上，说这花很香。

- **1944.06** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1944.09** `lc17-care.offers-to-care::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/hires-the-neighbour` tags: care, family, aging, health, household, duty, vienna, urban
  Mitzi算了一夜账，第二天去敲楼下退休护士的门。看护每天下午来两小时，工钱按周付；Alois Lechner嫌贵，Mitzi说“总比我辞工强”。第一周结束时，药盒上的时间第一次没有写错。
  _effects: state_add lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:853; cash -3 (the first week of paid care); relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1944.10** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1944.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Mitzi躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Mitzi去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

- **1945.01** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hired-competent` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  Mitzi把Rosa Bauer 2的名字写在编制表的空行上，又在旁边注了“试用三个月”。Alois Lechner提醒说预算只够半个位置，Mitzi还是把名字留下了。第二天，Rosa Bauer 2提前半小时到，把走廊里的旧档案按年份重新排了一遍。
  _effects: position 助理文书 @taxi:person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:012566; organization_funds; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; reputation 0.03; flag +hired_protege; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Mitzi是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Mitzi在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 30 -> 26; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 45 -> 47; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/small-relapse` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Mitzi晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Mitzi听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 40 -> 33; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 50 -> 58; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 40 -> 43; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/learned-the-building` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  又过了一个冬天，Mitzi能从楼梯的响声判断是谁在值夜，也能从煤气灯的颜色判断哪一段管道该修。师傅把巡查路线改了三次，最后仍然照Mitzi画的那张走；两个人都没有提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 40; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 20; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/carried` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Mitzi又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Alois Lechner今年记得住Mitzi的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 20 -> 16; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 5 -> 3; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1945.01** `p9-history-public-action.organize-relief/church-hall` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  教堂侧厅的长凳被挪到墙边，旧锅架在砖灶上，Katharina Lechner 蹲着添柴，Mitzi 把补好的账页贴在门板。第一锅汤滚起来时，排队的人从门口一直站到院里。
  _effects: cash -4 (垫付首日面粉和柴火); food; reputation 0.07; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000651:child_

- **1945.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1945.04** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1945.12** `r2-uni.fact-check::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/missed-the-deadline` tags: media, newspaper, fact_check, deadline, error, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi把校样交回去时，中间名还是错的。第二天，Maria Lechner 2来信说那位先生已经去世，家属要求更正。Elisabeth Bauer在更正栏里加了一行小字，Mitzi把自己的名字从“校对”一栏划掉。
  _effects: flag +missed_fact_check_deadline; relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer; knowledge “the deceased man family demanded a correction”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1945.12** `ad20-loan::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/loan-refused` tags: loan, debt, credit, everyday, obligation, money, vienna, urban
  Berta Weiss把Mitzi的收入证明翻来覆去看了两遍，说数目不够。Mitzi问能不能少借一点；Berta Weiss说“少借也要还得起”。走出门时，Mitzi把证明信重新叠好，发现上面有一道自己刚才捏出的折痕。
  _effects: flag +loan_refused; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; cash -1 (paperwork and a wasted morning)_

- **1946.01** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-list-was-already-signed` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  上一任在离任前签了一份四十人的任命名单，最后一行没有日期。Mitzi把Anton Hofer的名字添在第四十一行，注明“补录”；发薪日，会计问这四十一个人里为什么有六个已经去世。
  _effects: position 编外顾问 @taxi:person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:012566; flag +forty_first_name; knowledge “上一任留下的四十人名单里，有六个已经去世。”; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/worn-thin` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Alois Lechner今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Mitzi在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 16 -> 24; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 3 -> 9; health -0.03 (four nights on a hospital chair); cash -2 (medicine and hospital fees)_

  Mitzi刚提一句“那年分东西”，Maria Lechner就把勺子放下，说柜子是他先看中的，凭什么让。话越说越硬，汤还剩半碗，Maria Lechner起身拿了帽子，椅子在石地上刮出一声。Mitzi一个人坐到教堂的钟敲完，把两份饭钱都付了。

  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_

  _effects: reputation 0.04; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

## Josef

Born 1912.01, died 1917.03 (age 5).
Education: none 0; occupation: none; roles: infant, child.

### State snapshot

### 3 subject events

- **1916.09** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1916.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1917.03** `statistical_death/pneumonia` tags: death
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang发起高烧，咳嗽越来越深。邻居帮忙去请医生，医生到时只留下药粉和一张账单；几天后呼吸变得很浅。Anna Bauer在场。
  _effects: death (肺炎)_

  Rosa换把时用力过猛，弦轴崩了，琴弦抽在手背上。Emil Hofer没有责备，只让Rosa先学三个月空弦。Rosa每天对着镜子拉同一个音，拉到最后，连邻居都不再敲墙，只是经过门口时会放慢脚步。第二天Rosa照常来，手上缠着布。弦上还有血。
  _effects: cash -2 (借来的小提琴); health -0.020000000000000018 (借来的小提琴); skill_

  补考那天，Rosa在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Grete Stein跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Rosa去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; education apprenticeship_

  _effects: relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; skill; mood -0.05; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

  _effects: cash -12 (拉丁文的旧课本); flag +family_sold_ring_for_school; relation friend with person-grete-stein; education secondary_

  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:67; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  弥撒进行到一半，Rosa忽然唱不出声，只张嘴。Hermann Weiss用琴声盖过去，Katharina Weiss回头看了一眼。散场后Hermann Weiss把Rosa的独唱位置给了新来的男孩，说这不是惩罚，是嗓子到了年纪。Rosa把谱子折好，放进抽屉最底层。
  _effects: mood -0.1; flag +replaced_as_soloist; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  家里算了一笔账：学徒工钱扣掉车费和饭钱，剩不下多少。Rosa留在家里接缝纫店的零活，顺便照看弟弟妹妹。
  _effects: role; cash 2 (home piecework); health -0.010000000000000009 (cramped home work)_

  Rosa只把Elisabeth Bauer的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Rosa是不是要请人喝咖啡；Rosa说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。

  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +neglected_own_studies; relation rival with person-rosa-lechner_

  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-rosa-lechner_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +yf18_burned_pamphlet_
