  _effects: flag +returned_to_night_school; skill; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

  _effects: cash -6 (长椅上晃着两条够不着地的腿); condition; flag +parent_fined_for_child; relation rival with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “the fine will be deducted from the relief payment”_

  Lena把烟卷塞进Rosa嘴里，划了根火柴。第一口呛得Rosa眼泪直流，第二口装作没事。Lena笑得蹲在地上，说城里人都这么学。Rosa把烟夹在指间，直到烧到指节才扔掉。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (防火梯上的第一口烟); flag +tried_first_cigarette; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +left_school_for_family; role_

  _effects: reputation 0.03; mood 0.02999999999999997; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000140:child; flag +yf18_prater_story_

- **1930.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Rosa把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Rosa读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

  Rosa感冒了三天，鼻子像塞了棉花。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把十二只小瓶排在柜台上，让Rosa闭眼闻；前十一只都答错了，最后一只，Rosa说出“佛手柑和一点旧木头”。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把那只瓶子推到一边，说“记住这个味道，它比你的名字值钱”。

- **1930.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.join-second-row` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Rosa把两克朗月费和徽章钱一起放在啤酒渍斑斑的桌上，Alois Stein 2在名册上添了一行。钢琴缺的那个音在排练中途又卡住，Rosa站在第二排跟着哼，声音被两边的人盖住，胸腔里却震得发麻；散场时能把副歌完整唱下来，也记住了怎么在人群里找准自己的声部。
  _effects: cash -2 (合唱团月费和铜徽章钱); mood 0.07; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1930.04** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/report-to-official` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Rosa把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Rosa自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick

- **1931.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/the-logbook-mislaid` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  巡查簿在换班时不见了，第二天却出现在煤筐底下，最后几页被水泡得发皱。管事的说签字不能算数，扣了Rosa半个月的夜班钱；Rosa没有争，只在新的簿子第一页把日期写得很大。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 35 -> 19; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1931.01** `r13-civ.submit-manuscript::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/rejected-with-note` tags: culture, writing, manuscript, editor, publication, university, schooling, school
  Emil Lechner 2把稿子退回来，夹着一张写得潦草的便条：细节好，人物像纸。Rosa在电车上把便条读了两遍，回家后把稿子摊在桌上，从第一行开始改。改到天亮，只删掉一句。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +manuscript_rejected; skill; knowledge “the editor thought the characters were made of paper”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1931.06** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa正要把空床许给Katharina Weiss 2，Berta Weiss却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Rosa回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Katharina Weiss 2，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。
  _effects: mood -0.019999999999999962; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1931.07** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Rosa端着一只旧锅去敲Emil Weiss的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Emil Weiss让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Rosa连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.12** `vienna-history-schnitzler-theatre/speak-in-the-foyer` tags: history, culture, arthur_schnitzler, burgtheater, theatre
  Rosa 在门廊里拦住Arthur Schnitzler，说第二幕的医生讲得太像真的。Arthur Schnitzler 停了一下，回答说他当过医生，知道那种语气；然后礼貌地告辞，没有留下地址。Rosa 看着他走进雨里，才想起自己忘了问下一部戏什么时候上演。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-arthur-schnitzler; knowledge “Schnitzler 当过医生，他的剧作里医生和官员的说话方式来自医院与部委的经验，而不是单纯的舞台效果。”; cash -3 (theatre ticket); reputation 0.009999999999999998_

- **1931.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.hand-to-family` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工资袋上的油墨还没干透，Rosa的手指染了一点黑。母亲把硬币倒在厨房桌上，一枚枚排开，排到第六枚时停住。灶上的水壶响了，她拿起来又放下，没继续问。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1932.01** `r14-edu.sibling-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-failed` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, exam, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Elisabeth Lechner差三分。回家路上，Elisabeth Lechner一句话也没说，进了门就把自己关在屋里。Rosa把饭菜放在门口，敲了两下，没有回应。半夜，Rosa听见屋里有人在翻书，翻到天亮。第二天清早，Elisabeth Lechner出来洗脸，眼睛肿着，问Rosa能不能再教一年。补考费一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (弟弟妹妹的准考证); flag +sibling_failed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000525:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002650_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Rosa在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Rosa的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `oj18-perfume.recipe::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/recipe-stolen` tags: perfume, shop, recipe, theft, customer, work, odd_job, reform
  对街的店员Emil抄走了Rosa的配方：前调多放一滴橙花，后调少放半滴麝香，成本就能降一成。Rosa在市场上看见同样的瓶子，标签换了名字；店主没有去告，只在门口挂了一块小牌：本店不卖便宜的味道。
  _effects: cash -2 (lost sales to the copied scent); reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; flag +recipe_stolen; knowledge “对街的瓶子用了我的配方，前调多一滴橙花，后调少半滴麝香。”_

- **1932.01** `state_transition:apprenticeship-contract/expelled` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick, failed-apprentice
  Rosa从「学徒契约」转入另一段处境：the master ended the contract
  _effects: state_transition apprenticeship-contract -> failed-apprentice; flag +failed_apprenticeship; mood -0.07999999999999996; role_

- **1932.01** `r13-edu.workers-college::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/essay-rejected` tags: education, workers_college, adult_education, union, essay, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa的入学作文被退回，说写得太像申诉书，不像读书计划。Rosa改了三遍，把“我要控诉”改成“我想弄清”，才拿到面试机会。面试那天，Rosa把改过的稿子背得一字不差，手心却全是汗。面试官翻到最后一页，问了他一个别的问题。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1932.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.belt-patch` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Rosa拆下一条旧皮带的头，蒙在鞋底破口上，用钉子一点点敲进去。第三下时锥子扎透鞋面，扎进左手拇指根，血滴在鞋帮上。她用围裙擦了擦，还是把皮子钉完了，第二天走路时鞋底有块硬响。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (锥子扎伤拇指根); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1932.08** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/speak-with-rent-numbers` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Rosa没有喊口号，只问在场谁家的煤还够烧三天。十几只手举起来，连Alois Weiss也举了。书记说这些数字比演说有用，散会时几个人围上来问自己那条街怎么算。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1933.01** `r14-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/learned-to-read` tags: education, adult_learning, literacy, night_school, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Rosa下班后不回家，直接去夜校。教室由一间酒馆后屋改成，桌上还留着酒渍。Emil Hofer从字母表教起，Rosa把每个字母念三遍，喉咙发干。三个月后，Rosa第一次独立读完一张报纸上的短讯，把那张报纸叠好，放进工具箱最底层。学费一克朗，Rosa用加班钱付的。
  _effects: cash -1 (夜校的第二排长凳); flag +adult_literacy; skill_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003277_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Rosa的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Rosa看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005154_

  Rosa听说下奥地利的砖厂还招人，收拾了一只箱子，坐早班火车离开维也纳。厂里的工头在背后说，走了就别回来。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; unemployment (left the Vienna factory); cash -8 (train ticket and lodgings)_

  Rosa Lechner提着药箱走了三里地，鞋上全是泥。进门后她先摸了摸炉子，说水不够热。Rosa一趟趟往灶里添柴，听见Mitzi在里屋喊。孩子天亮前落地，Rosa Lechner在门槛上坐了很久才站起来。
  _effects: cash -2 (夜里敲接生婆的门); health -0.040000000000000036 (夜里敲接生婆的门); flag +rural_midwife_walked_to_birth_

- **1933.09** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Emil Weiss用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Rosa才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; flag +parish_registered_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Rosa在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Franz Lechner 2的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1934.01** `kernel-social.lending::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/lending.borrowed-instead` tags: task, task:social, social, lending, debt, neighbor, money, everyday
  Rosa没有借出钱，反向Emil Weiss借了四克朗，说下个月发薪就还。Emil Weiss犹豫了一会儿，还是从围裙里摸出硬币；Rosa把数目写在日历上，每天路过都看一眼。日历上那一格被铅笔圈了又圈。
  _effects: cash 4 (borrowed four kronen from the neighbour); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; role; flag +in_debt; knowledge “欠邻居四克朗，下个月发薪日还。”_

- **1934.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa没有立刻答话，先去敲Emil Weiss的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Emil Weiss提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Rosa把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1934.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Helene Lechner在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Helene Lechner敲开Rosa的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

  Rosa在约瑟夫城租下一间带钢琴的会客室，每小时一克朗，女房东把钥匙拴在一截蓝线上。Mitzi来时带着一束用报纸包好的紫罗兰，Ferdinand Berg坐在窗边打毛线，偶尔抬头看一眼钟。第三个星期日，Ferdinand Berg说自己要去楼下买咖啡，把门留了半掩。
  _effects: state_add forbidden-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:385; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; cash -1 (the parlour, the coffee and a bunch of violets); mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +rented_a_parlour; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; flag +secret_attachment_active_

  Rosa住进市立总医院的产房，六张床之间只隔一层布帘。同病房的女人教她怎么用布带裹住腰。孩子生下来时Hermann Kral 2还在走廊里，护士把襁褓递给他，他先看了看账单。出院那天，两人用借来的童车把孩子推回Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg，车轮一路吱呀响。

  Rosa在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Rosa把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。

  Hermann Kral 2把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Rosa留在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Rosa在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。

  Rosa把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Alois Moser 2没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Rosa回头看了一眼，Alois Moser 2正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。

  家里只肯借，不肯给。Alois Lechner拿出一张写明年限的借条，要Rosa毕业后第一年收入里还清，月息照银行算。他签了名，把借条和学费一起收进皮夹。
  _effects: cash -50 (借到的学费); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.06000000000000005_

  _effects: relation friend with person-helene-winter; food_

- **1937.05** `major-family.marriage-pressure/marriage-pressure.dowry-agreed` tags: family, marriage, dowry, household, major, lifecycle, partnership
  两家人把嫁妆写在纸上：四十克朗、一床鸭绒被、两只铜锅。Alois Weiss的父亲逐项划价，Rosa的母亲先凑出二十克朗，余下的答应秋收后补齐。清单由媒人保管，按了双方的手印。婚礼在教区教堂举行，Rosa搬进Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg时，箱子底层压着那张清单。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; household; role; role; cash -20 (first dowry payment and wedding costs); flag +dowry_partial; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  Rosa在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Alois Lechner记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Rosa带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: health -0.12 (beaten in police custody); condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1938.01** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-deposit` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household, injury
  窗子推开只有一线天光，墙纸边角翘起，Rosa用指甲一刮便掉下一层湿粉。看门人揣好定金，说这间是朝北的，冬天火炉要添得勤些。Ferdinand Berg站在楼梯口没进屋，只替Rosa看着箱子。
  _effects: cash -4 (当场付下定金和钥匙钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); residence; health -0.02999999999999997 (在新屋里待久了关节发凉)_

  Rosa的私事被洗衣房的女人讲给了整条街。有人当面笑，有人替Rosa惋惜；那天晚上，Rosa第一次认真数了数抽屉里还剩多少钱，想着要不要离开这个区。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 65 -> 95; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.grief 10 -> 20; reputation -0.07999999999999999; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  Rosa输了五票，要求把票重新数一遍。数到第二遍还是五票，Gustav Huber把铁盒盖上，说“再数就难看了”。Rosa说账本也得查，后厅一下子安静下来。那晚的合唱没有唱成。散会后，Gustav Huber把铁盒抱在怀里，锁进酒馆老板的柜子。铁盒被放回柜子，账本却留在了桌上。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; reputation -0.04999999999999999; flag +disputed_club_election; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

  Rosa和Maria Lechner把值班时辰写在门后的粉笔板上：Maria Lechner守前半夜，Rosa守后半夜，医生说静养二十一天。煤油灯芯拧小，药粉分成小包摆在床头，便盆换水时尽量不碰出响。二十一天熬下来，Rosa的眼皮肿了，走路发飘，但Alois Lechner每次起身总有人扶。
  _effects: health -0.04999999999999999 (连续夜里守夜睡眠不足); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001020:child; flag +night_care_duty_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  Rosa先盛了一碗，说“我先尝咸淡”。半个钟头后，Rosa开始呕吐，Alois Weiss跑去叫Anton Weiss 2。洗胃时，Rosa听见Alois Lechner在门外问“谁做的汤”，Alois Weiss说“是她自己”。Anton Weiss 2把铜勺放进证物袋，说“先查查锅”。
  _effects: cash -6 (汤锅边搁着一把铜勺（其中4克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +poisoned_by_own_soup; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; knowledge “the soup was poisoned before the subject tasted it”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1942.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/blocked-the-permit` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Rosa把一份扩建许可证压在抽屉最下面，理由是排水和消防通道不合规。申请人Hermann Kral 2在走廊里等到下班，最后只拿到一张写着“补件”的纸条。Rosa知道这份许可明年还会再来，但今年不会从自己的桌上出去。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000018; skill; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; flag +blocked_permit; knowledge “扩建申请的排水图与消防通道不符合现行规定。”_

- **1942.01** `state_expire:failed-apprentice` tags: failed-apprentice, craft, work, failure, stigma, state, state_tick, state:failed-apprentice
  Rosa的「学徒契约终止」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove failed-apprentice state:world-vienna-1912:306_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/kept-word` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Rosa把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Rosa这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 55 -> 61; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 30 -> 25; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 65 -> 69; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Rosa的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Rosa想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 4; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 50 -> 40; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:office-under-siege/pressure-year/new-allegation` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  第二天的报纸又登了一封匿名信，说Rosa把一份合同给了亲戚。信里没有证据，但足够让两个原本答应到场的人临时出差。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 40; state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 54; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1942.01** `task-vienna-family-care.ask-relative-money/loan-with-schedule` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Hermann Moser听Rosa把房租和药费的数目报完，从抽屉里数出二十克朗，推到桌上，同时拿出一张纸，要写还款日期。他说每月十五号来一趟，把账报一遍。Rosa签字时手有点抖，把纸角压得很平，说好先从月底还三克朗。
  _effects: cash 20 (舅舅借出的钱); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; flag +owes_uncle_

- **1942.10** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/list-rejected` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  名单在委员会卡住了。Hermann Kral 2的名字被划掉，理由是“没有相关资历”；Rosa没有争，只把名单收回公文包。晚上，Hermann Kral 2来问结果，Rosa说再等一年；Hermann Kral 2没有坐，站了一会儿就走了。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; flag +appointment_list_rejected; knowledge “委员会以“没有相关资历”为由划掉了联络员的名字。”_

- **1942.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa把通知念给Berta Weiss听，Berta Weiss摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Rosa去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.12** `ml18-inheritance.uncles-press::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/sell-the-subscription-list` tags: inheritance, family, business, debt, legal, money, vienna, urban
  公证人把一封厚信推到桌子中央：Rosa的叔父在第二区留下一间小印刷所、一台坏掉的压机和十四克朗的欠账。Rosa Bauer 2说那台压机早该当废铁，但订阅名单上还有三百个名字。Rosa把名单卖给对街的印厂，替叔父还了债，剩下的钱刚好够换一套冬衣。
  _effects: cash 12 (sale of the print shop subscription list); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:756; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; skill; flag +inherited_print_shop; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/small-relapse` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Rosa晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Rosa听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 61 -> 54; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 25 -> 33; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 40 -> 43; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `state_expire:office-under-siege` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  Rosa的「职位受围」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:694; flag -office_under_siege_

- **1943.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.reach-summit` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Rosa和Mitzi按原计划沿葡萄园间的土路往上走，鞋子沾满黄泥，到山顶时风把汗湿的衬衫吹得冰凉。下山在第一个酒棚坐下，两人合点了一壶新酒和一块面包，一共一克朗二十赫勒；Rosa喝到第二杯时脸颊发烫，把剩下的半块面包包进手帕带回家，第二天腿酸，却睡得很沉。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (卡伦山酒棚的新酒和面包); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (星期天登山远足); relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1943.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Alois Weiss一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Alois Weiss的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1943.05** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Rosa对Franz Winter 2说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Franz Winter 2把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Rosa的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108_

- **1943.08** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Hedwig把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Hedwig在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1943.12** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Rosa点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Franz Winter量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

- **1944.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa看Alois Weiss盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Alois Weiss愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Rosa门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Rosa打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; food_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011860_

- **1944.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Otto Moser这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

  Rosa把Helene 2的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Helene 2今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Rosa听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。

- **1944.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Rosa把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Rosa来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 4; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.01** `p10-migration-tasks.find-lodging/lodging-courtyard-room` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, lodging, arrival
  他挑了朝院子的那间单间，付了一个月押金和半个月房租，房东点清钱才给钥匙。看门人把备用钥匙挂进门房的木板上，嘱咐他晚上十点以后别在楼梯上跺脚。屋里有张床、一只铁炉和半截蜡烛。
  _effects: cash -9 (单间一个月押金与半个月房租（其中7.5克朗记入债务）); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 45 -> 48; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Rosa在圣文生会门口登记，领到一张面包券和一小袋土豆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1944.06** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Alois Weiss在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1944.09** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/shelter` tags: war, homefront, shortage
  警报响时Rosa躲进地下室，和十几户人挤在长凳上。头顶传来闷响，灰尘从梁上落下。警报解除后，街上多了一堆碎瓦。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1944.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.church-bell` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Rosa没有再修钟，把上工的时间交给楼下教堂的钟声。风从多瑙河那边刮过来时，钟声就散，窗户一关更听不见。有一回她误了钟点，到车间时工头已经在门口站着，扣了半个克朗。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (迟到被扣工钱); mood -0.050000000000000044_

  Rosa想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1945.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Gustav Berg 4借给Rosa一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Rosa睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1945.08** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002097:child; reputation -0.02999999999999997; mood -0.06000000000000005_

- **1946.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/the-vote-ties` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  第二轮投票是十七比十七。按章程，主席可以投第二票；Rosa把自己的名字念了两遍，会议室里没有人笑。散会后，Alois Lechner在门口说下一次会带更多的人来，Rosa说椅子够用。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation rival with person-alois-lechner; organization_funds; flag +tie_broken_by_chair; knowledge “章程允许主席在平票时投第二票，但没有人记得上一次使用是什么时候。”_

- **1946.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Rosa把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Gustav Hofer站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Rosa手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000866:child; reputation 0.030000000000000027_

  Rosa走到半路风忽然大起来，围巾抽在脸上生疼，她掉头往回走。路过面包店时橱窗里还剩几个昨天的圆面包，四赫勒一个，她买了两个，揣在怀里带回去当晚饭。
  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个（其中0.08克朗记入债务）)_

  天色还灰着，Rosa手里攥着几张折皱的证明。Ferdinand Kral让他念车皮上的号码，听见念对了，就朝装卸班一挥手，说先按日结钱，一天两克朗四十分。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ morgue:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3-evt:world-vienna-1912:009092; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1947.02** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.school-fork/apprentice-at-printer` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Rosa在印刷厂的招工窗口替孩子报了学徒，第一年每周带回两克朗工钱。Grete Stein在成绩单背面写的推荐话被折进信封，和学徒契约一起锁在抽屉里。孩子第一天回来，袖口沾着油墨，说师傅让先搬纸；Rosa数着那两克朗，没再提升学的事。
  _effects: cash 2 (孩子学徒第一周带回工钱); relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +child_apprentice_printer_

  玻璃后面的招工纸被门房用尺子压平，Rosa的名字写在横线本上，墨迹歪着。旁边的Anna Weiss凑过来看了一眼，说这厂子去年也招过两回，录用的多半是厂里老人的亲戚。
  _effects: flag +factory_callback; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.10** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Hermann Kral 2没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Rosa听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱)_

### 198 subject events

- **1913.07** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Josef Bauer提热水壶往木盆里兑水时壶底脱了焊，滚水溅在左手背上。她咬着牙把壶搁下，Emil被响声惊得大哭。手背起了水泡，接下来三天她只能用一只手洗尿布。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (左手被开水烫伤); mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1915.12** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Emil一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Josef Bauer听见哭声跑出来，把Emil抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Emil哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1917.08** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  煤车刚过去，路心还留着几块碎煤。Emil的铁环撞上去，弹到水沟边。Anna Winter先一步把铁环踩住，说要拿一颗石弹子来换。Emil蹲下去掏口袋，膝盖在石板棱上蹭掉一块皮，换回铁环时裤腿已经渗出血点；Emil没再争，只记住Anna Winter踩铁环的那只脚。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; mood -0.04; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Hermann Kral家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Emil递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Hermann Kral端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Emil把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1918.11** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Josef Bauer，楼里的Anna Moser正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Josef Bauer把裹着布条的Emil往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Emil前面的大婶一直往后靠，Emil就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Emil的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Emil拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Alois Lechner弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Emil接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1920.08** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Emil每天放学都在柜台前站一会儿，看玻璃板底下压着的烟画，顺手把歪了的报纸摞齐。Lena擦完柜台，从抽屉最里面摸出一张揉皱的风景卡，边角还沾着烟丝，塞过来时说了句“别挡着门”。Emil把卡夹进课本，第二天带了一张重复的来给Lena看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Anna Lechner朝Emil招手。Emil挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Emil把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1921.12** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Maria Weiss把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Emil伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Maria Weiss从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Emil手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Emil一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1922.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Emil的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1922.06** `statistical_recovery`
  Emil熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Emil的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Emil在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.029999999999999992; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1923.05** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Hermann Weiss从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Emil答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Emil回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Hermann Weiss看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1923.09** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店门口的石阶上摆着一排烟画。Emil把三张皱巴巴的卡摊开，Anton Winter挑了挑，从口袋里摸出一张皇帝像。交换完，Anton Winter转身进了杂货铺，把皇帝像往柜台上一拍，换了一块糖。Emil站在门外，手里还留着卡片的油墨味。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000140:child_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.02; flag +wish:love:pursuing_

- **1924.04** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-sign-up-hike` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Emil把十赫勒放在桌上的铁皮盒里，翻到歌本最后一页，用铅笔写下自己的名字。Mitzi Lechner在后面戳Emil的背，说乡下的草地能躺着听云。
  _effects: cash -10 (青年会远足车费); relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.03_

- **1924.12** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Emil在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Emil用牙咬断，重新来。Josef Bauer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Emil把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  客户刚把床单接过去，Alois Lechner 2就从楼梯口上来，盯着Emil看了两秒，说“你不是这家的”。Alois Lechner 2当场把工钱收回，说明天要去找Klara的雇主。Emil站在门口，手里还攥着空筐绳，回家一路上没吃东西，Klara问起来，只摇了摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西)_

- **1925.05** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Elisabeth Huber把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Emil看期限、工钱和出师考试。Emil签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:63; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.07** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/keep-it-in-the-satchel` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Emil把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Emil每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:62; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  Grete Stein在成绩单上写“建议升入文法中学”，又用红笔圈了学费。Josef Bauer把戒指摘下来，放在当铺柜台上，换回十二克朗。Emil背着新书包去考试，书包里只有一本借来的拉丁语课本。
  _effects: cash -12 (拉丁文的旧课本); flag +family_sold_ring_for_school; relation friend with person-grete-stein; education secondary_

- **1925.10** `r2-faith.parish-school-fee::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/expelled-for-arrears` tags: faith, school, charity, education, fees, reform, medical, schooling
  欠了兩個月学费，Hermann Weiss把Emil的座位调到最后一排，说书本先留下。Maria Berg 2在门口拦住Emil，说学校不是救济院。Emil回家没有说，第二天照常出门，在火车站坐到放学时间。Josef Bauer后来在书包里发现了一张空白的退学单。
  _effects: mood -0.14; condition; flag +expelled_from_parish_school_for_arrears; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; knowledge “two months of unpaid fees can cost a child his seat”_

  家里算了一笔账：学徒工钱扣掉车费和饭钱，剩不下多少。Emil留在家里接缝纫店的零活，顺便照看弟弟妹妹。
  _effects: role; cash 2 (home piecework); health -0.010000000000000009 (cramped home work)_

- **1926.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Emil翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Klara在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Emil翻回来时，Rosa把一支烟塞进Emil嘴里，手抖得点不着火。

  Emil把写给Klara的信折成四折，夹在拉丁文课本第三百页。第二天，书还回来时，页边多了一行铅笔字：“星期四，图书馆关门前。”两个人在书架之间站得很近，谁都没有先提那封信。

- **1929.06** `yf18-performance.choir-solo::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/finish-in-falsetto` tags: performance, choir, humiliation, school, voice, reform, medical, schooling
  教堂音乐会的独唱轮到Emil，第一句拉丁文还稳，唱到“荣耀”那个高音时，嗓子忽然劈了一下。Emil看见Josef Bauer在第三排低下头，Grete Stein在风琴旁边打手势让继续；Emil用假声把剩下的两小节唱完，声音细得像一根线。
  _effects: reputation -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +yf18_finished_solo; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1929.07** `r2-leisure.maid-follower::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/let-him-into-the-cellar` tags: domestic_service, courtship, servant, followers, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  地窖里堆着煤和酸菜桶，两人站在楼梯下面，Anna Huber 2把一枚纽扣塞进Emil手里，说是军装上的，掉了还能再缝。Mitzi Moser 2的钥匙在楼上响，Emil把纽扣含在嘴里，出去时脸很红。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +let_follower_into_cellar; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; knowledge “the housekeeper checks the cellar door after dark”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1929.08** `yf18-firstlook.atlas-margin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/write-back-in-pencil` tags: first_love, school, atlas, note, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  地理课上，Emil在地图册的空白处看见一行铅笔字：“多瑙河从维也纳流走，不从这儿。”Emil认出是Mitzi的笔迹，趁Emil Hofer转身，在下面添了一句：“那你往哪儿流？”下课铃响时，Emil把地图册塞进Mitzi的课桌。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:183; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_atlas_note_

- **1930.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.servants-room` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Rosa Hofer的东家说得很清楚：结婚就得搬出佣人房，工钱也要减。那间房在厨房后面，冬天靠炉灶取暖。Rosa Hofer算了一夜，第二天把Emil送的围巾叠好送回来。两人在面包房门口站了一会儿，各自去上工；围巾上还留着烤炉的暖气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; role; flag +marriage_blocked_by_lodging; cash -1 (bread from the bakery); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Rosa Hofer的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Emil最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Emil把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Emil说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 12 -> 18; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 55 -> 52; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; cash -0.06 (买了两块小甜饼); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  Emil把糖盒原样收进柜子，糖纸没动，第二天一早去请介绍人再去问一次原因。介绍人傍晚回来，站在走廊里压低声音说，对方家里嫌这边没有自己的灶。Emil把铁皮糖盒放进碗柜最上层，盖子上那束花磕掉了一块漆，屋里静得只剩水壶在响。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

  _effects: cash -1 (在厨房桌上写自己的名字); flag +enrolled_parent_in_night_school; role; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “the adult night school takes workers after the evening shift”; skill; condition_

- **1931.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Grete Weiss说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Grete Weiss陪着Emil去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1931.09** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Emil把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Helene Winter替Emil扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Emil在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Emil的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Maria Weiss没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Emil回头看了一眼，Maria Weiss正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1932.01** `major-task.trade-qualification/journeyman-passed` tags: task, task:work, work, trade, exam, apprenticeship, employment
  考场在印刷厂楼上的装配间。Josef Huber把一块松动的版锁推到Emil面前，要求不弄坏铅字重新固定；又让Emil读出自己排的一页价目表。Emil的手一直抖，最后还是把版锁压平了。傍晚，Josef Huber在证书上写下“Geselle”，工钱按熟练工重新算。

  Anna Huber 2把那张照片卖给了《晨报》，配文写“送别者的疲惫”。Emil收到两克朗模特费和一份报纸；照片里的Emil歪着头，嘴半张，确实像哭过。邻居开始叫Emil“那个在葬礼上睡着的人”，语气不算尊敬，也不完全是嘲笑。
  _effects: cash 2 (报纸给的照片使用费); reputation 0.02; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:286; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; flag +funeral_photo_in_the_paper; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.02; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1933.01** `bc18-fame.statue-unveiling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/second-commission` tags: fame, statue, mistaken_identity, public, ceremony, absurd, humiliation, reform

  _effects: cash 6 (三次模特酬劳); state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 22 -> 28; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 15 -> 27; reputation 0.03; role; flag +model_for_unknown_citizen; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Emil仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Emil学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 28 -> 35; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 0 -> 1; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000002; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `task-vienna-family-care.courtship-invitation/polite-refusal-church` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Anton Winter把手里的 Semmel 换到另一只手，说家里不许她单独跟人出门，连星期天下午也不行。她声音不大，但话说得很清楚，随后补一句：教堂下个月有集体郊游，母亲也去，Emil若愿意可以一道走。Emil谢过她，转身时纸袋被捏出一道折痕。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000140:child_

- **1933.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.beer-with-senior` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Emil把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; skill_

- **1933.08** `bc18-inherit.parrot-condition::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/parrot-outlives` tags: inheritance, family, condition, absurd, death, legal, quiet, reform
  姑妈的遗嘱只有一条：谁替她养那只灰鹦鹉满两年，谁就拿十二克朗。Emil搬进阁楼，每天煮谷子、换水、用旧报纸垫笼底。鹦鹉学会了学门铃响，也学会了在Emil咳嗽时叫“吃药”。两年后，公证人Josef Bauer来验鸟，鹦鹉在他帽子上拉了一泡；Emil拿到钱，先买了一只新笼子。
  _effects: cash 12 (养满两年鹦鹉得到的遗产); skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; condition; flag +kept_the_parrot; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  Emil把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Berta Hofer没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Emil回头看了一眼，Berta Hofer正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。

  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

  _effects: cash -2 (the return ticket and a wasted day); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; flag +mb17_turned_back_at_border; knowledge “边境窗口只承认带教区印章的出生证明。”_

  _effects: cash 2 (先走的那个人寄回了地址); mood -0.050000000000000155; flag +chain_job_cancelled; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; knowledge “the job order was withdrawn after a shutdown”_

  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 40 -> 50; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 20 -> 30; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 24 -> 48; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1938.01** `agency:publish` tags: agency, agency:publish
  Emil写完了稿子，却没有找到愿意印的人；手稿被折起来放回抽屉。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1938.05** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household, injury
  Emil把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Emil的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart); health -0.020000000000000018 (moving in winter)_

- **1938.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Rosa Huber在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Rosa Huber敲开Emil的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.12** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Ferdinand Berg在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1939.01** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-list-was-already-signed` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  上一任在离任前签了一份四十人的任命名单，最后一行没有日期。Emil把Alois Lechner的名字添在第四十一行，注明“补录”；发薪日，会计问这四十一个人里为什么有六个已经去世。
  _effects: position 编外顾问 @building:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006833; organization_funds; flag +forty_first_name; knowledge “上一任留下的四十人名单里，有六个已经去世。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Emil和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Emil在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 79 -> 83; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 31 -> 28; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Emil看了看自家两张床和已经见底的粮袋，说实在添不了一口人。Otto Weiss没有劝，只把Grete Bauer的手拉紧，转身下楼。Emil站在门口，听见那双小鞋在楼梯上一下一下地响，响到二楼才停。
  _effects: mood -0.02000000000000024; flag +refused_to_foster; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1939.02** `r3-mig.smuggler-guide::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/guide-tried-to-sell-us` tags: migration, border, smuggling, river, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  船到河心，Katharina Moser把桨横过来，说巡逻队今晚加了价，每人再加五克朗。Emil说没有钱，Katharina Moser就把船往哨所那边撑。Alois Stein 2在岸上喊话，Katharina Moser举手答应，Emil把口袋里的三克朗全扔进水里，趁他低头去捞时跳上了浅滩。
  _effects: cash -8 (船夫的价码); health -0.06000000000000005 (船夫的价码); condition; flag +guide_tried_to_turn_me_in; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; knowledge “the boatman sells passengers to the patrol when the price is”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1939.08** `r13-turn.refugee-arrival::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::war-refugee-columns/turned-away` tags: migration, refugee, papers, housing, displacement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Alois Lechner说今天的号发完了，让Emil明天再来。Emil问能不能在走廊里等到天亮，Alois Lechner说这里不许过夜。Emil把湿行李拖到街边，坐在门口的石阶上，看见里面的人一队队领到热汤。Alois Lechner关窗时，把百叶帘拉了下来。
  _effects: cash -3 (登记处门外的湿行李); food; flag +refused_refugee_registration; relation rival with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the registration office stops issuing numbers before noon”_

- **1939.09** `r2-leisure.perfume-counterfeit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/bought-it-anyway` tags: leisure, fashion, perfume, fraud, class, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil还是买了，因为瓶子的形状和橱窗里那瓶一模一样。回家喷在手腕上，前调是橙花，过一会儿变成肥皂水，再过一会儿什么都没有了。Anton闻了闻，说像刚洗过手。Emil把瓶子放在梳妆台上，没有扔。
  _effects: cash -8 (香水瓶底的沉淀); mood -0.06000000000000005; condition; flag +bought_counterfeit_perfume; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the counterfeit perfume smells of orange flower and soap”_

- **1940.01** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/reappointed` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  计票拖到凌晨，Emil以两票之差留任。Alois Stein 2把已经写好的交接清单折起来，笑着说下次再来。Emil回到办公室，发现桌上的文件被人重新排过；最上面那份，是新一年的预算草案。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; mood 0.06000000000000005; organization_funds; state_add office-patronage state:world-vienna-1912:642; flag +reappointed; knowledge “桌上的文件被人重新排过，最上面是新一年的预算草案。”; flag +office_patronage_network_

- **1940.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Lena拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失)_

  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Emil最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Emil把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Emil说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 28 -> 34; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 44 -> 41; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi Berg 2站在走廊尽头，脚边一只用绳子捆住的纸箱，问能不能借宿两晚。Emil把厨房的折叠床打开，又烧了一壶水。夜里，Mitzi Berg 2在梦里说了一种他听不懂的话，Emil把门闩又检查了一遍。
  _effects: cash -2 (走廊里的陌生口音); food; flag +sheltered_refugee_two_nights; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1940.05** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.beer-with-senior` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Emil把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1941.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Lena抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Emil把床单对折，腾出一半，Lena用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009822_

- **1941.01** `oj18-lift.night::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sealed-floor` tags: lift, building, night, secrecy, office, work, suspense, vienna
  Elisabeth Bauer告诉Emil，九层从一九三四年起就封了，里面堆着被撤掉的人留下的档案。Emil有一天趁没人，用万能钥匙打开防火门，看见一地纸箱和一只没喝完的茶杯；回来时，电梯停在了九层，门怎么也关不上。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 20; knowledge “九层从一九三四年起封了，里面堆着被撤掉的人留下的纸箱和一只茶杯。”; mood -0.030000000000000027; condition; relation colleague with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +sealed_floor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

  Emil把梦告诉房东。房东笑了一声，说老房子下水慢，做梦都带水汽。第二天，房东带人来查，真在墙里找到一条裂缝，顺手把地窖的租金减了一克朗。Emil把省下的钱买了一盏新灯，放在楼梯口。
  _effects: cash 1 (房东减掉的一克朗地窖租金); reputation 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; knowledge “房东因为我的梦查了墙，减了地窖租金。”; flag +df18_landlord_fixed_crack; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter office-patronage.exposure 15 -> 27; state_parameter office-patronage.loyalty 55 -> 49; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +patronage_rumor_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Emil的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Emil想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。

  手续办到一半，孩子的亲戚赶来，说自家人还在，轮不到外人领走。Elisabeth Bauer把已经填好的表格扣在桌上；Elisabeth Weiss躲到Emil身后，手指抓着外套下摆。那人在走廊里骂了很久，最后被门房请了出去。
  _effects: cash -4 (登记簿上的新姓氏); condition; flag +adoption_contested_by_kin; relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007390:child; knowledge “the child’s aunt claims guardianship”_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +relative_listed_missing; role; knowledge “a missing declaration delays the pension”_

  Emil原定在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang开会，但来的人不够，或者有人临时退缩；最后只留下几张没发完的纸条。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.009999999999999995_

- **1942.02** `r2-law.citizenship-papers::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/tutor-hired` tags: law, citizenship, papers, exam, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil请Grete Stein每周补三次课，学费按小时算。Grete Stein把公民课本拆成问题卡，让Emil在厨房里背；Berta Hofer来串门时也被拉着听写。补考那天，Alois Stein 2问的还是国歌和第二句，Emil这次答得很快，快得自己都不太相信。
  _effects: cash -12 (入籍宣誓前的口试); flag +passed_citizenship_test_after_tutoring; role; relation friend with person-grete-stein; knowledge “the examiner reuses the same questions on the retest”_

- **1942.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil请楼下的Alois Lechner白天来照看Josef Bauer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Alois Lechner上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Emil下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Josef Bauer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1942.11** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-shared-crates` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  箱子棱角硌着肋骨的滋味到傍晚还没消。工头把工钱数给Emil时少了一半，Berta Weiss凑过来把扁担一横，说名单上少写一个，钱可不能少算一个。
  _effects: cash 2 (争执后补到的搬运工钱); relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1943.01** `ml18-family.school-gate-car::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/keep-the-child-and-face-the-talk` tags: family, money, child, school, reputation, dark, vienna, urban
  Emil去参加了家长会，把中奖的票据、邮局收据和那张登了地址的报纸一起放在桌上，说钱是运气，不是偷的。没有人鼓掌，但也没有人再提转学。Emil Bauer在门外等，书包带子绕在手指上；回家路上，Emil第一次听见孩子主动说起学校的事。
  _effects: cash -5 (the parents' meeting and a small donation to the school); state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 63; reputation -0.009999999999999995; flag +child_stayed_in_school; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Emil和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Emil在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 100 -> 100; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 23 -> 20; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.08** `r13-rel.introduction-friend::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/two-silent-ends` tags: relationship, introduction, friend, acquaintance, social, vienna, urban, crisis
  Berta Lechner介绍完就去厨房帮忙，桌上只剩Emil和Katharina Moser。Katharina Moser盯着墙上的圣像，Emil数着桌布上的补丁。十分钟后Berta Lechner回来，看见两人各坐一头，笑得有点勉强。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1943.08** `af17-wish.family-inheritance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sold-the-shop` tags: wish, love, family, inheritance, shop, debt, irony, vienna
  房产经纪人只用了两天就找到买主，要把烟铺改成一家卖进口香皂的店面。Emil在契约上签了字，拿到十二克朗，比继承来的赊账多出不少。Helene Winter和她的姐姐在月底搬去城郊的侄女家，临走前把猫留在空柜台下面；Emil没有回去看那只猫，只在账本最后一页写下“已结清”。
  _effects: cash 12 (卖掉烟铺的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; reputation -0.05; condition; flag +sold_the_tobacco_shop; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Josef Winter拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000581:child_

- **1944.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef Winter嫌Emil的床单滴水滴到Josef Winter家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Emil下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Emil把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Josef Winter那侧。

  Emil把一份扩建许可证压在抽屉最下面，理由是排水和消防通道不合规。申请人Alois Weiss在走廊里等到下班，最后只拿到一张写着“补件”的纸条。Emil知道这份许可明年还会再来，但今年不会从自己的桌上出去。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004; skill; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; flag +blocked_permit; knowledge “扩建申请的排水图与消防通道不符合现行规定。”_

  Emil没有剪，让那卷新闻片继续走。观众先以为这是加映，直到有人站起来喊“这不是我们买的票”。警察在散场前堵住侧门；环城大道写字楼管理处说是放映员接错了本，Emil被记了名字。

  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +old_employer_cordial; cash 1 (a small gift from the old employer)_

  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在衙门走廊站了大半天); relation colleague with person-elisabeth-bauer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

  Hermann Weiss把Lena和Anton一起叫到讲台前，问墨水瓶底下的公式是谁写的。Lena说不知道；Anton低着头，袖口还沾着同样的蓝墨水。Hermann Weiss让两人各写一份检讨，Lena写到一半，听见Anton在旁边小声说“对不起”，笔尖把纸戳破了。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_exam_suspicion; mood -0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/hand-caught` tags: adolescence, labour, union, injury
  Lena在压纸时右手被滚轴卷进去，工头拉下电闸，血滴在铅字盘上。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让人把Lena送到医院，医药费先由厂里垫付。
  _effects: health -0.17999999999999994 (hand caught in press); condition; cash -4 (hospital transport)_

- **1928.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/friend-cuts-in` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Anton在第二支曲子前把Hedwig请走，说Lena答应过要替她看管外套。Lena抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Lena把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 50; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 33 -> 25; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 20 -> 25; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.04000000000000001_

  Lena只把Hedwig的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Lena是不是要请人喝咖啡；Lena说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 28 -> 40; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 19; reputation -0.03; mood -0.03_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/delivery-paid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  床单叠成四方，压得筐绳勒进肩膀。Lena照着地址找到利奥波德城的两户人家，一户在院子深处，一户要上四层楼。按件算下来是一克朗六十赫勒，Lena把铜币一枚枚码在Hermann Hofer枕边。Berta Hofer在门口看见，只说“下回别敲错门”；家里省下的那份晚饭，让Hermann Hofer多歇了一天。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (替妹妹送床单的计件工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (背着湿床单走了几个街区); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000152:child; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1928.09** `yf18-performance.choir-solo::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/finish-in-falsetto` tags: performance, choir, humiliation, school, voice, rural, agriculture, small_town
  教堂音乐会的独唱轮到Lena，第一句拉丁文还稳，唱到“荣耀”那个高音时，嗓子忽然劈了一下。Lena看见Josefine Hofer在第三排低下头，Emil Hofer在风琴旁边打手势让继续；Lena用假声把剩下的两小节唱完，声音细得像一根线。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; flag +yf18_finished_solo; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1928.11** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Lena把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Lena用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 48 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 18 -> 20_

  德语作文题是《我的家乡》。Lena写了半页多瑙河，剩下半页写一个总在电车上看书的人。Emil Hofer没有打分，只在页边写“感情太多，论据太少”，把作文折好放进自己的公文包；放学后，Lena看见Hedwig在门口等，忽然不敢走过去。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 56; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 25 -> 28; skill; reputation -0.010000000000000002; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Lena只把Hedwig的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Lena是不是要请人喝咖啡；Lena说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 40 -> 52; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 19 -> 13; reputation -0.03; mood -0.03_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.learn-the-task/polished-type-redo` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  铅字上积了墨垢，Lena趁Rosa Hofer去喝水，用布蘸着清洗剂把字面挨个擦亮，擦完还对着光看了看。第一张样张出来，字面亮得挂不住墨，价目糊成一片。Rosa Hofer把纸摔在案上，让Lena把整盘字倒回字匣；重排到天黑，Lena的肚子一直叫。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (饿着肚子加班重排); mood -0.06; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1929.02** `ld18-letter.schoolbook::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-letter-changes-hands` tags: love, letter, school, secret, queer, courtship, secret_intimacy, rural
  Lena把写给Hedwig的信折成四折，夹在拉丁文课本第三百页。第二天，书还回来时，页边多了一行铅笔字：“星期四，图书馆关门前。”两个人在书架之间站得很近，谁都没有先提那封信。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:184; mood 0.04999999999999999; flag +letter_in_the_schoolbook; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1929.09** `ld18-dance.private-club::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/danced-for-tickets` tags: adolescence, dance, queer, secret, public, desire, public_desire, rural
  Friedrich Hofer让她们在星期六的“特别节目”里跳一支舞，票价比平时贵一倍。台下的人看得很安静，只有一个老太太在角落里哭；Lena后来才知道，老太太年轻时也和一个女人跳过舞。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:185; cash 1 (a share of the special programme tickets); relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; mood -0.01999999999999999; flag +danced_for_tickets; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1929.09** `vienna-youth-threshold.first-wage/half-for-shoes` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  鞋铺橱窗里一双结实的工作鞋标价四克朗二十赫勒，Lena的鞋底已经能吸进雪水。回家后Lena把信封拆开，一半放在桌上，一半揣进内袋，说下个月不向家里要鞋钱。Friedrich Hofer没说话，只把桌上的钱一张张抹平。
  _effects: cash 4 (工资的一半交给家里); cash -4.2 (买一双不漏水的工作鞋); health 0.050000000000000044 (脚不再整日泡在湿冷里); relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1929.12** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Lena在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Lena用牙咬断，重新来。Friedrich Hofer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Lena把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1930.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.bad-blood` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Emil的母亲把Lena带来的点心原封退回，说家里不娶有肺病亲戚的人。点心是早上排队买的，油纸还热着，Lena把它抱在胸前。Emil站在门后没有出来，只让妹妹传话。Lena把戒指留在门房，走回Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg时天还没黑，楼下的狗一直叫。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; role; flag +marriage_refused; cash -2 (pastries and the returned ring)_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Lena把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Emil没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Lena回头看了一眼，Emil正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Lena最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Lena把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Lena说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 21; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 55 -> 52; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  有人把一张账单寄到Lena家，背面写着“封口费”，数目正好是她一个月的工钱。Lena把钱数了两遍，最后还是装进信封；第二天，街上又有了新版本，比上一个更贵，也更难听。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 30 -> 42; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 10 -> 2; cash -2 (the first payment to a blackmailer); mood -0.03999999999999998_

  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:65; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1930.01** `kernel-social.reconciliation::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/reconciliation.debt-settled` tags: task, task:social, social, reconciliation, family, apology, conflict, rural
  Friedrich Hofer把两人叫到厨房，把借条和账本摊在桌上，说今天要么还钱，要么写个期限。Anton先还四克朗，余下的按月在面包房门口交；Lena把借条折好，放进饼干盒。两人没有握手，但一起把账本翻到了新的一页。
  _effects: cash 4 (first instalment of the family debt); relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; role; flag +family_debt_settled; knowledge “余款每月在面包房门口交，借条放在饼干盒里。”_

- **1930.02** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/accepts-the-file` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, rural
  户籍官Josef Bauer为了结掉一桩陈年失踪案，在档案上给Lena盖了“推定死亡”。三天后，Lena收到一张慰问卡和一本作废的配给簿；同一天，追了半年的债主忽然不再上门。旧货商听说后，花两克朗买走一份抄本，说要给一个躲债的朋友看看格式。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖给旧货商的死亡证明抄本); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:215; reputation -0.010000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; flag +declared_dead_while_alive; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1930.06** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Lena把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Anton站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Lena手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.03_

- **1931.01** `major-family.marriage-pressure/marriage-pressure.shop-counter-bride` tags: family, marriage, dowry, household, major, lifecycle, partnership
  对方不要嫁妆，只要Lena婚后到他们家的杂货铺站柜台。婚礼很简单，Lena第二天就系上围裙，工钱直接交给婆婆，婆婆每天把铜板数两遍。晚上Otto Winter给她留了一块面包，什么也没说；柜台下的木凳太高，她踮着脚才能够到秤。
  _effects: relation partner with person-otto-winter; household; role; role; role; cash -8 (apron, shoes and wedding clothes)_

- **1931.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-wedding-invitation` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  请柬上写着Lena的名字，旁边用铅笔注了一句“一个人来也可以”。婚礼上，新娘把手捧花直接递给她，笑着说“下一个该你了”；同桌的人都在等Lena怎么回答。她把花放在膝上，说这花很香。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 40 -> 48; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 15 -> 20; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004645_

  学务督察按着新户籍找上门，说有一名适龄儿童从未入学。Lena躲在阁楼里，Emil Weiss在楼下说孩子出疹子，不能见风；督察留下了一张入学通知和一瓶鱼肝油。晚上，Lena从阁楼下来，看见通知上写着“七岁”，忽然觉得自己这一生确实该重新开始。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 20 -> 35; reputation -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; condition; flag +school_notice_for_newborn; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Lena和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Lena在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 45 -> 49; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 21 -> 18; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Lena是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Lena在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 42 -> 38; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 48 -> 50; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  一个税务员把Lena的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Lena收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Lena先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 25 -> 32; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 35 -> 45; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  排练到一半，有人提出只唱德语，另一边的成员把歌本合上。Hermann Weiss敲了敲谱架，说教堂不是议会。Maria Lechner 2站起来走了，Lena坐在原位，听见门在身后关上。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +choir_split_over_language; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; knowledge “half the choir wants German only and the other half refuses”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

  Katharina Stein说只需要Lena在明天下午去一趟车站，把一个箱子交给穿灰外套的人。Lena问箱子里是什么；Katharina Stein说“你不需要知道”。Lena还是去了，因为当年Katharina Stein替Lena垫过一笔医药费。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:252; cash -1 (the trip to the station); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; flag +favor_done; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “箱子里是什么，邻居没有说。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1931.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Helene Lechner借给Lena一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Lena睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Lena在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Lena的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/a-knock-at-the-door` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, reform
  头发刚擦到一半，Otto Winter来敲门收煤气钱。Lena把Grete Stein推进壁橱，自己披上外套去开门；床上的湿毛巾还没来得及收。Otto Winter看了一眼，说下次记得把水倒了，楼梯会滑。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 18 -> 26; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 52 -> 57; relation friend with person-grete-stein; mood -0.019999999999999962_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Lena最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Lena把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Lena说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 26 -> 32; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 57 -> 54; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Lena，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Lena没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 38 -> 46; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 20 -> 24; reputation -0.030000000000000002; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Lena学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Lena的材料齐全。年底，Lena从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 32 -> 36; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 45 -> 40; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:ad20-private-bargain/the-favor-ages/called-in` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  对方在一个不合时宜的时刻提起旧事，语气很客气，意思却不客气。Lena听见自己答应得太快，挂断以后才发现手心有一层薄汗。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 50 -> 58; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 55 -> 59; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 55 -> 51; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  半夜，Katharina Weiss敲开楼门，说有人看见生人进了这栋楼。Lena说阁楼堆着煤，没人上去。Katharina Weiss还是上去看了一圈，靴子踩得楼板吱响。人走后，Lena发现Mitzi把煤箱盖从里面顶住了。
  _effects: health -0.06000000000000005 (阁楼上的脚步声); reputation -0.05; condition; flag +police_searched_attic; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the police searched the attic but did not move the coal box”_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Lena在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Lena把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003945_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-depression/laid-off` tags: adulthood, depression, work, unemployment
  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Lena的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Lena把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Franz Winter没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Lena回头看了一眼，Franz Winter正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; cash -1 (the raised bar bill after the bribe); state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 32 -> 37; flag +club_owner_paid_the_police_

  Josef Bauer把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Lena去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Katharina Weiss 2给Lena一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Lena把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); health 0.06000000000000005 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1934.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Lena把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Lena读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Franz Lechner 2的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 49 -> 46; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +broken_comb_quarrel; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  Lena想起这家上次的诊费也没结，把处方推回去，只留下那张饮食单，说先照这个吃，药缓缓。Emil Huber把硬币收回口袋，站在药房门口没走，最后把饮食单折成小方块捏在手心，回去的路上下起了雨。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; health -0.03 (没有拿到药)_

- **1934.07** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family

  Lena和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Lena在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 46 -> 50; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 83 -> 80; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +refused_fugitive_shelter; knowledge “the fugitive went down the back stair without shoes”_

  Otto Winter把钥匙放在桌上，说母亲已经替她答应了婚事。Lena没有问是谁，也没有问日期；她只是把钥匙推回去，说留着吧，万一哪天用得上。Otto Winter走后，Lena在门口站了很久，直到楼道里的灯自己灭了。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; food_

- **1936.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/refuses_grudge` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Elisabeth Bauer敲门想借半块煤给发烧的孩子烧热水，Lena那天刚和Elisabeth Bauer为走廊堆东西吵过嘴，没开门，隔着门板说自家也不够用。后半夜听见Elisabeth Bauer抱着孩子下楼去敲药房的门。第二天在走廊碰见，Elisabeth Bauer低头绕过去，手里的铜壶碰在栏杆上，响了一声。
  _effects: relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1937.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Anton Hofer没有答应，只说现在不合适。Lena把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000700:child; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007412_

- **1937.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Lena在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Lena拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Maria Weiss找到Lena时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1937.06** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena把通知念给Alois Lechner听，Alois Lechner摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Lena去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food_

- **1937.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Katharina Moser说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Katharina Moser陪着Lena去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1937.08** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Lena走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  样张放在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的吸墨纸上，对方只说了一句知道了。Alois Lechner从办公室出来时脸是白的，走过字盘时肩膀撞了Lena一下。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-alois-lechner; cash 1.5 (雇主给的赏钱); reputation -0.15_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Lena在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Alois Lechner记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Lena带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

  Lena在《新维也纳日报》的失物栏里登了三行小字，花了四十个铜板。第二天，一个卖旧货的人说在电车终点站见过那样的铜牌。Josef Lechner放学后跟着Lena去找，一路上把书包带绕在手指上，绕得发白。
  _effects: cash -1 (在报纸上登失物启事); state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:557; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004584:child; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +advertised_for_the_childs_medal; flag +something_is_missing_

  Lena数了数前面攒动的人头，把口袋里的硬币又攥紧了些，转身离开队伍。走到街角的教堂时，管风琴正从敞开的门里传出来，Lena在最后一排长凳上坐下，听完整段前奏曲，一个赫勒也没花，只跪得膝盖发酸。出门时与Katharina Lechner道了别，约好下回还来排队。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000651:child_

  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Lena手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  抽水机运到山里才发现，矿洞的岩壁上早被人撒过金粉，岩芯也是挑过的。Gustav Kral在两个月前把股份转给了一家空壳公司，办公室只剩一张桌子和半瓶墨水。Mitzi Moser 2把调查报告放在Lena面前，最后一页写着“建议不予起诉”，因为找不到被告。

  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 14; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 60 -> 50; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +odd_job_scare_

  Lena跑了一趟医院，把Friedrich Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Lena回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.030000000000000027 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); flag +parent_hospitalized_

  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Lena靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费)_

- **1942.01** `task-vienna-family-care.care-sick-parent/night-watch` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Lena整夜没合眼，按时把最后两包药粉喂给Friedrich Hofer，水盆换到第五次。天快亮时咳嗽声稀了，老人的呼吸慢慢平下来。Lena在椅子上打了个盹，醒来时炉子灭了，赶到工坊迟了一刻钟，被扣了半天工钱。
  _effects: relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; cash -1.2 (迟到被扣的半天工钱（其中1.2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1942.03** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.notary-inheritance/demand-accounts-first` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  旧账本摊开，纸页上的铅笔字迹深浅不一。Hedwig念一笔，Lena就在边上记一笔，Elisabeth Bauer的钢笔悬在遗嘱上方没有落。Friedrich Hofer咳了一声，说屋顶那八克朗是自己看着修的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +demands_estate_accounting_

- **1942.11** `r15-curse.betrayal-at-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/sold-a-secret` tags: betrayal, social, family, trust, secret, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Huber把Lena在饭桌上说过的名字和地址卖给了别人。几天后，Lena发现自己的话出现在一封不该出现的信里。Luise Huber没有否认，只说“大家都要活”。
  _effects: flag +betrayed_after_trusting; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:755; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_
