  Lena给孩子擦了额头，也念了女人带来的祷词，但孩子的烧没有退。女人终于去请了医生，医生在门口说，再晚半天就麻烦了。Rosa Bauer 2把这一切记进本子，合上时没有看Lena；走廊里只剩下孩子咳嗽的声音。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (延误了请医生的时间); state_parameter under-investigation.progress 15 -> 30; reputation -0.04999999999999999; cash -2 (替孩子家垫付的诊费); flag +fm17_healing_failed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Lena谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。

- **1943.01** `task-vienna-medical-work.poor-patient/poor-patient-refused` tags: health, medicine, hospital, care, knowledge, profession, task, task:health
  Lena想起这家上次的诊费也没结，把处方推回去，只留下那张饮食单，说先照这个吃，药缓缓。Otto Winter把硬币收回口袋，站在药房门口没走，最后把饮食单折成小方块捏在手心，回去的路上下起了雨。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; health -0.030000000000000027 (没有拿到药)_

- **1943.03** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/give-camp-bed-and-board` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Lena让Berta Hofer睡厨房的行军床，讲好每月三克朗饭钱，煤钱另算。白天把铺盖卷起来靠在碗柜边，晚上再铺开；厨房多一个人，面包和煤都下去得快，伙食上短了两克朗。看门人再来问时，Lena把藤箱指过去，说人住下了，灯油钱也记在账上。
  _effects: cash 3 (表亲交来的饭钱); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; flag +kin_staying_over_

- **1943.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Alois Stein 2说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Alois Stein 2陪着Lena去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

  Lena没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.03 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_

  Lena在十月第一个星期三交了六克朗学费，又花一克朗二十赫勒买了课本。每个星期三下工后赶到商业夜校，Grete Stein在煤气灯下把借方贷方写在黑板上，粉笔灰落进袖口。年底结课时他领到一张结业证，上面写着簿记、商业算术、书信三门均及格。
  _effects: cash -7 (夜校学费与课本费); skill; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1944.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena没有立刻答话，先去敲Franz Lechner的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Franz Lechner提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Lena把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1944.10** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Franz Lechner，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Franz Lechner点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1944.11** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/speak-with-rent-numbers` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Lena没有喊口号，只问在场谁家的煤还够烧三天。十几只手举起来，连Josef Hofer也举了。书记说这些数字比演说有用，散会时几个人围上来问自己那条街怎么算。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001627:child_

  Lena走到半路风忽然大起来，围巾抽在脸上生疼，她掉头往回走。路过面包店时橱窗里还剩几个昨天的圆面包，四赫勒一个，她买了两个，揣在怀里带回去当晚饭。
  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个); health -0.010000000000000009 (在风口里走了一段，脸被吹得发红)_

  苏联士兵进城时，Lena躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Lena去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

  入会的事第二天就传到车间。工头在过道里拦住他，说厂里不反对工人交朋友，但下个月的排班要看表现。他回到机器旁，发现自己的工具柜被挪到了最靠门的位置，冷风直往领口灌。
  _effects: role; mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +foreman_watching_union_activity_

- **1945.06** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena上楼敲了Helene Hofer的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Helene Hofer把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Lena能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001432:child; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

  Lena只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Hedwig手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Hedwig把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Lena记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912_

- **1945.12** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Lena把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Ferdinand Bauer提着空碗上来时，只看见Lena把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

  Lena把传单推回Hedwig手里，说这活不能在我这台机上印。Hedwig收起纸走了，第二天车间里多了一个不说笑的角落。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912_

- **1946.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Lena在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Lena拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Gustav Lechner找到Lena时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000844:child_

- **1946.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Katharina Lechner攥着Lena的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧)_

  Lena在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Lena把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_

  Rosa Weiss把手里的 Semmel 换到另一只手，说家里不许她单独跟人出门，连星期天下午也不行。她声音不大，但话说得很清楚，随后补一句：教堂下个月有集体郊游，母亲也去，Lena若愿意可以一道走。Lena谢过她，转身时纸袋被捏出一道折痕。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003204:child_

- **1947.05** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.friend-intercession/intercession-too-far` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Lena听到Franz Winter把工钱和家底夸过了头，脸沉下来，当晚就照地址找上门去解释。话到嘴边绕成一团，Friedrich Winter的母亲把网兜往桌上一搁，说年轻人有话该早说，不该让旁人来传。Lena站在门里，名声在面包店门口折了一道，Friedrich Winter始终没有出来。
  _effects: reputation -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000323:child_

  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Lena转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Mitzi Winter还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.11** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena数出八赫勒交给Franz Winter，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Franz Winter说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Lena盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  那把菠菜根上还带着湿泥，叶子被压得有些蔫。Mitzi Winter先松了手，说你家人口少，拿半把够了。Lena把菜装进篮子时，听见摊主开始收秤砣，铁盘碰得叮当响。
  _effects: cash -0.04 (分买最后一把菠菜); food_

  Anton从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Anton站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Hermann Hofer睡醒哭了一声，Anton把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Josefine Hofer回来时，Anton正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000152:child; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Josef Bauer朝Anton招手。Anton挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Anton把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1919.07** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Anton跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Berta Weiss从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss_

- **1919.11** `p9-childhood-household.water-and-coal/ignored-mother-call` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  Anton听见Josefine Hofer喊，故意把楼梯踩得很响，空着手跑出巷口。放学回来，灶上的锅干着，水缸也见了底。Josefine Hofer没骂，只把扁担递过来。Anton挑到第四趟时天已经黑透，桶里的水晃出来，打湿了裤脚。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (傍晚被罚挑水四趟); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Josef Bauer从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Anton。Anton把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1920.06** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Anton把书压在算术本底下，只露出书脊。Mitzi Moser走过来，抽走算术本，书也跟着滑出来。折角那页画着一艘帆船。Mitzi Moser合上书，说放学后擦黑板，擦一星期；书被锁进讲台抽屉，没往校长室送。
  _effects: mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Helene Winter把Anton拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Anton把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Anton蹲下把两包并排，按Helene Winter说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Maria Berg把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Anton伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Maria Berg从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Anton手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Anton一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1922.08** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳下挂着湿床单，孩子们绕着跑，鞋底把水甩到墙上。Anton在第三圈超过Josef Bauer，手先碰到墙根。两颗玻璃弹珠进了Anton口袋，外套第二颗纽扣却崩进下水道。Josef Bauer从二楼窗口骂了一句，扔下一根别针。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1922.11** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/neighbor-interrupted` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Josefine Hofer探头进来时，Friedrich Hofer手里的皮带还没放下。Friedrich Hofer把它挂回门后，改口数落Anton，声音一阵高一阵低。Josefine Hofer接过盐，站了一会儿才走。Anton缩在灶台后面，等脚步声下了楼，才慢慢挪出来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; mood -0.03; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Anton的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Anton在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1923.09** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Anton每天放学都在柜台前站一会儿，看玻璃板底下压着的烟画，顺手把歪了的报纸摞齐。Rosa擦完柜台，从抽屉最里面摸出一张揉皱的风景卡，边角还沾着烟丝，塞过来时说了句“别挡着门”。Anton把卡夹进课本，第二天带了一张重复的来给Rosa看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1924.07** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Anton的手，又让Anton推两下刨子试试。Anton用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Josefine Hofer，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1924.10** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-one-visit-only` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Anton去了一个下午，看那些旧歌本里夹着褪色的圣像卡，工厂来的几个年轻人在最后一排用手指敲长凳打拍子。第二个星期天Anton没再去，在院子里帮母亲晾被单。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; mood 0.010000000000000002; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/delivery-paid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  床单叠成四方，压得筐绳勒进肩膀。Anton照着地址找到利奥波德城的两户人家，一户在院子深处，一户要上四层楼。按件算下来是一克朗六十赫勒，Anton把铜币一枚枚码在Lena枕边。Grete Stein在门口看见，只说“下回别敲错门”；家里省下的那份晚饭，让Lena多歇了一天。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (替妹妹送床单的计件工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (背着湿床单走了几个街区); relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; food_

- **1925.03** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Anton把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josefine Hofer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Anton那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1925.06** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, rural, agriculture
  下一站，Anton追下车，把那只手套递还给Ferdinand Winter 2。Ferdinand Winter 2说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Anton回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:61; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1925.06** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, rural, agriculture, small_town
  补考那天，Anton在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Hermann Weiss跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Anton去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1926.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Anton在衣帽间堵住Ferdinand Winter 2，问那两张票怎么到了Hedwig手里。Ferdinand Winter 2说Anton从来没敢开口，自己只是替Anton把话说了；Anton把票根揉成一团，塞进Ferdinand Winter 2的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 50; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 10; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 20; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.05000000000000001_

- **1926.01** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/ride-in-the-dark` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, rural, agriculture, small_town
  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Anton和Ferdinand Winter 2从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Ferdinand Hofer提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.05; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman); reputation -0.009999999999999995; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; flag +yf18_prater_stunt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Ferdinand Winter 2。这一次对方先点了头，Anton准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Anton才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 55; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 20 -> 28; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; mood 0.03_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.trade-school-application/missing-certificate` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Anton交了钱，Emil Hofer却把小学毕业证推回来，说印刷模糊，要他下星期三补一份学校盖章的副本。报名表压在桌角，夜班名额只留到星期五。他连跑两趟学校，第二趟才找到管事的人。
  _effects: cash -5 (报名手续费与押金); mood -0.04; flag +needs_school_certificate; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1926.01** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Anton把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Anton用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 55 -> 52; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 12_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002643_

- **1926.05** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/go-to-brickworks` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social, injury
  Friedrich Hofer把算术本合上，说家里等钱，下周一去砖厂。Anton没争。放学时Grete Stein在门口等着，塞来一张纸条，上面抄着六区夜校的地址。Anton把纸条折小，塞进鞋垫。砖厂第一周结四克朗，Anton搬砖搬到手指发抖，晚上脱鞋，袜底全是红砖末。
  _effects: role; cash 4 ({subject}砖厂首周工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (砖厂头几天搬砖); flag +has-evening-school-address; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1926.07** `r3-teen.inherited-tool::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/stolen` tags: family, inheritance, craft, grief, work, rural, agriculture, small_town
  收工回来，工具箱被撬开，刨子不见了。Anton去派出所报案，Katharina Weiss问工具上有没有记号，她说柄上有一道裂缝，是父亲用铁箍箍过的。警察写下来，说找到了会通知。
  _effects: cash -1 (刨子柄上的手汗); condition; flag +inherited_tool_stolen; knowledge “the plane had an iron band over a crack in the handle”_

- **1927.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton的「第一次心动」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:61; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/damp-linen-returned` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  客户把床单贴在脸上，又摸了摸边角，说“还是潮的”，把门合上一半。Anton把床单卷回筐里，背回洗衣房。Hermann Kral 2没骂，只让在后院重晾，件钱等重送完再结。Anton蹲在地上翻布角，晚风吹得膝盖发酸，天黑前才夹完最后一只夹子。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (蹲久了膝盖发酸，又吹了晚风); mood -0.039999999999999994; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1927.03** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/jump-from-the-tower` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, rural, agriculture
  Anton爬上河边的木跳台，下面的水黑得像一块铁。Hedwig在岸上抬头看，Anton想挥手，脚下一滑就掉了下去；浮上来时，嘴里全是河水的腥味，岸上的人都在鼓掌，Anton却只听见自己的心跳。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:127; health -0.020000000000000018 (a hard landing in the Danube); reputation 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_jumped_tower; mood 0.05_

- **1927.03** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/unpack-and-boil-water` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Anton把包袱重新打开，衬衫上的褶子已经压平了。Anton把洗礼证明放回抽屉，去厨房烧了一壶水；Josefine Hofer醒来时，桌上多了一杯茶，Anton说今天不想去上学。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; flag +yf18_unpacked_

- **1927.05** `yf18-friend.inkwell::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/deny-everything` tags: exam, cheating, friendship, betrayal, school, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Hermann Weiss把Anton和Hedwig一起叫到讲台前，问墨水瓶底下的公式是谁写的。Anton说不知道；Hedwig低着头，袖口还沾着同样的蓝墨水。Hermann Weiss让两人各写一份检讨，Anton写到一半，听见Hedwig在旁边小声说“对不起”，笔尖把纸戳破了。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_exam_suspicion; mood -0.04_

  舞卡上第一支曲子被Lena写走了。Anton在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Lena说没关系，把手放回Anton背后；第三支曲子响起时，Hedwig在门口冲Anton比了个手势，Anton假装没看见。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 68; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 20 -> 30; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.04_

  Anton只把Hedwig的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Anton是不是要请人喝咖啡；Anton说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 5 -> 17; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 19; reputation -0.03; mood -0.03_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/asked-to-come-back-later` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Elisabeth Huber看了看Anton抄的字母表，说大小不一，让他回去用直尺画格子重抄一遍，下个月再来。少年把纸折好，在咖啡馆门口站了一会儿，走回印刷厂时绕到排字房窗外看了很久。
  _effects: mood -0.03; skill; flag +mentor_pending_retry; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  Anton在学校的煤炉边把油印纸烧了，灰烬和煤渣混在一起。Emil Hofer第二天问第三十四页读得怎么样，Anton说译文看不出来；Emil Hofer点点头，把课本收回去，从此没有再借给Anton任何书。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; flag +yf18_burned_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

  Anton的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Lena骂了一句，Hedwig却把围巾解下来递给Anton；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 71 -> 79; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.secret true -> true; flag +yf18_failed_essay; mood -0.04000000000000001_

  Anton在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Hedwig。这一次对方先点了头，Anton准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Anton才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 79 -> 84; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 19 -> 27; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

  煤油烧到一半时灯芯冒起黑烟，Anton只好借面包房后窗透出的光背公式。后半夜他靠在墙上睡着了，醒来已经七点。考场上脑子发沉，算术卷子只答了一半，Grete Stein收卷时敲了敲桌面。
  _effects: health -0.06000000000000005 (熬夜且睡眠不足); mood -0.07999999999999999; flag +failed_entrance_exam; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.03999999999999998; health -0.040000000000000036 (连续夜间加班)_

  _effects: mood -0.05000000000000002; flag +hid_diary_after_snooping; skill; relation rival with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the diary lock was scratched while the subject was at school”_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; role; flag +marriage_blocked_by_lodging; cash -1 (bread from the bakery)_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Anton，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Anton没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 42 -> 50; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 28 -> 32; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/someone-asks-for-the-papers` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  房东要续租，必须出示结婚证或亲属关系证明。Anton说伴侣是表妹，房东却问为什么表妹的姓和籍贯都对不上。那天晚上，两个人把箱子重新打包，又把已经叠好的衣服一件件拿出来；谁也没有说下个月住哪里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 35 -> 47; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 40 -> 32; reputation -0.029999999999999995; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1933.01** `kernel-health.task-tuberculosis-cure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/tb-sanatorium-six-weeks` tags: health, task, tuberculosis, sanatorium, cure, reform, medical, schooling
  疗养院在山里，Anton带着介绍信和一只旧箱子住进六人病房。每天量体温、晒太阳、吃一盘带血的煎肉；六个星期后，咳嗽轻了，体重回来两公斤。出院时护士把体重记在卡片上，让他明年再来。
  _effects: cash -45 (six weeks in a mountain sanatorium（其中45克朗记入债务）); move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; health 0.05999999999999994 (sanatorium rest, fresh air and forced feeding); condition; role; knowledge “疗养院按六人一间收治，治疗是量体温、晒太阳和带血的煎肉，六周能回来两公斤。”_

- **1933.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Mitzi在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Mitzi敲开Anton的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1933.11** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Ferdinand Berg把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Anton留在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Anton在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06000000000000005; role_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Anton在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Katharina Weiss 2的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1934.01** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-water-goes-cold` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, rural
  Lena坐在床边，Anton用搪瓷盆替她洗头。水凉了又添，肥皂泡顺着耳后流下来；Lena闭着眼说，小时候她母亲也是这样替她洗的。窗外有人收煤，铁锹刮过石板，两个人谁也没有动。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 54 -> 59; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 47 -> 44; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 59 -> 63; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 44 -> 41; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Katharina Weiss 2把手里的 Semmel 换到另一只手，说家里不许她单独跟人出门，连星期天下午也不行。她声音不大，但话说得很清楚，随后补一句：教堂下个月有集体郊游，母亲也去，Anton若愿意可以一道走。Anton谢过她，转身时纸袋被捏出一道折痕。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1934.08** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Anton下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1934.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton跑了一趟医院，把Friedrich Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Anton回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.030000000000000027 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +parent_hospitalized_

  _effects: knowledge “报纸最后一栏被抽掉，不是因为版面调整。”; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +read_pulled_column; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1938.01** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-water-goes-cold` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Lena坐在床边，Anton用搪瓷盆替她洗头。水凉了又添，肥皂泡顺着耳后流下来；Lena闭着眼说，小时候她母亲也是这样替她洗的。窗外有人收煤，铁锹刮过石板，两个人谁也没有动。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 75 -> 80; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 52 -> 49; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Anton最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Anton把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Anton说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 49 -> 55; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 17 -> 14; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Anton把Rosa Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Rosa Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Anton听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 39; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 39; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `p9-work-career.journeyman-exam/p9-work-career.journeyman-exam.debt` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, apprentice, journeyman
  柜上要两克朗考试费，Anton翻遍口袋只有六赫勒。Gustav Berg 3把硬币替他排在台面，说钱先垫着，条件是出师后多留一年，工钱按伙计的八折。Anton盯着那张纸看了很久，最后签了名。证明拿到了，可接下来十二个月他仍旧离不开这间作坊。
  _effects: cash 2 (师傅垫付考试费，现钱过手); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; flag +bound_by_apprenticeship_debt_

- **1938.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Elisabeth Bauer嫌Anton的床单滴水滴到Elisabeth Bauer家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Anton下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Anton把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Elisabeth Bauer那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

- **1938.12** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1939.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Berta Hofer没有答应，只说现在不合适。Anton把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。

  Ferdinand Berg在门口和Alois Stein 2低声说了几句，塞过去一只信封。警察走后，他转头对Anton说，下个月的酒钱要加一倍。Grete Stein问要不要换地方；Anton说先把这个月过完。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; cash -1 (the raised bar bill after the bribe); state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 55 -> 60; flag +club_owner_paid_the_police_

  办公室有人问Anton，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Anton说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Anton把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 60 -> 68; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 14 -> 10; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

  Anton和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Anton在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 80 -> 84; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 68 -> 65; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `r13-civ.quarantine::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::r13-civ.quarantine-crisis/crisis-lodger-fever` tags: health, quarantine, epidemic, neighbor, confinement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara半夜敲墙，说同屋的人烧得说胡话。Anton隔着板壁问要不要去叫医生，Klara说医生来了也只给退热粉，还要收出诊费。天亮时，楼梯间多了一股石灰水味，Alois Stein 2把白线画到了隔壁门口。
  _effects: cash -2 (门框上的石灰线); food; health -0.040000000000000036 (门框上的石灰线); flag +crisis_quarantine_house; knowledge “a second household on the stair was placed under quarantine”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1939.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Anton在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Berta Hofer从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1939.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Anton的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1939.06** `statistical_recovery`
  Anton熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1939.10** `r3-crime.poisoning-case::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::one-microscope/drank-from-the-pot` tags: crime, medical, poison, marriage, suspicion, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton先盛了一碗，说“我先尝咸淡”。半个钟头后，Anton开始呕吐，Klara跑去叫Maria Lechner 2。洗胃时，Anton听见Alois Lechner在门外问“谁做的汤”，Klara说“是他自己”。Maria Lechner 2把铜勺放进证物袋，说“先查查锅”。
  _effects: cash -6 (汤锅边搁着一把铜勺); health -0.20000000000000007 (汤锅边搁着一把铜勺); condition; flag +poisoned_by_own_soup; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the soup was poisoned before the subject tasted it”_

  Anton的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Anton看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine)_

  Anton没掏自己的钱，先去了教区互助会，管事的人翻着册子答应补上差额，条件是要Klara在册子上按下手印。药费凑齐了，Klara把拇指在印泥上按了一下，红印落在名字旁边，像一枚小小的负担。
  _effects: health 0.04 (经由互助会拿到药); reputation 0.03; flag +parish_aid_arranged_

  Hermann Stein看完窗子，说这栋楼的地税涨了，房租也得跟着涨。Anton把租约摊在桌上，指给他看还有半年；Hermann Stein说“那就从下半年开始”。他走后，Anton把窗缝用旧报纸塞住，报纸上正好登着新房租的广告。
  _effects: cash -2 (rent increase after the landlord’s visit); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; flag +rent_raised; mood -0.020000000000000018; knowledge “房东说地税涨了，房租从下半年开始加。”_

  油灯下Gustav Kral递过来一块干面包，Anton一边啃一边把纸喂进压印机。天蒙蒙亮时Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne数出几枚硬币拍在桌上，没多一句话。

  棉纱缠了三圈，橙色碎屑沾在布面上。Anton用左手推料，右手悬着不敢使劲，每压一下冲床，指根就跳一下。工头走过来瞥了一眼，说这才像干活的样子。
  _effects: health -0.05999999999999994 (手指伤口未妥善处理); condition; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

  Anton和Alois 2趁午休去找工会代表，说护罩没装好就不能开机。代表记在本子上，答应晚上带去活动室说。Gustav Kral脸色难看，当天没再让挪皮带，但把两人的计件单压在抽屉底，拖到星期五才结算。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; cash -0.2 (计件钱被压到周五才结); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000430:child_

  Anton只喝了半杯就看见门口站着等位的老主顾，手里转着帽子。Anton把报纸叠好放回木架，起身让出窗边的位子，一杯 Melange 三十赫勒，侍者点头致意，没再收第二杯的钱。走出门时雨小了些，Anton站在檐下理了理衣领，觉得这半个下午也不算白来。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (一杯 Melange 的价钱); reputation 0.03; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Anton拒绝在写着“自己不小心”的事故单上按手印。Maria Lechner 2把单子收了回去，说那先去看仓库。厂里把他调去看仓库门，工钱照旧，只是胸口那口气一直没顺下去。
  _effects: employment 仓库看门 @ org-winter-print; reputation 0.04000000000000001; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Anton嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Anton用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; reputation 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108_

- **1941.09** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Klara却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Anton把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: flag +still_looking_for_room_

  Maria Lechner把Anton寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Anton追到码头时，Maria Lechner已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Anton在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。
  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱); relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001020:child; flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition_

  _effects: cash 2 (长桌两边的水杯); reputation 0.08; flag +work13_eight_hour_clause_won; employment Facharbeiter @ org-winter-print_

  Anton放弃存款，只拿走母亲的怀表；Hedwig答应替父亲还清杂货店的十一克朗。Alois Stein 2在清单上注了一笔，两人在走廊里站了一会儿，Hedwig说下个月去上坟。Anton把表上弦，表针走得很响。
  _effects: role; flag +kept_family_watch; knowledge “兄弟答应替父亲还清杂货店的十一克朗。”_

- **1943.03** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Alois Lechner追出来问怎么不领了，Anton说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Anton把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; food_

- **1943.03** `r3-ind.telegraph-office::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/buried-it-in-the-queue` tags: work, telegraph, office, delay, message, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton把湿电报压在待领筐最底下，继续抄当天的商业电文。下午Anna Weiss 2来问，Anton说没有这封。Anna Weiss 2走后，Anton把电报翻出来，纸角已经粘在筐底，收报人的名字被水洇成一团蓝。
  _effects: flag +hid_a_delayed_telegram; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; knowledge “the telegram was still at the bottom of the pending basket”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

  Franz Winter 2把Anton领到工会代表面前，说护罩少了螺丝不是头一回。代表翻开小本记下机器编号，答应陪Anton去医院，费用由工会垫付，但要Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故单上签字，工头磨蹭半天才落笔。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (工会垫付后自理的零头（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999994 (及时就医处理伤口); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; flag +union_contact_

  Grete Stein说Mitzi Bauer这学期退步，是因为家里晚上太吵。Anton想说自己夜里在做计件活，话到嘴边又咽回去；Mitzi Bauer站在走廊尽头，书包带子断了一边。回家的路上，Anton把书包带打了个结，谁也没有提老师的话。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 51; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; cash -1 (extra exercise books after the meeting)_

  _effects: cash -2 (账本上的红字（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; skill_

  _effects: cash -3 (床位按八小时轮班); condition; flag +rented_hot_bed; knowledge “the bed is rented in eight-hour shifts”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; reputation -0.020000000000000004; health -0.020000000000000018 (在湿冷街头耗到傍晚)_

  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  Anton托人介绍了一位老师傅，见面时对方只问Anton能不能吃苦。Anton说能，老师傅让Anton搬了三天铁块，第四天没再来。Anton在作坊门口等到天黑，门房说老师傅去了外地，Anton把带来的礼包留在门房。看门人把礼包收进柜子，没说到哪去。

- **1918.05** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/neighbor-carries` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  Anna Moser的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Hedwig坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Anna Moser把桶一放，夹起Hedwig进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Josefine Hofer赶来时，Hedwig正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (膝盖擦破，已冲洗); food_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Maria Kral把Hedwig拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Hedwig把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Hedwig蹲下把两包并排，按Maria Kral说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1919.07** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/strap-raised-then-dropped` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  皮带从门后抽出来，Friedrich Hofer举到半空，停住了。Hedwig闭着眼等，只听见皮带扣碰在桌沿上。Friedrich Hofer把它放下，说晚饭后不许出门，把碎碗片捡干净。Hedwig蹲在地上一片片捡，窗外传来同伴的笑声，指尖被瓷片划出一道白口。
  _effects: mood -0.04; health -0.010000000000000009 (手指被瓷片划破); relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Hedwig站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Otto Winter在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Hedwig掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Hedwig才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

- **1920.10** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  Hedwig想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Grete Stein听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Hedwig试着站起来，被Grete Stein按回台阶上。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1921.01** `p9-childhood-household.water-and-coal/ignored-mother-call` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  Hedwig听见Josefine Hofer喊，故意把楼梯踩得很响，空着手跑出巷口。放学回来，灶上的锅干着，水缸也见了底。Josefine Hofer没骂，只把扁担递过来。Hedwig挑到第四趟时天已经黑透，桶里的水晃出来，打湿了裤脚。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (傍晚被罚挑水四趟); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Hedwig的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Hedwig在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.030000000000000006; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1921.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Mitzi Moser从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Hedwig答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Hedwig回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Mitzi Moser看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Helene Bauer把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Hedwig伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Helene Bauer从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Hedwig手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Hedwig一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1922.03** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  土豆从围裙里滚出来，Hedwig蹲下去追，手心一松，三枚十克鲁泽的铜板叮叮当当掉在石板路上。两枚捡回来了，最后一枚滚进排水格栅，在铁条下转了个圈。Hedwig趴下去够，指甲刮在铁条上。回家Josefine Hofer数完钱，脸色沉下来，Hedwig站在桌边不敢动。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; mood -0.06_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Hedwig的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Hedwig在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1924.03** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-follow-without-paying` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Hedwig口袋里没有十赫勒，却还是跟着队伍走到车站。修女在栅栏边拦住Hedwig，翻了翻名册，说没交钱的孩子不能上车；Ferdinand Winter 2在车厢门口回头看。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  客户刚把床单接过去，Anna Lechner就从楼梯口上来，盯着Hedwig看了两秒，说“你不是这家的”。Anna Lechner当场把工钱收回，说明天要去找Anton的雇主。Hedwig站在门口，手里还攥着空筐绳，回家一路上没吃东西，Anton问起来，只摇了摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.07000000000000003; relation kin with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1925.05** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/stay-in-school` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Emil Hofer没有催Hedwig回家，把黑板上的题擦了，又写了一道更长的。Hedwig算到一半，Emil Hofer说“再念一年”。门外的Friedrich Hofer把帽子摘下来又戴上，帽檐压得比刚才低。Hedwig看见门缝里的影子，铅笔停了一下。
  _effects: education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1925.07** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, rural, agriculture
  下一站，Hedwig追下车，把那只手套递还给Lena。Lena说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Hedwig回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:64; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/printshop-apprentice` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Hedwig下周一到排字房报到，先搬铅字和扫地，月薪十八克朗。家里用第一笔预付工资买了双结实的鞋。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance); role_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/rehearsed-conversation` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Hedwig在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Lena三步远的地方拐进面包店。Hedwig买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 56; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 13; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.learn-the-task/small-ad-set` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  字盘里的铅字反着光，Hedwig用镊子一个个夹起来，照价目表排下去。空铅填到最后一格，版心不晃，Berta Hofer对着窗看了看，把一张小广告推过来。Hedwig排到“本店新到”，手腕抖了一下，字却没散；旁边的伙计吹了声口哨。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.04; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1926.03** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Hedwig站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Emil Hofer追出来说座位可以保留两周，Hedwig点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.03999999999999998; health -0.040000000000000036 (连续夜间加班)_

- **1926.06** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/hide-the-bundle` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Hedwig把两件衬衫、一双袜子和小时候的洗礼证明卷进一块旧桌布，塞到床底下。Josefine Hofer在厨房里数硬币，数了三遍都没有抬头；Hedwig躺在黑暗里，听见夜车从远处经过，铁轨的声音一直传到枕头下面。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:94; flag +yf18_bundle_hidden; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.10** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Hedwig在衣帽间堵住Anton，问那两张票怎么到了Lena手里。Anton说Hedwig从来没敢开口，自己只是替Hedwig把话说了；Hedwig把票根揉成一团，塞进Anton的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 50; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 10; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 20; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.04999999999999999_

  Lena把车票放在桌上，说家里给她找了活，走不了。Hedwig把两张票叠在一起，撕掉一张，另一张塞进内袋；Lena问Hedwig一个人怕不怕，Hedwig说不怕，出门时却把地址写给了她。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 53 -> 63; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 15 -> 20; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 13 -> 18; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_go_alone; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Hedwig在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Lena。这一次对方先点了头，Hedwig准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Hedwig才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 59 -> 64; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 15 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 20 -> 32; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 18 -> 20; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Hedwig的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Lena骂了一句，Anton却把围巾解下来递给Hedwig；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。

  Hedwig凑不够十二克朗的正班学费，在门口站了一会儿，转身去了两条街外的教会补习班。修女收下他，发了一本旧算术册子，说每晚七点来，不要钱，但要帮着擦黑板。
  _effects: mood 0.02999999999999997; skill; flag +church_tutoring; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1928.08** `r1-cul.fan-letter::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/quoted-without-permission` tags: culture, letters, fandom, literacy, ambition, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Hedwig的信寄出半年，没有回音。后来Theresia Huber在一篇文章里引用了这封信，没有署名，只写一个读者说。Hedwig在报上读到自己的句子，先是高兴，随后发现文章里的意思和自己写的相反。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation 0.03; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-theresia-huber; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1928.09** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/wade-in-first` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, rural, agriculture
  多瑙河的浴棚按男女分开，木板缝里能看见对面的人影。Hedwig穿着借来的毛线泳衣，站在第三间门口不敢下水；Lena从水里冒出来，头发贴在额头上，说水不冷。Hedwig走进河里时，满脑子都是自己肋骨上那道疤。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:157; health 0.020000000000000018 (cold river water and a long swim); relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.02999999999999997_

- **1928.12** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Hedwig在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Hedwig用牙咬断，重新来。Josefine Hofer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Hedwig把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1929.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/friend-cuts-in` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Anton在第二支曲子前把Hermann Hofer请走，说Hedwig答应过要替她看管外套。Hedwig抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Hedwig把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 55; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 15 -> 7; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 5; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000152:child; mood -0.03999999999999998_

  Hedwig在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Lena。这一次对方先点了头，Hedwig准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Hedwig才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 55 -> 60; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 7 -> 15; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Hedwig把公式用铅笔写在左手心，出门前在水龙头下搓了搓，字迹只剩浅浅灰印。考场上他凭记忆答题，算到第三题时手心出汗，灰印化开成一道黑线。他答完了，但不知道对不对。
  _effects: flag +exam_guilt; mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

  考试周里，Hedwig把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Hedwig用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。

  为赶选举传单，Hedwig连排三个晚上。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把加班算成“学徒练习”，只多给半克朗。Katharina Weiss 2在楼梯间说这是常事，叫Hedwig周末去八角亭替人抄货单。
  _effects: cash 0.5 (三晚加班只补半克朗); health -0.050000000000000044 (连续三晚排字熬到十点); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1929.02** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/ride-in-the-dark` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, rural, agriculture, small_town
  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Hedwig和Anton从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Rosa Hofer提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman); reputation -0.010000000000000002; flag +yf18_prater_stunt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1929.06** `r2-leisure.scullery-punishment::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/burned-palm` tags: domestic_service, abuse, punishment, burn, servant, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne抓住Hedwig的手腕，把掌心按在熨斗底座上。皮肉先发白，随后才红起来；Hedwig没有叫，只听见自己的呼吸。Josef Huber把一盆冷水端过来，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说不用，让她记住。
  _effects: health -0.12 (熨斗底下的手); mood -0.19999999999999996; condition; flag +burned_by_mistress; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; knowledge “the mistress burned a maid with an iron”_

- **1930.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Hedwig没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_

  Hedwig把最后一箱书搬下楼时，Grete Hofer 2堵在门口，说墙皮被蹭掉一块，箱子先留下。Hedwig说要赶车，Grete Hofer 2说那就把修墙的钱付了。板车在巷口按铃，Hedwig只抱走了装证件的木盒。
  _effects: cash -2 (板车与押金收据（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +landlord_withheld_trunk; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; knowledge “the landlord keeps the trunk against a wall repair claim”_

  _effects: health 0.010000000000000009 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:251; flag +accusation_echoed; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Grete Hofer 2病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: health -0.05 (the curse answers a real betrayal); condition; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 0 -> 1; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  面试在一间改作办公室的公寓里进行。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把三把椅子摆成一排，自己坐中间，让Hedwig坐最边上。问到第三句，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne忽然说：“客人若认出你，你先介绍店，还是先介绍自己？”Hedwig说先介绍店；Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在纸上画了一个圈，又把它涂成实心。

  成绩过了，厂里却没有正式空缺。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Hedwig先做三个月替工，按日结算，一个月四十八克朗，出师纸压在账房抽屉里。Josef Huber说等秋天有人走，再谈正式位置；Hedwig把工具袋搬到自己那张案子下面。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Aushilfssetzer @ org-winter-print; role; flag +temporary_journeyman; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Hedwig不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Hedwig拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Hedwig的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

  Josef Bauer把Hedwig叫进办公室，桌上摊着Emil Weiss交来的三页证词。Hedwig逐条回答，Josef Bauer逐条记录，写到“是否曾诅咒他人”时停了很久。最后Hedwig被允许回家，但门口多了一个每月来一次的访客。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 20 -> 45; state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 15 -> 25; condition; reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; relation rival with person-emil-weiss; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。

- **1932.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Hedwig的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Hedwig被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call); health 0.03 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Hedwig用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Emil Weiss指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/counts-the-interval` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  Hedwig把两次背叛和两次发烧的日期写在同一张纸上，发现中间都隔着十一天。Hedwig没有告诉任何人，只把纸折成很小的一块，塞进缝衣盒的夹层。那天夜里，Hedwig第一次试着在门口撒盐，动作熟练得让自己停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “背叛与发烧之间隔着十一天。”; flag +curse_counted; skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Hedwig谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 10 -> 25; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1936.01** `r13-work.reference-letter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/letter-refused` tags: work, reference, hiring, favour, reputation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Berta Bauer 2说Hedwig走的时候没有打招呼，现在写信等于打自己的脸。Hedwig站在门房外，把来意又说了一遍，Berta Bauer 2低头去拨炉子，不再接话。回家的路上，Hedwig把准备好的一小包烟叶捏碎了，撒在路边的雪里。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +work13_reference_letter_refused; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1936.03** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Alois Moser 2把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Hedwig把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Alois Moser 2低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; flag +rent_increase_accepted_

- **1936.05** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/organize-relief-table` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Hedwig用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Alois Moser 2指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.06** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Hedwig当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-marie-huber_

- **1937.01** `cj17-police.lineup::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/bought-the-witness` tags: police, court, legal, investigation, mistaken_identity, humiliation, vienna, urban
  家里凑了三克朗，由Hedwig的家里人在咖啡馆后门交给Grete Hofer 2。第二天，Grete Hofer 2在预审室里说光线太暗，认不清。Alois Stein 2合上本子，说证据不足，但没有把案子结掉。Hedwig知道那张纸还在某个抽屉里，只是暂时不压在自己身上。
  _effects: cash -3 (在咖啡馆后门交给证人的钱); state_parameter under-investigation.progress 25 -> 20; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; flag +witness_paid_off; knowledge “证人收了钱才说光线太暗，案子并没有真正结掉。”; mood -0.030000000000000027; state_add cj17-wanted state:world-vienna-1912:520; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; flag +wanted_by_authorities_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Hedwig谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 20 -> 35; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `r13-work.advertise::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/poster-brought-customers` tags: commerce, advertising, shop, competition, literacy, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa Hofer用红色油墨印了三十张海报，Hedwig把它们贴在面包房、澡堂和电车站的柱子上。海报上写着“新到咖啡，一磅九赫勒”，字歪了一点，价格却看得清。第三天，三个从没进过店的客人拿着海报来问，Hedwig把海报上的价又让了半赫勒。
  _effects: cash 5 (橱窗里的手写海报); reputation 0.05; flag +work13_poster_brought_customers; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1937.07** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Grete Hofer 2追出来问怎么不领了，Hedwig说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Hedwig把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.029999999999999916; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; food_

- **1937.11** `bc18-crime.inept-blackmail::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/mourning-card` tags: crime, blackmail, photograph, mistake, humiliation, absurd, reputation, vienna
  Grete Hofer 2在葬礼上偷拍了一张Hedwig靠着棺材睡着的照片，说只要不给四克朗，就把照片寄给Hedwig的老板。Hedwig还没有凑钱，照片先被家里人选去做了悼念卡；亲戚们说，只有真正伤心的人才会累成这样。Grete Hofer 2拿着底片站在门口，第一次不知道该威胁谁。
  _effects: cash 3 (相馆支付的悼念卡照片使用费); reputation 0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; condition; flag +blackmail_photo_became_mourning_card; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Mitzi Moser 2在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Hedwig排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

  _effects: cash -0.06 (教堂捐献与烤土豆); food; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000844:child_

- **1939.01** `df18-curse.name-in-the-door::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/name-and-date` tags: curse, name, door, omen, neighbor, dark, supernatural, vienna
  Hedwig在自家门框上看见一行新刻的字，是自己的名字，下面还有当天的日期。刀痕很浅，像是用钥匙划的。Rosa Hofer说不是他，Hedwig也没有再问。那天夜里，Hedwig把门闩插了两遍，听见楼梯上有脚步声停了一下。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “门框上出现了我的名字和当天的日期。”; flag +df18_name_carved_on_door_

- **1939.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.volksbibliothek` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Hedwig听Emil Lechner 3报出押金数目，把伸进内袋的手又抽了回来。问清免费阅览处的位置后，Hedwig穿过两条街，在 Volksbibliothek 的阅览室门口登记了姓名，坐在长桌边读了一下午；一个赫勒没花，只是不能把书带回家，天黑前就得还回去。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1939.05** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/reply-at-bakery` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  第四天，Anna Stein的回信从门缝底下塞进来，纸边被门槛磨毛了。Anna Stein约Hedwig星期六早上在街角面包店门口见，说想买一个两赫勒的小圆面包，两个人分。Mitzi Lechner在楼道里撞见Hedwig读信，笑着问是不是好消息；Hedwig把信折回四折，塞进内袋贴着胸口。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.07000000000000006; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1939.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/jam-at-door` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Hedwig只送来一罐杏子果酱，没有进门，把罐子放在门槛里边，说了句汤你们先喝。Lena在屋里听见了，走到门口时人已经下楼。果酱罐上没有标签，只裹着一层报纸，瓶口用蜡封着；这一罐甜食抵得上一克朗的吃食，却没能把两个人重新按到一张桌上。
  _effects: food; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.11** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Hedwig走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1940.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/investigation` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Mitzi Moser 2来问过一次话。问题围绕Maria Weiss的病，也围绕Hedwig最近有没有说过什么威胁的话。Hedwig如实回答，Mitzi Moser 2把每个名字都写进了本子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:616_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007834_

- **1940.01** `df18-omen.crows-on-the-wire::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/ignore-the-birds` tags: omen, legal, investigation, crow, court, eerie, superstition, vienna
  Hedwig没有理那些乌鸦。开庭时，一个原本说没看见的证人改了口供，Alois Lechner把新证词夹进案卷。Hedwig坐在长椅上，听见自己的名字被念了两遍；窗外的乌鸦还停在电线上，像一排没有表情的旁听者。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 10 -> 25; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “证人在开庭时改了口供。”; flag +df18_witness_changed_story_

- **1940.01** `p10-family-tasks.appeal-inheritance/appeal-inheritance.court` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, appeal
  职员把信翻过来看背面，说私人纠纷不归这里管，去法院。律师的写字间在隔壁楼，开价三十克朗。Hedwig数了数证件夹里的钱，差得远。兄弟在门口说，那就等传票。Hedwig把信收好，下楼时鞋跟磕在石阶上。
  _effects: knowledge “遗产诉讼费要三十克朗，办事处不受理私人纠纷”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1940.03** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig自己发起了高烧；Franz Lechner第二天照常出门。没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 2 -> 3; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009721_

- **1940.07** `r3-crime.neighborhood-patrol::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::party-men-at-the-lamp/beating-at-the-corner` tags: crime, neighborhood, patrol, violence, community, vienna, urban, crisis
  巡逻队把那个收破烂的老人按在墙上，说他半夜撬门。Hedwig看见老人手里只有一根铁钩，想上去拦，Rosa拉住她的袖子，说“别管”。Alois Stein 2的警棍落下去时，马灯晃了一下，墙上的影子也跟着晃。第二天，老人没有再来这条街。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; condition; flag +saw_patrol_beat_an_innocent_man; relation rival with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the old ragpicker was beaten for carrying a hook”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

- **1940.10** `hist15-occ.seed-occupation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-old-clerk-stays` tags: occupation, war, curfew, history, place, institution, vienna, urban
  新来的军官站在市政厅门口，旧办事员还在窗后盖章。Hedwig排队领一张新的通行证，窗口里的人低声说：“名字照旧写，住址别改。”队伍里没有人说话，只有纸张翻动的声音。
  _effects: state_add occupied-city-regime state:world-vienna-1912:644; knowledge “旧办事员仍在窗后盖章，只要名字和住址不改，通行证还能办。”_

  捐款交出去三个月，诊所还没有开门，先来了一辆新的弹簧马车和一张桃花心木办公桌。Rosa Bauer 2把账本摊在桌上，说委员会认为“体面的门面”也是诊所的一部分。Grete Stein在门口骂了一句，Hedwig把账本翻到最后一页，发现自己捐的钱只剩一半。
  _effects: cash -30 (the clinic endowment was spent on furniture and a wagon); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 30 -> 0; reputation -0.05000000000000002; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; condition; flag +clinic_funds_misused; knowledge “诊所的捐款先买了一辆弹簧马车和一张桃花心木办公桌。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

  Hedwig把纸从柜底拿出来，交给便衣，说这是家里留下的旧账。便衣对着灯看了很久，问立字的人现在在哪里；Hedwig说死了。他们把纸折好带走，临走时记下了门牌。Anton从里屋出来，问交了什么，Hedwig说一张没用的旧纸。

- **1942.09** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family

  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

  Gustav Hofer数到十，声音比鸟叫还轻。Hedwig把枪口抬高一寸，子弹打进橡树，树皮落了一地。Rosa Bauer 2愣了一下，也把枪口转向天空。Gustav Hofer把两支枪收进盒子，说面子保住了。Alois Lechner从树后走出来，药箱上的铜扣还是没有打开。

  Hedwig把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Hedwig的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。

  Hedwig把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Berta Weiss借给Hedwig一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Hedwig睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。

## Marie Huber

Born 1904.02, died 1936.12 (age 32).
Education: higher 1; occupation: none; roles: orphan, pupil, orphanage_inmate, factory_hand, scholarship_holder, day_laborer, refused_suitor, strike_delegate, transit_lodger, rumor_stopper, nurse.

### State snapshot
No mechanism state was ever attached.

### 86 subject events

- **1913.01** `vienna-1912-orphan-guardianship/guardian-orphanage` tags: childhood, bereavement, guardianship, orphan, poor_relief, migration
  Hofer 认为 Theresia 六十二岁、眼睛又不好，无法独自监护。Marie 被送到市立孤儿院，床位在二层大通铺；Theresia 每周日去看她。她的学籍转到院内学校，包袱里只带了一件换洗罩衫。
  _effects: move place-wien-orphanage; role; relation guardian with person-marie-huber; education primary_

- **1913.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Theresia Huber从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Marie Huber。Marie Huber把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-theresia-huber; skill_

- **1913.02** `iw18-school.bell-rope::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-school/the-teacher-rings-it` tags: childhood, school, bell, comic, play, injury, vienna, urban
  Grete Stein没有让孩子再爬煤箱，而是把Marie Huber抱起来，让孩子自己抓住铃绳。铃响时，Marie Huber离地很高，能看见院子里所有孩子的头顶。Grete Stein放下孩子后，说“以后想摇铃就来找我”，Marie Huber点头，手心还留着粗麻绳的印子。
  _effects: mood 0.04; flag +lifted_to_the_bell_

- **1913.07** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Grete Stein从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Marie Huber答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Marie Huber回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Grete Stein看。
  _effects: skill_

- **1913.10** `vienna-1912-school-place/school-book-aid` tags: childhood, school_access, school_fee, orphan, teacher, poor_relief
  Grete Stein 在一年级教室给 Marie 留了最后一个靠窗座位。Hofer 的监护确认书补上缺失的出生证明，学校免掉课本费，Theresia 只需买一双结实的鞋。Marie 当天领到一本二手读本，封皮上还写着前一个学生的名字。
  _effects: cash -3 (shoes for school); education primary; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber_

  _effects: mood -0.1; flag +rejected_by_music_teacher; relation rival with person-grete-stein; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  Marie Huber的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1915.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-early-morning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  Marie Huber把错题纸举到讲台边，话说到一半就低下去了。Grete Stein放下红笔，说“明早提前来”。第二天Marie Huber摸黑走到Leopoldstädter Volksschule，教室门已经开了，炉子冒着青烟。Grete Stein把竖式写在黑板上，先擦掉一半，让Marie Huber补上，再一笔一笔讲为什么。
  _effects: skill; health -0.020000000000000018 (天没亮出门，早春的冷)_

- **1916.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Marie Huber拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Theresia Huber弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Marie Huber接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-theresia-huber; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1917.01** `r13-edu.ask-teacher::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/teacher-explains` tags: education, teacher, help, school, confidence, university, culture, schooling
  Marie Huber在办公室门口站到下课，才敢把作业本递进去。Emil Hofer翻到错得最多的那一页，没有骂，只让Marie Huber把题目再读一遍。读到第三遍，Marie Huber忽然明白自己一直看漏了条件。Emil Hofer把红笔放下，说以后不懂就来。那天以后，Marie Huber的作业本上多了几行红字，都是Emil Hofer夜里改的。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1918.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/first-recital` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, university, schooling, school
  Marie Huber在期末演奏会上拉完一首短曲，弓毛断了两根，最后一个音还是稳住了。Alois Stein在台下点头，师母把手套摘下来鼓掌。回家后，Marie Huber把琴擦了三遍，才发现肩托把脖子磨出一道红印，碰一下就疼。Marie Huber没有告诉任何人。琴盒没关。
  _effects: reputation 0.049999999999999996; flag +first_recital; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

  _effects: reputation 0.04; flag +refused_theft_dare; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  Marie Huber等到天黑，Alois Stein一直在改别的班的卷子，头也没抬。Marie Huber把问题写在纸条上压在墨水瓶下，第二天发现纸条还在原处，只是背面多了一行字：“先看第三章。”Marie Huber按纸条翻到第三章，发现那页夹着一张旧的辅助线图，图上的线画得很轻。
  _effects: skill_

  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); health -0.06000000000000005 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne只招日工。Marie Huber天亮前到门口，名字写在临时名单上，干一天算一天，一天一克朗，月底结二十六克朗。工头说下个月可能有正式位置，也可能没有；Marie Huber把每天的工时记在烟盒背面，怕漏掉一天。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; role; flag +marriage_blocked_by_lodging; cash -1 (bread from the bakery)_

  Marie Huber的试讲被督学打断，督学说新方法太散，还是背书写字牢靠。Marie Huber没有争辩，在课后把两种教案都写了一遍，附上学生的作业对比。督学看完，没有表态，只把材料带走了，Marie Huber在门口站到天黑。教案被留在督学的公文包里。

  _effects: flag +film_censored; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; cash -2 (被剪后补租胶片的费用); reputation 0.02999999999999997; knowledge “被剪掉的胶片接回去也过不了审查，画面会跳一下”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1927.10** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Marie Huber正要把空床许给Otto Winter，Ferdinand Hofer却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Marie Huber回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Otto Winter，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

  Marie Huber把湿床单拧干，隔着蒸汽问Maria Weiss那句话是从哪儿听来的。Maria Weiss说全楼都在传，不止一个。Marie Huber让对方把原话再说一遍，然后当着Ferdinand Hofer的面把日期、工钱和那晚的去处一条条对给对方听。Maria Weiss的手在围裙上擦了半天，说也许是听岔了。
  _effects: reputation 0.08000000000000002; flag +confronted_laundry_rumor; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; knowledge “the rumour started at the market stall, not in the laundry”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

  Marie Huber发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Helene Bauer问是谁动的。Helene Bauer说是Alois Weiss嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  Marie Huber第一次给病人扎针，手抖得厉害，Grete Stein让Marie Huber先在自己胳膊上试。Marie Huber扎了两次才回血，病人反倒安慰Marie Huber。三个月后，Marie Huber能闭着眼找到血管，白大褂口袋里总装着一小卷胶布和一块剪好的纱布。Grete Stein在排班表上把Marie Huber的名字往前挪了一格。

### 189 subject events

- **1913.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/damp-linen-returned` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Franz Lechner解释说路上没下雨，是床单叠得太厚。客户不听，Josef Bauer也没帮腔，只把湿床单抖开，让Franz Lechner自己晾。Franz Lechner把布角一寸寸翻过来，跟Josef Bauer说以后会分开叠、不让潮气闷在里头。Josef Bauer哼了一声，算是记下了。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (蹲久了膝盖发酸，又吹了晚风); mood -0.04; skill; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1913.02** `r1-fam.schooling-decision::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-school/school.apprenticeship` tags: family, education, apprenticeship, poverty, future, vienna, urban, peace
  Josef Bauer把学徒合同摊在桌上，说三年管饭，出师后一天两克朗。Grete Stein说Franz Lechner的算术是班里最好的，再读两年可以考师范。Alois Lechner把合同推回去，说家里等不起两年；Franz Lechner看着窗外，操场上有人在踢一只破球。
  _effects: cash 2 (学徒合同还是课本); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; role; relation colleague with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “老师认定这个学生能考上师范。”; education apprenticeship_

- **1913.03** `r3-teen.disability-at-school::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-school/accommodated` tags: disability, school, injury, access, institution, vienna, urban, peace
  腿伤拆了石膏以后，Grete Stein把Franz Lechner的课桌搬到一楼，又在楼梯口放了一把旧椅子，让她课间坐着。Friedrich Hofer来量了台阶的高度，说坡道要等明年预算。Franz Lechner把拐杖靠在桌边，第一次没有迟到。
  _effects: condition; flag +school_accommodated_disability; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; education secondary_

- **1913.06** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/sent-to-country` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, university, culture, schooling, school
  Franz Lechner没有进厂，也没有留在家里，而是搭了去乡下的火车。姨妈家在葡萄园边上，缺人摘果子。Franz Lechner把课本塞进箱底，白天干活，晚上在油灯下认几个字。秋天寄回家的信里，Franz Lechner说这里的星星比城里多。车票两克朗，是姨妈先垫的。
  _effects: cash -2 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); flag +left_school_for_country; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house_

- **1913.09** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, vienna, urban, peace
  Friedrich Hofer把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Franz Lechner看期限、工钱和出师考试。Franz Lechner签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-friedrich-hofer; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:1; education apprenticeship_

- **1914.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Franz Lechner废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; cash -1 (wasted material（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1914.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.ask-for-trial/too-young-refused` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Friedrich Hofer把Franz Lechner的袖子往上捋了捋，看了看手腕，说骨头还没长开，搬不动整摞的纸。Franz Lechner没走，蹲在门口把被风刮散的纸边捡回筐里。Friedrich Hofer给了两个赫勒，挥手赶人；Franz Lechner沿着街把另外两家作坊的门牌记下，才回家。
  _effects: cash 0.2 (替作坊捡纸边得到的两个赫勒); mood -0.04000000000000001; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1914.02** `r2-leisure.diary-snoop::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/hid-the-diary` tags: leisure, diary, privacy, family, betrayal, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Franz Lechner把日记本塞进床垫和墙之间的缝里，又用一件旧毛衣盖住。第二天放学回来，本子还在原处，小铜锁上多了一道划痕。Mitzi Lechner在厨房里哼歌，声音比平时高。Franz Lechner把钥匙从项链上取下来，换了一个地方藏。
  _effects: mood -0.049999999999999996; flag +hid_diary_after_snooping; skill; relation rival with person-mitzi-lechner; knowledge “the diary lock was scratched while the subject was at school”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 30; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Rosa Lechner把火柴盒放回窗台，说等发了工钱再补给他。Franz Lechner把抄好的纸袋叠成小册子，用缝衣针别在一起，封面写了书名，字比平时大。
  _effects: cash -2 (积蓄被挪去买煤（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06; skill; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1915.02** `r3-teen.inherited-tool::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/sells` tags: family, inheritance, craft, grief, work, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Franz Lechner把刨子押在当铺，换了几天的面包。当票上写着“旧工具一件”，她看了很久，才把纸折好。晚上做饭时，她总觉得自己少了一只手。第二天，她用一块旧布把空出来的工具箱格子盖住。
  _effects: cash 4 (刨子柄上的手汗); mood -0.12000000000000002; condition; flag +pawned_inherited_tool_
