- **1916.01** `task-vienna-youth-ask-old-worker/lesson-given` tags: task, task:education, work, apprenticeship, learning
  Franz Lechner等老工人收拾完字盘，才开口问那道总夹不紧的铅字。Mitzi Moser让他把手放低，示范了三遍，又让他自己做一遍；最后只说“明天再练”。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; knowledge “铅字夹不紧时，先放松手腕再重新上力。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1916.03** `yf18-body.false-alarm::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/carry-the-coal` tags: body, pregnancy_scare, class, shame, exam, comic, rural, agriculture
  Rosa Hofer在电车月票背面写了“晚了十天”，Franz Lechner把攒着买课本的钱塞进鞋垫，陪她去了她母亲家。助产士Anna Moser 2把窗帘拉上，问了几句，又让Rosa Hofer喝了一杯糖水；最后她说只是考试周乱了日子，转头让Franz Lechner把后院那筐煤搬进厨房。
  _effects: cash -3 (the textbook money went on a doctor's visit（其中3克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; flag +yf18_false_alarm; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1916.06** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/ride-in-the-dark` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, rural, agriculture, small_town
  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Franz Lechner和Gustav Weiss从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Emil Hofer提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.01; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; flag +yf18_prater_stunt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1917.01** `kernel-migrate.city-arrival::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement::civic-city-arrival-rural/civic-city-arrival-rural-leave` tags: migration, city, work, housing, rural, agriculture, small_town, peace
  他把葡萄园的最后一批筐搬上车，家里托人带话，说城里地下室还有一张空床。火车开动时，他看见田埂上的石墙一段段退后；到城里已是傍晚，他按地址找到那间屋子，房东说工作要等下周。
  _effects: move place-wien; residence; cash -4 (车票、行李费和第一周的饭钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); flag +left_vineyard_for_city; knowledge “城里亲戚给的地址只能提供一张床，工作还要自己去找”_

- **1917.10** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-part-cash-part-furniture` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你数出四十赫勒搁在秤盘上，说剩下的一克朗用那张旧折叠桌抵。老板撇了撇嘴，让伙计跟你上楼搬桌子。桌子腿有点晃，但木料还结实。邻居在楼道里搭了把手，账本上这一页只写了一行，没写利息。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买煤付的部分现钱（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser_

- **1917.10** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.rent-note/rent-note.pay-increase` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Franz Lechner把纸条压在糖罐下面，算了半天：两克朗正好是四天的面包钱。月底还是把涨后的房租数出来交给看门人，铜币在桌上摞成两小堆。那一周的晚饭改成面包和稀汤，猪油省着抹。
  _effects: cash -2 (按涨租纸条多付的两克朗（其中2克朗记入债务）); food_

- **1917.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Franz Lechner把通知念给Hermann Kral听，Hermann Kral摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Franz Lechner去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1917.11** `r1-cul.bookshop::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/found-the-io-u` tags: culture, bookshop, debt, discovery, censorship, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Franz Lechner在旧书摊翻到一本植物志，书脊里夹着一张借条，借款人是Hedwig Stein的父亲。Hedwig Stein看了半天，说这本植物志原本就属于这家书店，被人借走后再没回来。Franz Lechner把书买下，借条留给Hedwig Stein，自己只带走书。
  _effects: cash -3 (书脊里的借条（其中3克朗记入债务）); mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +found_borrowed_book; relation friend with person-hedwig-stein; knowledge “the borrower was the bookseller’s own father”_

- **1918.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-born-at-home` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Berta Hofer在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg生下一个孩子，取名Berta Lechner。Franz Lechner在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Franz Lechner从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.029999999999999784 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); role; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:22_

- **1918.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.got-ticket` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Franz Lechner挤到窗口前，把一克朗硬币推进凹槽，换回一张印着座号的薄纸片。顶层站席的栏杆被前面的人汗湿，Franz Lechner侧身找了个能看见舞台边的位置，倚着烫手的铁栏看完两幕；散场时小腿发麻，嘴里还哼着刚听到的曲调，幕间也和邻座搭上了两句话。
  _effects: cash -1 (约瑟夫施塔特剧院顶层站票); mood 0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1918.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Hermann Kral在门口替Franz Lechner捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Franz Lechner的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Franz Lechner看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000669_

- **1919.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/violets-on-the-step` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  话在喉咙里滚了几遍，终究没有等到对方进门。门一响，有人就把紫罗兰放在门槛边，转身往街角走。花被早晨的扫帚拨到墙根，对方捡起来闻了闻，追到巷口只看见一个拐弯的背影，午后托人捎来一句话，说钱的事再算。
  _effects: cash -0.24 (一束紫罗兰); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; mood -0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1919.06** `p10-work-career.first-position/p10-work-career.first-position.turned-away` tags: adulthood, work, career, wages, injury, pension, employment, first_job
  名额给了排在前面的人。Franz Lechner在厂门口等到下班，汽笛响过，铁门拉上，他把另一家厂子的招工日期记在纸条背面。Hedwig Stein从职业介绍所出来，说下星期三还有一处招人，让他早点去排队。
  _effects: knowledge “下星期三早上七点，另一家机器厂在职业介绍所登记招上油工”; mood -0.06_

- **1919.07** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Franz Lechner说这个月先欠着，等发工钱补上。Rosa Lechner在名字旁画了个小圈，写明欠两周，叮嘱别拖过月底，否则要重新排进新会员的等待期。她走时把楼道的门带严，说冷风灌进来煤烧得快。Franz Lechner回头数了数抽屉里的零钱，还差三个赫勒。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1919.11** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/home-remedy` tags: health, family, home
  邻居过来看了一眼，教Franz Lechner用湿布敷额。Rosa天亮时安静睡了，Franz Lechner却耽误半日活计；一克朗五十赫勒的工钱没了，床边的醋味却留了一整天。
  _effects: health 0.05 (家庭护理后烧略退); cash -1.5 (缺工半日被扣工钱)_

- **1920.01** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Anna Moser 2用膝盖顶住，Franz Lechner把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1920.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Franz Lechner把Grete Lechner的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Grete Lechner今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Franz Lechner听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1920.01** `kernel-crisis.task-rescue-neighbor::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/rescue-betrayed` tags: task, rescue, fire, neighbor, betrayal, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Franz Lechner把Emil Weiss从后窗接下来，藏进自家地窖。第二天，Emil Weiss却在巡警面前说是Franz Lechner先点的火；Franz Lechner被带走问了一夜，回来时地窖门开着，里面的煤少了一半。门锁却没有撬痕，像是有人用钥匙开过。地窖的锁也换了。
  _effects: role; flag +betrayed_after_rescue; cash -6 (lost coal, a broken cellar lock and a night of questioning); knowledge “被救的人可能为了自保说火是你点的；问一夜放回来，地窖里的煤也会少一半。”_

- **1920.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Grete Lechner这周主动擦了黑板。Franz Lechner把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 31; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001176_

- **1920.04** `ml18-luck.strongbox-in-the-wall::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-inflation-ate-them` tags: money, luck, housing, windfall, inflation, ownership, rural, agriculture
  铁盒里的纸币已经作废，银扣也只值几个铜板。Emil Hofer把纸币一张张摊在窗台上，说战前的纸现在只能糊墙；Anna Moser笑得直不起腰，说这盒子在他家墙里藏了二十年，原来是一堆废纸。Franz Lechner把当票收好，去当铺换回一只银勺，勺柄上刻着外祖母的姓。
  _effects: cash 2 (the silver spoon redeemed with the old pawn ticket); relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; flag +worthless_strongbox; knowledge “墙里的旧纸币已经作废，当票还能换回一只银勺。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1920.08** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Berta Hofer把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Franz Lechner留在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Franz Lechner在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods); mood -0.06; role_

- **1920.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Franz Lechner说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Anna Moser一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-anna-moser_

- **1921.01** `task-vienna-bourgeois-youth.concert-outing/balcony-bread` tags: bourgeois, education, culture, salon, university, family, task, task:culture
  散场时人挤着下楼，Franz Lechner把票根揣好，说下回还来。Ferdinand Hofer笑他票根留着有什么用，他说夹进书里就知道听过什么。
  _effects: cash -4 (两张楼座票); mood 0.09000000000000002; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; skill_

- **1921.11** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/report-to-official` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Franz Lechner把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Franz Lechner自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.02_

- **1922.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.buy-for-elder` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Franz Lechner替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Franz Lechner自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.04; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1922.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Franz Lechner傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Alois Lechner 2在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Alois Lechner 2敲开Franz Lechner的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; mood 0.04999999999999999_

  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把Franz Lechner的证明信从头看到尾，最后说店里不缺人。Franz Lechner站起来时，椅子在地板上刮出一声长响；Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien送到门口，又补了一句“以后有机会”。街上正在下雨，Franz Lechner把证明信折成四折，塞进内袋。
  _effects: flag +interview_rejected; mood -0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (tram fare to the interview); relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; knowledge “面试后，店主说店里不缺人。”_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; reputation -0.03; mood -0.06_

- **1923.01** `r13-edu.seminar-presentation::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-university/freezes-at-podium` tags: education, university, seminar, presentation, rhetoric, medical, knowledge, student
  Franz Lechner站到讲台前，手心全是汗，第一句就念错了人名。后排有人笑出声，Franz Lechner越念越快，最后把结论吞回喉咙里。Grete Stein让Franz Lechner坐下，说下次把稿子背熟再来。第二次报告Franz Lechner把稿子背了七遍，站上台时还是先看了一眼门口，才开口。
  _effects: mood -0.12; skill_

- **1923.08** `kernel-health.mental-breakdown::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/mental-asylum-admission` tags: health, mental, exhaustion, asylum, crisis, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Franz Lechner在夜里被送进精神病院。登记表上写着“忧郁症”，随身物品只有一根皮带和一只鞋；病房里没有钟，吃饭靠铁门响。出院时医生嘱咐家属“别让他受刺激”。他把出院证明折成小方块，塞进鞋垫。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; condition; role; cash -20 (ward fees and lost work); knowledge “精神病院入院时收走皮带和鞋，病房里没有钟；出院嘱咐是别受刺激。”; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  _effects: mood -0.17999999999999994; flag +tagged_patient_expectant; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

  _effects: cash -2 (临时找住处与搬家的费用); relation rival with person-hermann-kral; reputation -0.04; flag +rent_book_taken; condition; mood -0.09999999999999998_

  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Franz Lechner胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; reputation -0.05000000000000001; flag +disputed_club_election; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-emil-weiss_

  _effects: health 0.039999999999999925 (邻居帮助后烧退); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1926.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; flag +union_debt_written_off; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  _effects: cash -1 (粘回去的搪瓷杯); flag +repaired_friendship; role; relation friend with person-emil-weiss; knowledge “the quarrel began with a lie about money”_

  Franz Lechner把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Emil Weiss借给Franz Lechner一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Franz Lechner睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。

  苹果堆里混着几个已经发软的，摊主用围裙兜着往她篮子里倒，说拿回去喂猪也不亏。Franz Lechner在井边洗了半篮子，削皮时看见果核边有一圈褐色的痕，刀子挖下去，甜味还是冒出来。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (买收摊前的碰伤苹果); food_

  Franz Lechner把湿布拧到不再滴水，敷在Grete Lechner的额头上。孩子烧得说胡话，Franz Lechner就一遍遍念窗外的街名，直到天快亮时，孩子的手心终于出了一层汗。Franz Lechner把被子掖好，自己靠在床沿睡了过去，醒来时锅里还剩半碗冷粥。
  _effects: cash -2 (第三遍换湿布); condition; flag +nursed_child_fever; health 0.05999999999999999 (the fever broke after a night of cold compresses)_

  Franz Lechner在约瑟夫城租下一间带钢琴的会客室，每小时一克朗，女房东把钥匙拴在一截蓝线上。Maria Lechner 2来时带着一束用报纸包好的紫罗兰，Emil Weiss坐在窗边打毛线，偶尔抬头看一眼钟。第三个星期日，Emil Weiss说自己要去楼下买咖啡，把门留了半掩。

  Franz Lechner把那段关系继续藏在日常的缝隙里。一次擦肩、一张没有署名的纸条、借来的半个钟头，都足够撑过这个月；回家前Franz Lechner在街角站了一会儿，把围巾重新系好。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 5 -> 0; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.reciprocity 50 -> 53; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1930.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/settle-the-account` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  两个星期不来往，门口碰见时Franz Lechner和Franz Winter都先看别处。这次Franz Lechner先开口，把账本里的一页撕下来摊在柜台上，说数字是自己写错的；Franz Winter看了半天，说下回别在酒馆里算账。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一小束紫罗兰和两只 Semmel); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; mood 0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1930.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/nod-and-pots` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  汤喝到一半，Rosa Lechner放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Berta Lechner 2在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Franz Lechner，Rosa Lechner把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1930.10** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Franz Lechner正要把空床许给Otto Winter，Emil Weiss却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Franz Lechner回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Otto Winter，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

  Emil Hofer在家长会上摊开新课本，说从明年起，小学四年改成八年，女孩也要上完。Alois Stein 2在门口记笔记，Emil Weiss在下面小声说家里缺人手。Franz Lechner问学费涨不涨，Emil Hofer说涨一点，但课本可以传给下一个孩子。
  _effects: cash -1 (新学制下的家长会); flag +attended_school_reform_meeting; role; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; knowledge “the new law extends primary school from four years to eight”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1930.11** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.wash-day/wash-day.listen-gossip` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, laundry, neighbor, household
  肥皂沫从Maria Lechner 2的指缝里冒出来，她说话时手里不停，衣领在搓板上蹭得吱吱响。Franz Lechner把湿被单搭上绳子，被单沉甸甸地坠下来，她踮脚才够到第二根竹竿。
  _effects: mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-born-at-home` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Berta Lechner 2在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个孩子，取名Mitzi Lechner。Franz Lechner在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Franz Lechner从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:233_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 20; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled false -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash -1 (the first school books and a slate); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1931.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/cupboard-again` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Franz Lechner刚提一句“那年分东西”，Mitzi Lechner就把勺子放下，说柜子是他先看中的，凭什么让。话越说越硬，汤还剩半碗，Mitzi Lechner起身拿了帽子，椅子在石地上刮出一声。Franz Lechner一个人坐到教堂的钟敲完，把两份饭钱都付了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; cash -0.4 (付了两份饭钱); mood -0.05999999999999994_

  Emil在办事处说了Franz Lechner一整个下午的坏话，从欠债说到那场没人看见的火灾。旁边的人没有替Franz Lechner说一句话。几天后，Franz Lechner发现自己的名字从互助会的名单上被划掉了，Emil只说“我也要养家”。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

- **1931.11** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Franz Lechner觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Anna Moser 2在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

  Grete Stein问Franz Lechner，Berta Lechner最近为什么总在课上打瞌睡。Franz Lechner说家里晚上在赶一批活；Grete Stein点点头，说“别让孩子熬太晚”。Franz Lechner应了一声，心里却在算那批活还能不能按时交。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 30; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +teacher_asked_homework_

  Franz Lechner在神学院的入学考试上默写拉丁文诗篇，写到第三段时忘了词，便按意思补了一句。Grete Stein在卷边批了“非原文”，仍让Franz Lechner通过。入学第一年，Franz Lechner每天清晨在冷得像冰窖的礼拜堂里念经，手指冻得翻不开书页，嘴里却一字不差。
  _effects: flag +seminary_admitted; role; education higher_

- **1932.04** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/call-doctor` tags: health, family, home
  夜里Emil Lechner哭醒，Franz Lechner独自用湿布敷额，熬到街上有人扫雪才去请医生。医生进门时靴底带着雪泥，听完胸口说无大碍；那三克朗花得心疼，却换回一条稳下来的呼吸。
  _effects: cash -3 (支付医生诊费和药粉); mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

  Franz Lechner刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Helene Bauer一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Helene Bauer的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

  _effects: reputation -0.08; relation rival with person-maria-kral; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴)_

- **1933.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Helene Bauer嫌Franz Lechner的床单滴水滴到Helene Bauer家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Franz Lechner下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Franz Lechner把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Helene Bauer那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

  Grete Lechner先开口，说老师问家里是不是有客人。Franz Lechner问怎么答的；Grete Lechner说“我说有，但客人睡在厨房”。Franz Lechner笑了一下，又觉得不该笑。那天晚上，Grete Lechner把厨房的门帘换成了新的。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 44; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +child_spoke_to_teacher_

  Franz Lechner押的那匹马在最后一个弯道超了过去。Alois Huber把十五克朗拍在柜台上，说“运气不错”。Franz Lechner请Hermann Stein喝了半升啤酒，剩下的钱在口袋里响了一路。第二天，Franz Lechner又去了赛马场。那匹马的号码被Franz Lechner写在烟盒上，后来烟盒被雨淋烂了，号码还认得出。回家后，Franz Lechner把那张湿透的烟盒摊在炉边烘干。
  _effects: cash 15 (第三个栏架前的马); mood 0.20000000000000007; condition; flag +gambling_winnings; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

  Franz Lechner在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Grete Weiss却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Franz Lechner把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

  Franz Lechner在《新维也纳日报》的失物栏里登了三行小字，花了四十个铜板。第二天，一个卖旧货的人说在电车终点站见过那样的铜牌。Emil Lechner放学后跟着Franz Lechner去找，一路上把书包带绕在手指上，绕得发白。

  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +rehearsal_broken_up_by_landlord; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

  _effects: condition; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1936.01** `p9-history-medical-daily.patient-family/dispute-bill` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily, injury
  Franz Lechner把费用单摊在长椅上，一项项问Berta Bauer 2。医生用指节敲了敲纸面，说这是医院定的；最后Franz Lechner只付了药费三克朗五十赫勒，Alois Lechner留在观察室，护士给了一杯水。
  _effects: cash -3.5 (只付药费和部分材料费（其中3.5克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.020000000000000018; health -0.02 (押金未交齐，治疗暂缓); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Franz Lechner忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Franz Lechner听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 77 -> 69_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/a-receipt-in-the-lining` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  半年过去，Franz Lechner又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 10 -> 16; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 55 -> 53; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `r13-fam.join-club::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::club-crisis/turned-away-for-safety` tags: task, social, club, choir, organization, belonging, vienna, urban
  Ferdinand Bauer在门口拦住Franz Lechner，说这阵子别来了，名单上多一个名字就多一份麻烦。Franz Lechner把歌本还回去，走到街角又回头，看见Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的窗户用报纸糊了一半，里面还有人低声唱。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +kept_off_choir_roll_for_safety; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; knowledge “the choir is being watched”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

- **1936.06** `af17-luck.wallet-in-the-snow::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/kept-the-wallet` tags: luck, money, street, chance, irony, daily, vienna, urban
  Franz Lechner把钱包塞进内袋，走了两条街才敢回头看。三克朗够买一周的煤，彩票也许什么都不是；但那天晚上，Franz Lechner把彩票压在盐罐底下，像压着一张还没有兑现的明天。
  _effects: cash 3 (the money in the lost wallet); state_add af17-lucky-idiot state:world-vienna-1912:453; flag +kept_the_snow_wallet; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +lucky_idiot; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Franz Lechner在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Franz Lechner把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007234_

- **1937.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-born-at-home` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Maria Lechner 2在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个孩子，取名Elisabeth Lechner。Franz Lechner在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Franz Lechner从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:493_

- **1937.01** `pc20-family.parent-mistake::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/cover` tags: parent, child, mistake, second_chance, family, school, repetition, intervention
  Franz Lechner替Elisabeth Lechner向Grete Stein请了病假，又把那件可能惹麻烦的东西藏进自己的工具箱。Grete Stein看了Franz Lechner一眼，说病假可以补，别的东西补不了；Elisabeth Lechner在里屋听见，门没有关严。
  _effects: state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:494; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000525:child; flag +covered_child_mistake; knowledge “病假可以补，别的东西补不了。”; flag +second_chance_open_

- **1937.01** `state_expire:hs18-street-witness` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Franz Lechner的「街面上的目击者」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:357_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Franz Lechner把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Franz Lechner忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 53 -> 61; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 16 -> 14; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/the-bill-comes-due` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  好运气没有出现。Franz Lechner错过电车，鞋跟断在环城大道的石缝里，回家才发现钥匙也丢了。邻居没有安慰，只说“总算轮到你了”，语气像在结一笔旧账。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 12 -> 11; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 25 -> 35; cash -1 (修鞋和配钥匙); health -0.010000000000000004 (在冻雨里走回家)_

- **1937.01** `r13-civ.join-choir::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/voice-rejected` tags: culture, choir, music, community, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Grete Stein听完，说音准还行，但声音太薄，撑不起礼拜天。Franz Lechner问能不能先站在最后一排，Grete Stein说等下次有空位。回家的路上，Franz Lechner在无人的巷子里把那首歌唱完。
  _effects: flag +choir_audition_rejected; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1937.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Lena把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Franz Lechner打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: health -0.04 (高烧硬熬伤身); relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1937.07** `r3-war.resistance-courier::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/received-patrol-times` tags: war, occupation, resistance, courier, checkpoint, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef Weiss 2在教堂后门等Franz Lechner，说下一个路口加了暗哨。Grete Stein把一张弥撒单塞给Franz Lechner，单子背面用铅笔写着巡逻时间；Franz Lechner把单子折好，夹进唱诗本。出门时，Alois Stein 2正站在台阶上抽烟，问Franz Lechner是不是来望弥撒的。
  _effects: flag +received_patrol_times_from_priest; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; relation friend with person-grete-stein; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; knowledge “the patrol schedule is written on a mass sheet”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -20 (escape and lodging（其中20克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Franz Lechner晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Franz Lechner听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 40 -> 33; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 50 -> 58; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 40 -> 43_

  舅父在里屋翻账簿半天没出来，舅母趁空从围裙里掏出一卷私房钱，数出十克朗塞给他，说到了家乡先给她写封信。他把钱收下，出门时在门口站了一会儿，才把帽子戴正。
  _effects: cash 10 (舅母私下给的路费); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1938.03** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/shifts-with-sibling` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Franz Lechner和Mitzi把值班时辰写在门后的粉笔板上：Mitzi守前半夜，Franz Lechner守后半夜，医生说静养二十一天。煤油灯芯拧小，药粉分成小包摆在床头，便盆换水时尽量不碰出响。二十一天熬下来，Franz Lechner的眼皮肿了，走路发飘，但Alois Lechner每次起身总有人扶。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; flag +night_care_duty_

- **1938.03** `state_reaction:af17-lucky-idiot/windfall-talk` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  咖啡馆里有人问Franz Lechner，是不是替哪个议员跑过腿，或者认识一个会看牌的女人。Franz Lechner说自己只是运气好；这句话说出口，连Franz Lechner自己都觉得像在遮掩。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 35 -> 47; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +luck_explained_as_a_deal_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008436_

- **1938.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Franz Lechner胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙)_

- **1938.11** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/take-in-a-lodger` tags: housing, home, health, family, injury
  四克朗放在桌上，Franz Lechner数了一遍，又添进房租的信封。孩子半夜翻来覆去，学徒在厨房里咳嗽；Franz Lechner睁着眼到天亮，眼下青了一圈。
  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); health -0.03 (overcrowded housing); condition_

- **1939.01** `bc18-luck.civic-mascot::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/mascot-union` tags: luck, charity, public, reputation, absurd, social, suspicion, vienna
  城里几个自称运气好的人凑在一起，成立了一个“幸运者互助会”，每人每月交二十赫勒，谁遇到倒霉事就领一笔小钱。Franz Lechner被推举为会长，因为Franz Lechner的运气最没有道理；第一次开会，七个人带来七种护身符，最后决定把会费锁在一只旧饼干盒里。
  _effects: cash 2 (幸运者互助会的会费结余); state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 57 -> 52; role; relation colleague with person-karl-kraus; skill; flag +founded_lucky_peoples_club; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/kept-word` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Franz Lechner把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Franz Lechner这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 33 -> 39; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 58 -> 53; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 40 -> 44; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Franz Lechner按目录去取卷宗，Alois Lechner翻了半天，说编号错了，那箱东西在十年前就移交给了别的部门。Franz Lechner在地下室待了一下午，出来时满身灰尘，咳嗽了三天，Franz Lechner把目录上的错号抄下来，准备下次再问。出门时天已经黑了，门房在打瞌睡。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.07** `ad20-former-employer::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-door-is-open` tags: work, employer, return, everyday, stigma, public, vienna, urban
  Leopoldstädter Volksschule在门口认出Franz Lechner，先问家里怎么样，又问这些年在哪。Franz Lechner说换了几份工；Leopoldstädter Volksschule点点头，说“有空来坐”。两个人都知道这不是一句工作邀请，但谁也没有把话挑明。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +old_employer_cordial; cash 1 (a small gift from the old employer)_

- **1939.10** `r3-war.ration-scale::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/kept-short-weight-tally` tags: war, ration, shortage, market, protest, vienna, urban, crisis
  Franz Lechner没有争，把少掉的面包钱记在墙上。一个月后，墙上画了二十道杠；Alois Moser 2说再画下去房东要涨租，Franz Lechner说那就画在门背后。Hermann Kral 2看见了，偷偷给Franz Lechner留了一小袋碎面，说别让Alois Stein 2看见。
  _effects: cash -2 (配给秤下的铅块); food; flag +kept_short_weight_tally; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

  Franz Lechner在包袱里看见一件工装，袖口绣着Mitzi的名字。第二天Mitzi来还顶针，说孩子病了，实在没法；Franz Lechner收下顶针，把毛毯也给了他。
  _effects: cash -6 (门锁被撬成了麻花（其中6克朗记入债务）); flag +recognized_thief_as_neighbor; role; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the burglar was the neighbour whose child is ill”_

  Franz Lechner把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Mitzi抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Franz Lechner把床单对折，腾出一半，Mitzi用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Franz Lechner把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Franz Lechner忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 67 -> 75; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 18 -> 16_

- **1940.01** `state_expire:af17-lucky-idiot` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  Franz Lechner的「走运的傻瓜」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove af17-lucky-idiot state:world-vienna-1912:453; flag -lucky_idiot_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/kept-word` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Franz Lechner把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Franz Lechner这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 39 -> 45; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 53 -> 48; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 44 -> 48; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `kernel-culture.radio-broadcast::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/civic-radio-cut` tags: culture, radio, broadcast, writing, reform, vienna, urban, crisis
  稿子被Hermann Stein删得只剩三行，他问能不能加一句失业救济的电话。Hermann Stein说“上面不让”；他还是把那句写在手心，直播时没有念出来。他把那行字在手心攥出了汗，回家洗手时才发现墨迹已经晕开。
  _effects: flag +radio_script_cut; reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; knowledge “广播稿不能提失业救济电话，编辑会提前删掉”; cash 2 (短稿的稿费); health -0.010000000000000009 (把想说的话咽回去)_

- **1940.03** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Franz Lechner把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Berta Weiss替Franz Lechner扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss_

- **1940.11** `hs18-occupation.piano::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/refuse-to-sign` tags: occupation, piano, requisition, witness, neighbor, history, vienna, urban
  Franz Lechner把笔推回去，说钢琴的主人就住在隔壁。Alois Stein 2没有争，只让工人把琴盖也搬走，又记下Franz Lechner的门牌号。卡车开走时，Hermann Kral 2家的窗子开了又关；Franz Lechner站在空出来的门洞前，听见自己的呼吸声。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:619; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; reputation 0.020000000000000018; flag +refused_piano_receipt; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `pc20-family.parent-mistake::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cover` tags: parent, child, mistake, second_chance, family, school, repetition, intervention
  Franz Lechner替Grete Lechner向Grete Stein请了病假，又把那件可能惹麻烦的东西藏进自己的工具箱。Grete Stein看了Franz Lechner一眼，说病假可以补，别的东西补不了；Grete Lechner在里屋听见，门没有关严。
  _effects: state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:660; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; knowledge “病假可以补，别的东西补不了。”_

- **1941.01** `state_expire:sm18-missing-object` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Franz Lechner的「一件说不清下落的东西」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:402; flag -something_is_missing_

- **1941.01** `state_expire:pc20-second-chance` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Franz Lechner的「第二次机会」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:494; flag -second_chance_open_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Franz Lechner忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Franz Lechner听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 90 -> 82_

- **1941.01** `task-vienna-family-care.ask-relative-money/loan-with-schedule` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Gustav Berg 3听Franz Lechner把房租和药费的数目报完，从抽屉里数出二十克朗，推到桌上，同时拿出一张纸，要写还款日期。他说每月十五号来一趟，把账报一遍。Franz Lechner签字时手有点抖，把纸角压得很平，说好先从月底还三克朗。
  _effects: cash 20 (舅舅借出的钱); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; flag +owes_uncle_

- **1941.03** `r2-leisure.mourning-dress::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/finished-the-mourning-dress` tags: leisure, dressmaker, mourning, grief, craft, vienna, urban, crisis
  寡妇的丧服不能有亮边，也不能露出一点旧色。Gustav Lechner站在镜子前，说腰要松，她这几天吃不下东西。Franz Lechner把最后一针藏在褶子里，说黑绸会记住人的形状。Luise Huber在门口送来一篮炭，说葬礼在明天下午，别误了时辰。Franz Lechner把裙子叠好，纸包里放了一小枝迷迭香。
  _effects: cash 6 (黑绸裙的最后一针); flag +finished_mourning_dress; role; skill; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000844:child_

- **1941.03** `oj18-taxi.night-fare::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/coop-keys` tags: taxi, night, driver, passenger, work, odd_job, everyday, vienna
  合作社的调度Grete Lechner 2把一枚铜牌和一把车钥匙推过柜台，说夜班只跑环城路，十一点以后不接去普拉特的车。Franz Lechner把铜牌挂在后视镜上，第一晚就拉了七个客人，最后一个在车上睡着，打鼾像一台小马达。
  _effects: organization_found 第二区夜班出租合作社 (other); position 夜班司机 @taxi:person-franz-lechner-evt:world-vienna-1912:010278; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:661; cash 2 (night taxi fares); flag +taxi_night_shift; knowledge “夜班只跑环城路，十一点以后不接去普拉特的车。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1941.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Gustav Kral在门口替Franz Lechner捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

- **1942.01** `hs18-witness.reporter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refuse-and-warn` tags: witness, reporter, memory, money, risk, history, vienna, urban
  Franz Lechner把钞票推回去，说那个人还住在这条街上。Emil Lechner 3收起本子，问是不是有人威胁。Franz Lechner说没有，只是不想在下一个门洞里再认出谁。出门时，Franz Lechner绕到Gustav Lechner家，把记者来过的事说了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 60 -> 70; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.retellings 0 -> 1; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000844:child; reputation -0.010000000000000009; flag +refused_witness_payment_

- **1942.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Franz Lechner听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 18; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 27; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011072_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Franz Lechner又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 82 -> 84_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/small-relapse` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Franz Lechner晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Franz Lechner听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 40 -> 33; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 50 -> 58; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 40 -> 43; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Franz Lechner的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Franz Lechner想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 27 -> 11; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1942.01** `r13-civ.soup-kitchen::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::r13-civ.soup-kitchen-crisis/crisis-doubled-queue` tags: social, charity, soup_kitchen, hunger, volunteer, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa把搪瓷缸伸过来，说已经两天没找到活。Franz Lechner把勺子沉到底，捞了稠的给他，后面立刻有人喊不公平。Anna Bauer 2把锅盖一扣，说再吵就都别吃。队伍安静下来，只剩勺子碰缸的声音。
  _effects: reputation 0.08000000000000002; flag +soup_kitchen_crisis_shift; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94_

- **1942.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  老工人凑近看那张表，说这纸比工资袋还薄。Franz Lechner把拇指蘸进印泥，按下去时听见工头说了句“下一个”。学徒在旁边数少掉的铜板，数到四十赫勒时不出声了。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

- **1942.04** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/wrong-coal-delivery` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, vienna
  Josef Moser把二十袋“劣质煤”送到Franz Lechner门口，货到付款，想看Franz Lechner拿不出钱。卸货时，煤块黑得发亮，是上好的无烟煤；Franz Lechner付了钱，转手卖给面包房，净赚五克朗。Josef Moser来打听煤的去向，Franz Lechner说已经烧了，还谢谢他记得自己怕冷。
  _effects: cash 5 (转卖无烟煤的差价); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; condition; flag +enemy_coal_was_anthracite; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1942.05** `ad20-neighbor-dispute::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-line-moves` tags: neighbor, dispute, home, everyday, public, obligation, vienna, urban
  隔壁把晾衣绳往Franz Lechner这边挪了一尺，水滴正好落在窗台上。Franz Lechner拿粉笔在墙上画了一道线；Maria Berg 4看见了，又把绳子挪回去半尺。两个人都觉得自己让了步，晚饭时却都把窗户关得很紧。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +laundry_line_dispute_

- **1943.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/nothing-moved` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  账本看上去是平的：修屋顶的钱被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。Franz Lechner在每一栏都签了字，知道明年还会这样。散会后，Elisabeth Bauer把一张催修屋顶的纸条压在台灯下面。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.010000000000000009; flag +deferred_maintenance; knowledge “屋顶的修理费被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。”_

- **1943.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Franz Lechner走到Berggasse 19, Wohnung und Ordination门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1943.02** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/fever-hand` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, vienna
  一连三天的高烧退下去以后，Franz Lechner的手腕上多了一块暗褐色的印子，边缘像五根很短的手指。诊所的医生说只是皮下色素，Maria Lechner 2却在院子里看了很久，说这形状不是随便长的。Franz Lechner把袖子拉下来，回家路上一直用另一只手按着那块皮。
  _effects: cash -1 (去诊所问这块印子（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:778; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_fever; flag +df18_mark_noticed; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1943.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Franz Lechner看Berta Bauer 2盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Berta Bauer 2愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Franz Lechner门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Franz Lechner打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; food_

- **1943.08** `r2-rural.hailstorm::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/repaired-the-roof` tags: rural, weather, neighbor, repair, vineyard, vienna, urban, crisis
  雹停后，屋顶漏了三处。Franz Lechner爬上梯子铺油毡，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在檐口。Alois Lechner在下面扶着梯子骂了两句，又去烧热水。修屋顶的木板和钉子花了九克朗，账记在欠条上。
  _effects: cash -9 (五月的冰雹（其中9克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +repaired_hail_damage_

- **1944.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/motion-fails` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  会议室里有人念了一份动议，说Franz Lechner把预算当成人情。Elisabeth Bauer把签名页传了一圈，最后停在自己手里：支持票比反对票少三张。Franz Lechner没有发言，只把下一季度的支出表放在桌上，表上每一笔都有收据。
  _effects: reputation 0.04000000000000001; relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +survived_removal_motion; knowledge “动议只拿到少数支持，三名原本签名的委员在投票前撤回了。”; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

  Franz Lechner的「奇职学徒」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:661; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Franz Lechner在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Franz Lechner低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 25 -> 35; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 5 -> 11; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed illness -> curse; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1944.01** `r13-civ.help-refugee::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sent-to-relief-office` tags: social, refugee, shelter, war, trust, vienna, urban, crisis
  Franz Lechner没有让Alois Moser 2进门，只把救济站的地址写在烟盒背面，又画了怎么绕过岗亭。Alois Moser 2把烟盒攥在手里，鞠了一躬。第二天，走廊的墙上多了一张用铅笔写的谢谢。

Born 1867.07, died 1933.01 (age 66).

  Maria Kral只把外套的里衬拆了一半，就搁在椅背上。冬天天短，她每天回来都看见那件敞着口子的外套，想着明天接着弄，明天又总是有别的事。到开春外套还搭在那儿，呢面上落了一层灰。
  _effects: mood -0.03; flag +unfinished_coat_

- **1917.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.read-all` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Maria Kral把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Maria Kral折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

  提问时，Maria Kral 问Karl Kraus 是否也会嘲笑普通读者的迟钝。Karl Kraus 盯着Maria Kral 看了几秒，说“我先嘲笑自己，再嘲笑你们”；台下有人笑，也有人起身。Maria Kral 没有等到散场，只把没喝完的咖啡钱压在杯底。

- **1915.01** `task-vienna-daily-culture.cinema-concert/cinema-concert.neighbor-pays` tags: elderhood, daily, culture, cafe, church, market, task, task:culture
  售票员已经不耐烦地敲窗框了，Theresia Huber的硬币却怎么也不够。Anna Moser叹了口气替他付了，他嘴里说下个月一定还，回家路上却一直低头踢着人行道上的碎石子。
  _effects: relation friend with person-anna-moser; mood -0.03_

- **1916.01** `p9-gap-elderhood-care.join-aid/aid-joined` tags: elderhood, care, pension, housing, task, task:social
  Theresia Huber在圣坛边的长椅上等Grete Huber写完册子，交了第一季的会费一克朗二十赫勒。她的名字被写进那本厚册，旁边注着门牌号。回家走的是巷子，风从教堂门口灌过来，她把围巾掖紧了，脚步比来时稳。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (缴纳互助会第一季会费); mood 0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1916.11** `vienna-life-old-age/family-care` tags: elderhood, pension, health
  邻居每天来看Theresia Huber，带来面包和报纸。Theresia Huber坐在窗边读讣告，认出几个旧同事的名字，把报纸折好放在桌上。
  _effects: cash -2 (bread and newspaper); relation friend with person-anna-moser_

- **1917.01** `p9-gap-elderhood-care.pension-appeal/pension-appeal-two-trips` tags: elderhood, care, pension, housing, task, task:daily
  第一次去，窗口说管档案的人不在，让她改天再来。Theresia Huber隔了四天又跑一趟，这回Emil Weiss陪她，Hedwig Stein才收下身份纸和邻居写的证明，说工龄要核，补发的钱月底汇到。她来回走了两趟，鞋底磨薄了一层。
  _effects: cash -1 (两趟往返的车钱与手续费); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-elderhood-care.ask-neighbor-repair/stove-repaired-free` tags: elderhood, care, pension, housing, task, task:daily
  楼下在铁工厂干活的Emil Weiss傍晚拎着工具上来，蹲在炉前敲敲打打，换了一截铁皮烟道，把炉门铰链紧好。他要走时，Theresia Huber把那锅汤热了端给他，他坐在门槛上喝完，没收一个赫勒。
  _effects: health 0.020000000000000018 (炉子修好，不再倒烟呛咳); food_

- **1918.12** `p9-gap-elderhood-daily.old-colleague-funeral/p9-gap-elderhood-daily.old-colleague-funeral.stood-long` tags: elderhood, daily, pension, housing, care, funeral, colleague, memory
  葬礼在城外公墓，Theresia Huber穿着袖口磨亮的黑外套，在墓前站得比旁人久，靴底让冻土冰得发麻。回来的电车上她把旧工资册翻开，一页页看那些签名和日期，邻座的Hermann Kral问起，她才说起那同事当年替她顶过一班夜工。
  _effects: mood -0.06; health -0.020000000000000018 (冻土上久站，脚趾发木); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-elderhood-care.give-tools/tools-given-all` tags: elderhood, care, pension, housing, task, task:social
  Theresia Huber把柜底的刨子、凿子和小锯擦干净，用一块旧布包好，交给新搬来的Marie Huber。年轻人当场试了试刨子，刨出一卷薄木花，说比作坊里的好用。她把空出来的柜底又摸了摸，才关上柜门。
  _effects: mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1919.07** `p9-gap-elderhood-daily.pension-window/p9-gap-elderhood-daily.pension-window.recovered` tags: elderhood, daily, pension, housing, care, bureaucracy, cash
  Elisabeth Stein从后面柜子回来，把册子摊在窗台上，用铅笔在Theresia Huber名字旁添了一道小线，说下次别再缺页。Hermann Kral在旁边点了点头，没再说什么。
  _effects: cash 8 (补发少算的六个月养老金差额); mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-elderhood-care.ask-neighbor-repair/stove-needs-part` tags: elderhood, care, pension, housing, task, task:daily
  Emil Weiss看过炉门，说铰链座子裂了，得换零件，今天先别生火。他第二天从厂里带了一小片铁板回来，锉成座子装上，收了她二十赫勒当材料钱。Theresia Huber当晚把炉子生起来，烟是直的。
  _effects: cash -20 (付给邻居的零件材料钱)_

- **1920.12** `vienna-elder-care.winter-stairs/ask-neighbor-to-buy-bread` tags: elderhood, health, family, social
  Theresia Huber在楼梯口叫住Emil Weiss，托她代买一条黑面包和半升牛奶。
  _effects: cash -2 (托邻居代买的食物); food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1921.01** `task-vienna-daily-culture.neighborhood-rumor/neighborhood-rumor.spread-further` tags: elderhood, daily, culture, cafe, church, market, task, task:social
  面包店柜台前排队的人伸着脖子听，Theresia Huber压低声音把楼道里的话讲了一遍，末尾还加了一句“我早觉得不对劲”。面粉的气味里，有人点头，有人只是把硬币往前推。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; reputation -0.06; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1922.01** `task-vienna-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/cafe-newspaper.share-and-argue` tags: elderhood, daily, culture, cafe, church, market, task, task:daily
  他把报纸折成两半，指着议会版上煤气税那一段念出声。邻桌的Hermann Kral凑过来，手指点着数字说算错了，两个人把杯子推到一边，差点打翻糖罐。
  _effects: cash -3 (两杯咖啡和一份报纸的钱); mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1923.01** `task-vienna-daily-culture.cinema-concert/cinema-concert.neighbor-pays` tags: elderhood, daily, culture, cafe, church, market, task, task:culture
  他把硬币一枚枚排在售票窗台上，数到第三遍还是差三赫勒。Hermann Kral从后面伸手把铜板推过去，说算我请你看。Theresia Huber把找零捏在手心，整场戏都没松开。
  _effects: relation friend with person-hermann-kral; mood -0.03_

- **1924.01** `task-vienna-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/cafe-newspaper.buy-paper-for-neighbor` tags: elderhood, daily, culture, cafe, church, market, task, task:daily
  报亭的帘子被风吹得啪啪响，Theresia Huber等摊主找零时跺了跺脚。等他回到咖啡馆，侍者已经把杯子收进托盘，Hermann Kral把报纸接过去，翻到讣告版先看了起来。
  _effects: cash -4 (替邻居买报又重叫了一杯咖啡); mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1925.01** `task-vienna-daily-culture.cinema-concert/cinema-concert.top-balcony` tags: elderhood, daily, culture, cafe, church, market, task, task:culture
  楼座的木头椅子硌得他后背发酸，可铜管一响他就忘了。散场灯亮时，他还坐着看空舞台，直到Alois Lechner 2从下面喊他，才慢慢扶栏杆站起来。
  _effects: cash -5 (楼座票和路上买的热栗子); mood 0.07999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1925.10** `vienna-elder-care.winter-stairs/stay-indoors` tags: elderhood, health, family, social, injury
  Theresia Huber看了看结冰的台阶，退回屋里，用剩下的面粉和水糊了一顿面糊。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04000000000000001; health -0.020000000000000018 (缺乏新鲜食物)_

- **1926.01** `p9-gap-elderhood-care.join-aid/aid-neighbor-paid` tags: elderhood, care, pension, housing, task, task:social
  会费凑不齐，Theresia Huber在教堂门口犹豫半晌。住楼上的Mitzi Berg看见了，替她垫了头一个月四十赫勒，说等她宽裕了再还。Theresia Huber在册子上签了名，回家把欠的钱记在日历背面，用红铅笔圈起来。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (邻居替她垫付的会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1926.05** `eh19-bridge.question-arrives::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/ask-a-neighbor-first` tags: elderhood, testimony, history, memory, bureaucracy, ordinary, reform, medical
  Theresia Huber没有立刻填，拿着问话单去敲隔壁的门。开门的人比她年轻二十岁，说那年还没搬来；两个人站在楼道里把纸看了一遍，最后只确认了门牌号。回家后，Theresia Huber把问话单压在糖罐下面。
  _effects: state_add eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:99; flag +late_testimony_neighbor; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +late_testimony_active_

- **1926.06** `sm18-legend.well-light::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-light-comes-back` tags: legend, well, light, neighborhood, uncanny, unresolved, reform, medical
  Theresia Huber把镜片扔掉以后，第二个星期井口又亮了。这次碎的是另一块，边缘还带着新茬。Mitzi Berg说别再去了；Theresia Huber站在院门口看了很久，直到那点白光被雾吞掉。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; knowledge “井口又出现了一块新的碎镜片，边缘带着新茬”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; flag +the_well_light_came_back; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:eh19-late-testimony/version-drift/someone-coaches` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  一个自称旧同事的人来家里，把一页写好的说法留在桌上，说这样对大家都好。Theresia Huber没有答应，也没有把纸扔掉；夜里，那张纸压在茶杯底下，边角被水汽卷了起来。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 20 -> 28; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 35 -> 29; knowledge “有人希望我把日期说成一九三八年，而不是一九三四年。”; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1927.01** `task-vienna-daily-culture.cinema-concert/cinema-concert.cover-ears` tags: elderhood, daily, culture, cafe, church, market, task, task:culture
  小号拔高的那一下，Theresia Huber觉得耳膜被人用指头弹了。他低头从座位间挤出去，楼梯间的冷空气才让太阳穴慢慢松开，楼下检票的人看了他一眼，没说话。
  _effects: cash -5 (没听完的楼座票); health -0.040000000000000036 (铜管乐太响引起头痛); mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1927.05** `kernel-health.chronic-pain::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pain-managed-at-home` tags: health, pain, elderhood, morphine, household, reform, medical, schooling
  Theresia Huber把楼梯改成一步一步上，买菜只走平路，疼得厉害就坐下来揉。Emil Weiss替Theresia Huber把煤筐拎上楼，换来一碗汤和一张旧报纸；两个人谁也没提医院。邻居把空筐靠在门边，顺手把楼梯上的冰敲掉。
  _effects: cash -4 (a cane, a hot-water bottle and tram fares to the market); health -0.010000000000000009 (chronic pain managed by rest and warmth); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; knowledge “膝盖疼的时候走平路、分几步上楼、用热水袋，比去医院的排队更管用。”_

- **1928.01** `pc20-testimony.retraction::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/retract` tags: elderhood, testimony, court, retraction, public_reversal, memory, witness, politics
  Theresia Huber在法庭上把上一次的日期改了回来，说那天先响的是门，不是窗。Mitzi Moser 2让他确认，Theresia Huber说确认；Emil Weiss在旁听席上低下头，手指在膝盖上敲了两下，像在数一件只有他记得的事。
  _effects: state_add pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:159; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 29 -> 14; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 28 -> 38; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; flag +retracted_testimony; knowledge “那天先响的是门，不是窗；{subject}在法庭上把日期改了回来。”; flag +public_reversal_active_

  _effects: state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.support 30 -> 37; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.proof 30 -> 34; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1930.04** `kernel-migrate.retirement::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/civic-retirement-papers` tags: work, retirement, pension, elderhood, postwar, reform, medical, schooling
  她去市政厅问养老金，窗口让她先补交三年的保险。她拿着表格回到厂里，Katharina Weiss 2帮她找出旧工资单；两个人蹲在更衣室里数到天黑，发现还差两个月。她把缺的两个月记在日历上，每天翻一页。
  _effects: flag +pension_application_started; knowledge “养老金申请要先补交三年保险，旧工资单还差两个月”; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; cash -1 (补交保险表格和复印费); health -0.010000000000000009 (蹲在更衣室数单据后的腰酸); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1930.12** `p9-gap-elder-solitude.gramophone/gramophone-both-sides` tags: elderhood, daily, solitude, memory, home, music, gramophone
  她听完一面，又摇了一次柄，把另一面也听完。唱针划过末尾的沙沙声里，她给自己倒了半杯热水，坐在椅子上没动，等唱片自己转停。
  _effects: mood 0.06_

  _effects: state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.proof 18 -> 10; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.danger 57 -> 63; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.pressure 70 -> 75; mood -0.03_

  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 35 -> 38; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 6 -> 4; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 52 -> 54; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:253; state_add fame state:world-vienna-1912:254; cash 1 (the radio appearance fee); reputation 0.04; flag +radio_impostor; mood 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; flag +late_bloom_active_

### 143 subject events

- **1924.06** `vienna-history-kraus-lecture/question-the-reader` tags: history, culture, karl_kraus, cafe_central, public_lecture, press
  提问时，Hermann Stein 问Karl Kraus 是否也会嘲笑普通读者的迟钝。Karl Kraus 盯着Hermann Stein 看了几秒，说“我先嘲笑自己，再嘲笑你们”；台下有人笑，也有人起身。Hermann Stein 没有等到散场，只把没喝完的咖啡钱压在杯底。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; knowledge “Kraus 的公开朗读既是文学批评，也是当场点名新闻界的表演；被点名的人可能在场。”; cash -2 (coffee and admission to the reading); reputation -0.01_

- **1924.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  夜里疼醒时，Hermann Stein摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Anna Moser 4第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Hermann Stein只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); health -0.020000000000000018 (牙病拖延未治); condition_

- **1925.06** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/denounced-to-police` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership, unemployment
  旅店伙计记下了Hermann Stein和Franz Winter的名字。几天后警察来敲门，把两人带去问话；Hermann Stein丢了工位，罚了八克朗，Franz Winter的家里把人接走。Hermann Stein在档案里留下一条警察记录，手指上还留着问话时磕出的淤青。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; reputation -0.2; unemployment (dismissed after police visit); cash -8 (fine and lost work); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  _effects: skill; flag +union_delegate_named; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  Hermann Stein摸了摸空口袋，说那不过是块旧铜，丢了就丢了。晚上，Gustav Lechner睡着以后，Hermann Stein又去电车站走了一趟，沿着白天的路线低头看每一块石板。

- **1928.01** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/second-bridge-promise` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  在第二个桥洞下，Ferdinand Kral先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Hermann Stein点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子); mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

  遗嘱里还附了一笔丧葬费，条件是要有“家属”到场。死者没有亲人，Hermann Stein只好穿黑衣站在墓旁，听牧师念一个从未听过的名字。雨把花圈上的纸花打湿，泥水漫过鞋面；葬礼结束后，Katharina Weiss把九克朗交给他，说账已结清。Hermann Stein回家脱鞋，发现袜子上沾着别人的土。
  _effects: cash 7 (以家属身份到场后的遗产余款); reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; condition; flag +buried_a_stranger_for_the_estate; mood -0.010000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  这一年，Hermann Stein学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Hermann Stein的材料齐全。年底，Hermann Stein从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。

  一个税务员把Hermann Stein的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Hermann Stein收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Hermann Stein先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。

  Hermann Stein的「被调查」到期；相关安排结束。

- **1930.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Hermann Stein去了Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus，坐在后排听完了关于维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:seed-vienna-social-democracy-1912 as participant; knowledge “我参加了维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济。。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.03** `bc18-ghost.pension-window::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/widow-for-a-day` tags: bureaucracy, pension, identity, legal, paperwork, absurd, poverty, reform
  窗口后的Friedrich Hofer说，死者不能领养老金，但遗属可以。邻居回家换了件黑裙，挽着Hermann Stein的胳膊又排了一次队；八克朗的补发款从铁栅栏下面推出来，两人在街角分成两份。Hermann Stein把属于自己的那份塞进鞋底，回家路上一直觉得那只鞋比另一只重。
  _effects: cash 4 (冒充遗属领到的补发养老金); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 32 -> 50; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; condition; flag +pension_collected_by_fake_widow; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  二楼的老木匠指着裂开的踏板骂起来。Hermann Stein手里还捏着念珠，Anna Huber 2挡在中间说木板本来就朽了。房东太太在门后听着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; reputation -0.03; mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1930.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hermann Stein把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Emil借给Hermann Stein一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Hermann Stein睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.06_

- **1931.01** `bc18-ghost.quiet-correction::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/keep-both-documents` tags: bureaucracy, paperwork, identity, absurd, legal, quiet, comic, reform
  Hermann Stein没有去销死亡证明，又去补了一张出生证明。此后办事，哪边队伍短就用哪张纸：领煤票用活人的，避开税务用死人的，给孩子报名用刚出生的。Mitzi Moser 2在档案里给Hermann Stein做了三个不同颜色的标签，说这样至少不容易拿错；Hermann Stein觉得，这是自己在官僚系统里得到过的最诚实的建议。
  _effects: cash 1 (用不同身份省下的配给差额); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 36 -> 41; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 60 -> 65; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.officialStatus 在户籍册上已死亡 -> 同时死亡与新生; flag +kept_both_certificates; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Hermann Stein把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Hermann Stein忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 16 -> 24; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 6 -> 4; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Hermann Stein学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Hermann Stein的材料齐全。年底，Hermann Stein从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 41 -> 45; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 65 -> 60; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1931.04** `vienna-history-schnitzler-theatre/speak-in-the-foyer` tags: history, culture, arthur_schnitzler, burgtheater, theatre
  Hermann Stein 在门廊里拦住Arthur Schnitzler，说第二幕的医生讲得太像真的。Arthur Schnitzler 停了一下，回答说他当过医生，知道那种语气；然后礼貌地告辞，没有留下地址。Hermann Stein 看着他走进雨里，才想起自己忘了问下一部戏什么时候上演。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-arthur-schnitzler; knowledge “Schnitzler 当过医生，他的剧作里医生和官员的说话方式来自医院与部委的经验，而不是单纯的舞台效果。”; cash -3 (theatre ticket); reputation 0.010000000000000009_

- **1931.08** `ad20-shop-opening::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-street-watches` tags: shop, business, opening, everyday, public, obligation, reform, medical
  Hermann Stein把新店的招牌挂上去时，街对面已经站了几个人。第一位客人买了一包针，第二位问能不能赊账，第三位什么也没买，只说“看看”。到傍晚，Hermann Stein发现自己记住了每一张脸，却忘了算今天的账。
  _effects: state_add ad20-public-attention state:world-vienna-1912:240; cash 3 (the first day’s takings); reputation 0.03; flag +shop_opened; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +public_attention_active_

  Hermann Stein把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1933.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/resigned` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, reform, medical
  Emil在门口堵了三天，第四天把Hermann Stein的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Hermann Stein把办公室的钥匙交给Friedrich Hofer，没有带走任何文件。
  _effects: position_end 联络员 @org-wien-magistrat; reputation -0.08000000000000002; mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +resigned_over_scandal; knowledge “辞职信被全文刊登，接任者当天就搬进了办公室。”_

  姑婆的遗嘱只有一页：六十克朗留给Hermann Stein，条件是那间放无声电影的小影院再开五年，并且把她的侄孙女送进学校。Mitzi Moser 2把三个条件念了两遍；Ferdinand Hofer在门外等着，手里捏着另一份没有拆封的遗嘱。Hermann Stein先领了十克朗，把影院的钥匙挂在腰上。
  _effects: cash 10 (first payment under the conditional will); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:320; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +conditional_heir; knowledge “遗嘱要求小影院再开五年，并把姑婆的侄孙女送进学校。”; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Hermann Stein的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Hermann Stein想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。

  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Hermann Stein替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Hermann Stein自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.04000000000000001; mood -0.02999999999999997_

  掌柜的木尺敲了两下柜台，声音短促。Hermann Stein把问题又问了一遍，掌柜才说师兄是“老规矩进来的”。师兄这时转过身，笑了一下，笑得不太自然，说“没事，你问得对”。
  _effects: reputation -0.04000000000000001; relation colleague with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; skill_

  四十七个弗朗茨·穆勒里，只有Hermann Stein拿得出干净的房租簿。公证人Alois Stein 2把遗嘱念完，遗产是一辆灵车、两匹老马和十四克朗的欠账。Hermann Stein没有钱下葬，先把灵车租给搬家的人；夜里跑环城大道，白天替人运钢琴。Berta Lechner 2起初等着看笑话，后来开始给他介绍生意。
  _effects: cash 5 (用灵车替人搬家的收入); role; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:319; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; flag +hearse_became_a_moving_van; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1934.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Rosa Huber把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Hermann Stein把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Rosa Huber低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; flag +rent_increase_accepted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1934.03** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Hermann Stein对Anton说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Anton把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Hermann Stein的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

  Hermann Stein没有等投票，而是让Alois Stein 2把委员会过去三年的差旅账目复印了十份。会议开始前，Mitzi Moser 2的动议被一份“程序审查”取代；审查没有找到Hermann Stein的问题，却找到了两次重复报销。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; flag +rival_audited; knowledge “委员会的差旅账目里有两次重复报销。”; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  窗外有人来问入会，接待的人一时不知道怎么开口，Hermann Stein替他说“进来坐”。老成员把账本拿回去，用一块旧布包好，放进抽屉最里面。抽屉关上的声音比平时响。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +union_debt_written_off_

- **1935.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Hermann Stein就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Mitzi Lechner摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner_

  Hermann Stein把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Hermann Stein走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Alois Stein 2在第三份合同上停住了：同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是Hermann Stein。罚款单当场开出，金额不算大，但足够让下一季度的办公用品全部改成最便宜的牌子。Hermann Stein没有争辩，只在收据背面写了日期。

  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill_

  _effects: position_end 编外顾问 @cinema:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50-evt:world-vienna-1912:004580; reputation -0.05999999999999997; mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +carried_out_of_office; knowledge “名牌在会议结束前就被翻了过去。”_

  Hermann Stein端着Mitzi Lechner的旧帽子，从一楼走到四楼，一家一家敲门。有人给两枚铜板，有人给一件旧衬衣，还有人家把门开了一条缝又关上。到傍晚，帽子里的钱够买一口薄棺；Luise Huber接过帽子时，先数了数，又把钱倒出来重数了一遍。

  _effects: reputation 0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001020:child_

  Hermann Stein把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Hermann Stein折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (三杯 Melange 的咖啡钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; skill_

- **1941.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Maria Berg 2把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Hermann Stein把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Maria Berg 2低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase)_

- **1941.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Alois Stein 2在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Hermann Stein把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水)_

- **1941.05** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/broken-tooth` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  夜里十一点，母狮不肯碰桶里的马肉。Hermann Stein举着煤油灯绕到兽栏后面，看见它右槽牙断了一截，牙龈肿得发亮。Hermann Stein把肉切碎，又去敲Anna Bauer 2的门；第二天兽医来了，Anton Weiss 2在晨会上第一次记住Hermann Stein的名字。
  _effects: organization_found 普拉特动物园夜班队 (other); position 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42-evt:world-vienna-1912:010367; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:672; skill; reputation 0.03; flag +zoo_night_shift; knowledge “母狮的右槽牙断了，牙龈肿得发亮，夜里不肯吃马肉。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1941.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.far` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Hermann Stein顺着河岸一直走，走过铁路桥又走回来，天已经黑透。回来时脚底发热，袜子磨在脚背上，她一路数着路灯走完最后一段巷子，进门先坐下脱了鞋。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1942.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/auditor-is-the-beneficiary` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  审计开始前，Alois Stein 2把名片放在桌上：他是Katharina Lechner的弟弟。Hermann Stein看着他翻开哥哥的考勤卡，又合上，说家里的事不影响审计。抽查只做了半天，报告只有一页，最后一页的签名是空的。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; flag +auditor_was_beneficiary; knowledge “审计员的哥哥就是去年那个顾问，报告最后一页没有签名。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  半年过去，Hermann Stein又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 6 -> 12; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 16 -> 14; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  又过了一个冬天，Hermann Stein能从楼梯的响声判断是谁在值夜，也能从煤气灯的颜色判断哪一段管道该修。师傅把巡查路线改了三次，最后仍然照Hermann Stein画的那张走；两个人都没有提这件事。

  市民礼仪处的办事员把一叠防疫告示交给Hermann Stein，说浆糊要刷匀，别让风揭了角。Hermann Stein沿着市场街贴到天黑，手指被纸边割出细口。第二天，告示下面多了一行铅笔字：“水要煮开”。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +pasted_public_health_notices; role; skill; knowledge “the notice was answered with a handwritten instruction to bo”_

  _effects: flag +volunteered_at_settlement_house; role; skill; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; knowledge “the settlement pays one krone for an evening literacy class”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1942.04** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Ferdinand Hofer用膝盖顶住，Hermann Stein把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

  二楼的老木匠指着裂开的踏板骂起来。Hermann Stein手里还捏着念珠，Luise Huber挡在中间说木板本来就朽了。房东太太在门后听着。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; mood -0.06000000000000005_

  _effects: position_end 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42-evt:world-vienna-1912:010367; reputation -0.05000000000000002; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; flag +removed_by_vote; knowledge “新编制表第一个划掉的是我的秘书。”_

- **1943.01** `kernel-civic.petition-drive::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-government/civic-petition-filed` tags: politics, petition, reform, neighbor, organization, vienna, urban, crisis
  他在一家面包店门口摆开签名纸，Ferdinand Hofer第一个签，写完又把丈夫的名字也添上。到傍晚，纸上有一百二十个名字；Alois Lechner收下时数了两遍，说“我们会研究”。回家的电车上，他发现签名纸的边角被汗浸软了。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; cash -1 (签名纸、墨水和面包店门口的租金); knowledge “请愿书要按街道分开，窗口职员会当面数签名”; flag +petition_filed_

- **1943.02** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hermann Stein说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Ferdinand Hofer把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Ferdinand Hofer在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

  _effects: flag +census_correct; mood 0.010000000000000009; cash -1 (the census fee)_

  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:838; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:008081:child; flag +something_is_missing_

  Hermann Stein看Maria Berg 2盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Maria Berg 2愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Hermann Stein门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Hermann Stein打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; food_

- **1944.01** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-favor` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, vienna
  牌桌上输掉最后一张钞票后，Anton Winter把一叠钱推到Hermann Stein面前，说不用写借条，只要Hermann Stein答应“以后有一天帮一个忙”。Hermann Stein数了数，正好二十克朗。Anton Winter没有说是什么忙，只把烟按灭在桌角，说时候到了会来找Hermann Stein。
  _effects: cash 20 (牌桌上借来的二十克朗); state_add df18-borrowed-luck state:world-vienna-1912:839; flag +df18_took_card_money; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +df18_luck_borrowed; knowledge “我得到了一点不该白拿的好运，代价还没有说清。”_

  _effects: cash -2 (请守墓人照料墓地); mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +grave_keeper_paid_

  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; knowledge “井口的白灯是一块嵌在井盖缝里的碎镜片”; flag +explained_the_well_light_

  _effects: cash -25 (mail-order electric belt advertised as a cure-all); condition; role; knowledge “报纸上的电腰带治不了膝盖，第一天就能烧出水泡，推销员只留一个邮局信箱。”_

- **1945.12** `eh19-bridge.question-arrives::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/put-it-in-the-box` tags: elderhood, testimony, history, memory, bureaucracy, ordinary, vienna, urban
  Hermann Stein把问话单折成四折，塞进装旧证件的铁盒。盒子已经满了，她挑出一张一九三八年的配给证放在上面，又放回去；第二天，区里又寄来一张，同样的纸，同样的第三个问题。
  _effects: state_add eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:905; knowledge “区里第二次寄来同样的问题，说明他们还在查。”; flag +late_testimony_second_letter; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +late_testimony_active_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Hermann Stein把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Hermann Stein忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 0 -> 8; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 16 -> 14; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:df18-borrowed-luck/luck-account/paid-a-little` tags: df18-borrowed-luck, luck, debt, obligation, superstition, risk, bargain, transfer
  半年过去，Hermann Stein还了一点钱，也替债主跑了两趟腿。债主没有夸Hermann Stein，只在账本上划掉一小行；Hermann Stein却发现，自己开始下意识地绕开那家店。
  _effects: cash -1 (还掉一小笔); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 46 -> 38; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 25 -> 28; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:eh19-late-testimony/version-drift/someone-coaches` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  一个自称旧同事的人来家里，把一页写好的说法留在桌上，说这样对大家都好。Hermann Stein没有答应，也没有把纸扔掉；夜里，那张纸压在茶杯底下，边角被水汽卷了起来。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 45 -> 53; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 40 -> 34; knowledge “有人希望我把日期说成一九三八年，而不是一九三四年。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `p9-gap-elderhood-care.give-tools/tools-given-all` tags: elderhood, care, pension, housing, task, task:social
  Hermann Stein把柜底的刨子、凿子和小锯擦干净，用一块旧布包好，交给新搬来的Hermann 2。年轻人当场试了试刨子，刨出一卷薄木花，说比作坊里的好用。她把空出来的柜底又摸了摸，才关上柜门。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1946.05** `em18-late-bloom.shop-sign::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-cooperative-shop` tags: elderhood, late_life, shop, craft, work, reinvention, neighborhood, vienna
  Hedwig提议合租门面：Hermann Stein修旧家具，Hedwig修钟表，房租对半。Hermann Stein把工具摆到靠窗的一边，又在墙上钉了一排钩子；Hedwig说这样像从前师傅的作坊，Hermann Stein说先别急着像。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:949; cash -1 (half of the first month's rent); relation colleague with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +shop_shared; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +late_bloom_active_

- **1946.11** `vienna-elder-care.pharmacy-debt/pay-with-coin-purse` tags: elderhood, health, family, social
  Hermann Stein在药房柜台前数出最后几枚克朗，买下止咳糖浆和一小瓶缬草酊。回家路上在楼梯口歇了两次。
  _effects: cash -6 (药房购买止咳糖浆与缬草酊); health 0.04 (服药后夜间咳嗽减轻)_

- **1947.01** `pc20-testimony.retraction::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/retract` tags: elderhood, testimony, court, retraction, public_reversal, memory, witness, politics
  Hermann Stein在法庭上把上一次的日期改了回来，说那天先响的是门，不是窗。Alois Lechner让他确认，Hermann Stein说确认；Maria Berg 2在旁听席上低下头，手指在膝盖上敲了两下，像在数一件只有他记得的事。
  _effects: state_add pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:997; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 34 -> 19; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 53 -> 63; flag +retracted_testimony; knowledge “那天先响的是门，不是窗；{subject}在法庭上把日期改了回来。”; flag +public_reversal_active_

- **1947.01** `sm18-coincidence.photograph::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/the-face-is-your-own` tags: coincidence, photograph, memory, elder, uncanny, unresolved, vienna, urban
  Hermann Stein在旧相册里翻到一张一九一二年的合影，最后一排站着一个和Hermann Stein年轻时一模一样的女人。照片背面只写着“四月，普拉特”。Maria Berg 2看了很久，说那个人也许是Hermann Stein的姑妈，可Hermann Stein记得姑妈那年还在乡下。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “旧照片里有一个和本人年轻时一模一样的女人，背面写着“四月，普拉特””; flag +found_a_double_in_the_old_photograph_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/a-receipt-in-the-lining` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  半年过去，Hermann Stein又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 14 -> 20; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 8 -> 6; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:em18-late-bloom/late-life-year/family-pressure` tags: em18-late-bloom, elderhood, late_life, reinvention, love, work, legacy, risk
  成年子女来了一趟，把账本翻到最后一页，说这年纪不该再折腾。Hermann Stein没有争，只把钥匙从桌上收进围裙口袋；那天晚上，新生活仍按原计划继续，只是没有再提。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 53 -> 61; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 45 -> 41; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `r13-civ.burial-society::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/joined-society` tags: social, burial_society, insurance, death, neighbor, vienna, urban, postwar
  Maria Berg 2把铜板在桌上排成一列，说每月两枚，死了由会里出棺材和一顿丧饭。Hermann Stein数出第一月的钱，Maria Berg 2在名字后面画了一个圈。墙上的名单已经写满了三张纸，最上面几张发了黄。
  _effects: cash -2 (丧葬会的铜板); flag +joined_burial_society; role; knowledge “the society pays for a coffin and a funeral meal”_

- **1947.01** `state_reaction:em18-late-bloom/a-death-in-the-household` tags: em18-late-bloom, elderhood, late_life, reinvention, love, work, legacy, risk
  家里少了一个人以后，Hermann Stein把新生活里最花钱的那一项划掉了。不是因为不想，而是因为账单、葬礼和空出来的房间都需要有人管；Hermann Stein把划掉的那一行又抄到另一张纸上。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 61 -> 71; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 47 -> 43; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1947.06** `em18-late-bloom.rent-increase::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/pays-the-increase` tags: elderhood, late_life, housing, rent, shop, money, risk, vienna
  房东把新租约摊在柜台上，说煤价涨了，房租每月多加一克朗。Hermann Stein把账本翻到最后一页，算了一遍修一把椅子的工钱，最后还是在租约上签了字；那天下午，Hermann Stein把修理价目也重新写了一张。
  _effects: cash -3 (the rent and the new price list); state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 45 -> 53; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 50 -> 47; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_paid_

- **1947.12** `pc20-reversal.quiet-exoneration::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/take-the-file` tags: elderhood, public_reversal, quiet_exoneration, reputation, family, archive, late_life, vienna
  Hermann Stein没有接受也没有拒绝，只把整份档案借出来，带回家和Ferdinand Kral一起读。两个人在厨房桌上按年份排到半夜，最后把其中一页抄下来寄给了一个还在等消息的旧同事；更正有没有登报，反而成了次要的事。
  _effects: state_remove pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:997; state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:998; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; cash -2 (copying and posting the file); flag +took_the_file_home; knowledge “档案按年份排到半夜，其中一页被抄下来寄给了旧同事。”; flag -public_reversal_active; flag +second_chance_open_

- **1947.12** `state_reaction:eh19-late-testimony/document-surfaces` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  一只旧信封从档案馆退回来，里面有一张当年盖过章的收据。日期和Hermann Stein记得的差了一天；Hermann Stein把收据放在窗边，看了很久，最后没有改口。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 19 -> 24; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 63 -> 66; knowledge “档案里有一张纸能对上证词里的日期。”_
